Text:

|| oṃ ||
cidgaganacandrikā ||
śrīmahākavi kālidāsaviracitā |
dhanadānandanāthadīkṣānāmaśālinā,
karrā agnihotraśāstriśarmaṇā viracitayā ,
divyacakorikākhyavyākhyayā
samalaṃkṛtā ||
karrā jogayyaśāstrihodayaiḥ kalikātānagarasyāgamānusandhānasamititaḥ svāmi trivikramatīrthaprakaṭitāṃ sampādya, śāstrīti vyavahṛtaiḥ rāmānandanāthadīkṣānaśālibhiḥ sikindrābād nagarasthaiḥ nijāṃkālejīnāmakakalāśālāyāṃ rājakīyavidyāniyamānuruddhāṃglādibhāṣātrayavidyāsu vidvān ityādipadatrayottīrṇapaṭṭabhadratayā lalitāśataka, haṃsatārāvalī, kāmavilāsādinaikakhaṇḍakāvyakalpanirmātṛtayāca prophesar padābhiṣiktaiḥ saripella viśvanāthaśāstrimahodayaiḥ prātipadikamātrāṃ glānuvādaṭīkāyāpi samalaṃkṛtya, īṣṭugodāvariḍi -- amalāpuraṃtālūkāśākhājānapadānāṃ bhaṭnavilligrāmasthitānāṃ …vidyāyārīṇānāṃ, svīya śrīśāradāmudrākṣaraśālāyāṃ …pariṣkṛtipūrvakaṃ sammudryaca prakāśyaṃ nītā, vijayate || 
atha cidgaganacandrikāyāṃ divyacakorikāvyākhyāyāṃ ca, upoddhāte pūrvapīṭhikā ||
ayibhoḥ ! paricaritravimarśanaikapravaṇapārīrṇacattāścaritracaturānanādimahābirudasahasrāṃkitāḥ jambvādidvīpadvīpāntaravarṣavarṣāntakhaṇḍakhaṇḍāntavāsinovāgarthaikavimarśanapāṇḍitīvimarśakāgresarāḥ paṇḍitprakāṇḍāḥ vijñāyapayāmi kāñcana vijñaptiṃ pratyūṣe pradoṣaikarasikāṃ pratipatkalāśarīrāmiva svīyā svārānusvāravaisargikīṃ naisargikīṃ vā nādavāgarthakalāṃ prātipadikavimarśenānuśīlanīyāmanuśīlyānujighṛkṣādṛśyāpyanugṛhṇantviti | vinivedayāmi sapraśrayaṃ sāñjalibandhapraṇāmahasahasram | svīyāṃ paracaritravimarśavikṣiptāmapi dakṣiṇāṃ dṛśamasavyāmapaśavyāṃ vā avāmatayaivātrāpi vinivartya vinipātayantviti ca |
viditamevaitat kaiścanapāścātyairataevāprācyavāsitavāmadarśibhistadanukārībhiśca prakṛtau cidgaganacandrikākṛtau, kartari kālidāse ca kṛtiprakṛtyoḥ kartṛnāmarūpeṣvaikyānaikyaparīkṣāsamīkṣābhyāṃ tattatsvarūpavimarśanapūrvakaṃ, kṛterapi prakṛteriva tadīyatvādīyatvānyatarakakṣyānirṇayaparyantam | soyaṃ mahākaviḥ kiyadvarṣadeśīyaḥ | kati vā, nāma, kalānvitāḥ, kālidāsamahākavayaḥ | ityādi kuhūcandāranveṣāyamāṇaiḥ, parokṣakalicandrānveṣamātraparyavasitaiḥ, praśnāvalibhissākaṃ nāmaikadeśīyakālivāgarthayottadīyayoḥ, svīyayorvā, cidgaganavāgarthayoḥ, kṛtiprakṛtyorvā yaugikavāgarthavigrahasya, mahārthamarthasambandhaviśeṣeṇāviduṣāmataeva, kati, nāmakalānvitāḥ, mahākavaya iti, gūḍhābhisandhineva svānupajñenāpi pṛṣṭāsu, mahākavicandravyaktiṣu, caritroṇāpi yāṃ kāñcana vyaktimajānatāṃ sāhasikānāmādhunikavimarśakadhaureyāṇāṃ paṇḍitaprakāṇḍānāṃ coddaṇḍaprākṛtabrahmāṇḍavimarśanavāgvivādaracanāvilāsaḥ dhāvadhāvamadya yāvannirṇayavidhau, prākṛtepi vā, prākṛtāṃ vā tāṭasthyakāṣṭhā madhiruhya, hanta na viśrāmyatītimahadidaṃ śokasthānaṃ lokasya | mamāpyetacchrāvaṃśrāvaṃ darśadarśa`maśvopyanaśvataro hyagṛdhnurgardabhāyate' iti lokoktyanusāreṇa kṛtiprakṛtināmai rūpaikyaviṣayepyasañjātavirodhanyāyenaivaṃ sañjātassaṃśayaḥ |
tadatra kṛtiprakṛtikartṛnāmarūpaikyagate vivāde viṣaye, kalikātānagaravāsibhirāgamānusandhānasamitīyairmūlakṛteḥ kṛtimūlasya vā prabandhasya sampādakaistatprakāśakaiśca, śrīsvāmitrivikramatīrthapādairupoddhāte svīye -- "granthasyāsya kartā kālidāsa iti granthapariśīlanenāpi niścetuṃ śakyam" ityuddhoṣya "janaśrutirapyevaṃ kila | sā ca satyā" ityādinā janaśruteḥ svaśrutyāsākaṃ śrutimūlatāṃ sampādya, mūlagranthepi mahākavinaivānuttaramahārthakramopāsanārahasyabodhikayā `kāli' iti, tathā `kālidāseti' ca, tathā `svoditaṃ dāsajalpitaṃ' `kālidāsaracitāṃ ca paṃcikām' ityantaṃ ślokasaṅkhyāyāṃ ślokeṣu svātmagurudevatācakrakramanāmarūpāntamūrtyāśayena jānaśrutinā śabdena sākaṃ svīyāṃ śṛtiṃ kṛtiprakṛtikartṛnāmarūpaikīkaraṇe svataḥ pramāṇaśabdavatsvayamapi pramāṇayadbhissusamādhinā samādhāya viśrāmitaścāturyacaturāprācyapragalbho vāgvivādaḥ | atha tadīyacidgaganacandrikāmahākṛtiprakṛtyostadīyatvātadīyatvavāgvādasamādhāne viśrāntau vā svantatvāsvantatvābhyāmapīdameva mānaṃ tatvajijñāsūnāṃ paryāptam | yadatra nādanādhatadantanavakatrayaikarūpayā, vaiṣayikyā, vaidikyā vā ahaṃgrahopāstyā, saṃsiddhakālīkālamithunatādātmyabhāvasya mahākavairvaidikatāntrikapañcavāhamahārthakramaikarasyena, nādanādhatadantanavakatripuṭītrayātmakānāṃ, vāgarthaṣaḍadhvamayānāṃ tricatuḥpañcavāhamahārthakramāṇāṃ, varṇanāvasare annamayādyānandamayānteṣu, vaidikapañcakośeṣu madhyamamānanyāyena tadīyaprāṇamanovijñānamaye sūkṣmalaiṅgike, gagane, vāṅmanaḥprāṇavaiśvanaranādamayaśarīrasya, kālīkāleśvaramidhunasya, sakalajagatkāraṇatvavivakṣayā "parasparatapassampatphalāyitaparasparau | prapañcamātāpitarau prāṃcau jāyāpatī stumaḥ"ityetallakṣaṇalakṣite anādinādaṣaḍadhvagarmite pravṛttinivṛtyekarūpe pramātrādimahātripuṭīsamaṣṭimidhune mahāsūtrātmanyeva "yato vā imāni bhūtāni jāyante" "yasya niśvasitaṃ vedāḥ yo vedebhyokhilaṃ jagat nirmame" "asyamahato bhūtasya niḥśvasitamevaitaghadṛgvedo yajurvedassāmavedaḥ" ityuktalakṣaṇaśrutyāditantrāgamāntanādavāgartheṣu pramāṇībhūtānāṃ svaravarṇakalāmātṛkānāṃ tripuṭaḥpañcīkaraṇādinā turīyatadatītānandamayopādhisamanvayena ca "cillocanena satataṃ śivamīkṣitamanvaye ca sadbhāve | labdhuṃca pūrṇabhāvaṃ yadi cetassamupādhvamimām" ityādinā | "sa vā eṣa puruṣaḥ pañcadhā pañcātmā yena sarvamidaṃ protaṃ pṛthivīcāntarikṣaṃ ca dyauśca diśaścāvāṃtaradiśāśca savai sarvamidaṃ jagatsasabhūtagaṃ sa bhavyam" ityādi taittirīyamahānārāyaṇīyopaniṣadupāntyakhaṇḍikoktanyāyena, "dhāmayadbhavati mudrayā samaṃ" ityādinā vakṣyamāṇadhāmasaṃvidvarṇakramatrayaikyena sākaṃ prakāśānandamūrtimayabṛndacakropāstiṃ sāṃgopāṅgaṃ varṇayato mahāvīdhaureyasya, kavicandrasya, vāgarthaikyasvarūpatatvāvabhodane, svopamānachadmanā, svīyaeva raghuvaṃśamahākāvyārambhamaṅgalaślokaḥ "vāgarthāviva sampṛktau" ityādinā sarveṣāṃ vādiprativādināmasādhyakoṭipraviṣṭatayā'vipratipannasūtraprāyaḥ | "māniṣā"detyākārakaśrīmadrāmāyaṇasūtraślokavat "yāhamityuditavākparā ca sā" ityādinā svārthatopyanūdya sakalavāgarthapratipattimayāya mahāvidyānandalābhāyāṃ, tataḥ | "pūrṇapīṭhakṛtaṃ siddhe stadbhāvastavamādarāt | vānarārthaṃ mahāgubyaṃ śrīvatso vidadhe sa tu " ityantagranthasadarbheṇa tadbhāṣyeṇeva pūrṇapīṭhīkṛtaśceti svoktyā svagranthānuśīlanenacāvagamyate | kiṃcātra "dhamamudrāvarṇakalāsaṃvidbhāvasvabhāvataḥ | pātāniketadṛ(sṛ) ṣṭyā ca bṛndacakraṃ prakāśitaṃ" iti tantrālokādyuktāṣṭavidhabṛndacakraṃ prakāśitaṃ" iti tantrālokādyuktāṣṭanidhabṛndacakropāstidṛṣṭibhedānāṃ dhāmasaṃvidvarṇakramatrayāntarbhāvavarṇanena | "aṣṭadhā sphurati yā purī, aṣṭapuryuditavāsanātmanā" ityāditattatpuryaṣṭakavāsanābhedavarṇanena cābhijñānaśākuntalanāṭakīyanāndīślokavarṇitātmīyamūrtyaṣṭakapratyabhijñāpanam | prathamavimarśe dvitīyamaṅgalaślokasya turīyacaraṇapūraṇe svīyamālavikāgnimātranāṭakīyanāndīślokavarṇitātmīyamūrtyaṣṭakapratyabhijñāpanam | prathamavimarśe dvitīyamaṅgalaślokasya turīyacaraṇapūraṇe svīyamālavikāgnimātranāṭakīyanāndīślokavarṇitātmīyamūrtyaṣṭakapratyabhijñāpanam | prathamavimarśe dvitīyamaṅgalaślokasya turīyacaraṇapūraṇe svīyamālavikāgnimitranāṭakīyanāndīślokaturīyacaraṇasyaiva "sanmārgālokanāya vyapanayatu sa va stāmasīṃ vṛttimīśaḥ" ityasyānanyavāgarthabhāvādicauryeṇa kaṇṭhoktyupanibandhanādīnibahūnivyāvartakānyasyā api kṛteḥ prakṛtyā sākaṃ tadīyatāṃ poṣayantyeveti punaruktaprāyaṃ | kiṃca `vāṅmayāmṛtanidhiḥ prakāśate kālidāsa iti kopi candramāḥ | yena cidgananacandrikā kṛtā hṛdaguhāsu sudhiyāṃ hṛtaṃ tamaḥ' ityādi kavicakravartiviracitaṃ hṛdayacandrikāvacanapapyasminnarthe śābdaṃ pramāṇapadamadhitiṣṭhati | tathātraprathamavimarśaeva `yaḥ kilāsa' ityādisūtrātmaprameyake sūtraśloke tadīyamahāprameyena sūtritaparaśāmbhavabhidhunaghaṭakī (ghaṭī) bhūtena parāhaṃmantramayavāgarthacaraṇasaghaṭṭena miśritavāsanācaraṇamayasyānuttaramahānirvāṇacaraṇamahārthasya varṇanavaiśāratvena meghadūtakāvye gaurīśikharavarṇanāvasare yakṣakṛtameghopadeśacchalena "yasmin dṛṣṭe karaṇavigamādūrdhvamuddhṛtapāpāḥ kalpiṣyante sthiragaṇapadaprāptaye śraddhadhānāḥ" ityādinā varṇitamahāśāmbhavacaraṇavibhūtiranāhūtāpi smṛtipathamadhirohatīti tadvidotra pramāṇamityanuttarasaṃpradāyamahārthasiddhānāṃ mahadudāharaṇam | athāpi madīyassaṃśayastadavasthaeva kālidāsakṛtiprakṛtināmarūpaikye | yataḥ syātkācana rūpāṃtaramāpannāpyarūpā sarūpā vā tadīyacidgaganacandrikākṛtiprakṛtirmahākṛtirivetyasanmūlasya turyātītamapyatītya vartamānasya prapañcapañcamasyānunmūlanātsaṃśayasyeti punaḥ punaruktabhayamapyavigaṇayya sudūraṃdhāvanvimarśakāgraṇībhyaḥ prācyāprācyavidvadvīradhaureyebhyo jihnemi na sāhasādullikhitamapīti kṣantavyosmi vinivedanalajjayāpyalamāmūlacūḍamasmadīvyākhyayā sākamaparokṣamūlakṛtivimarśakāṇāmiti cāvedya viramyete meṣāṇḍatolanatulyādaprācyavimraṣṭṛvimarśanaprayāsāt |
kiñcātra kiṃcidjñānāmaprastutāpi prākṛtakālikī svānubhūtyā'prākṛtakālikī ca vastugatirmāyayā'māyakalokavañcanakāriṇī dharmācāryaveṣasañchannā, mahājanānāṃ jāgatṛye kiṃcidivāvedyate "siddhanādakṛtatatkramastuteḥ kālidāsaracitāṃ ca paṃcikām" iti turīyavimarśaparyantoktāṃ mahākavicandrasūktiṃ pramāṇīkṛtyaitanmūlakṛtiprakṛterādisiddhanādhakṛtāyāḥ kramastutessuviliphsayā mahāntamapi vyayasāyādikaṣṭamavigaṇayya, sudūraṃ paribhrāmya, dussahaṃ duḥkhamapi soḍham | tadlābhena tatsarvaṃ bhasmanīva hutam | tato svasadgurunirvāṇacaraṇaprasārabalena mahākāveranuttarattvaranādabindukalāvāgarthakramatricatuḥpañcapuṭīsrotaḥsvacchandacāritā sākṣādupalabdhetyanuttarasiddhāntaprabandha dāridryepi yathā vyācikīrṣutā galagrāhiṇī saṃvṛttā tatpurastāduttarapīṭhikāyāṃ mūlāgamanivedanaprakaraṇe anupadabhāvedayiṣyate | paraṃtvatra rāṣṭrarājakīyaviḍvarādinā viḍvarāhādineva viruddhadigdeśakālavātyādibhayena laukikadraviṇalopena cāyasākṣaramudrapaṇopayuktaviśvamayagranthākṣaraṣaḍadhvalekhanādeḥ kṛtyapañcakakṣārodādantarāyayādassaṃkulāduttīrya kṣīrodaprāntasaṃcāriṇīṃ divya cakorikāṃ varṣadvayādārabhyeyatyapi kālasrotasi dharmātyayoṣassu cakravākīmivaśāradapradoṣeṣu laukikyā api candrikāyā āsvādatṛṣayā cāndrāyaṇavratasthāmiva virahiṇīmupoṣitāmavalokyāpi "sarvārambhāstaṇḍulaprasthamūlāḥ" ityabhiyuktasubhāṣitaṃ smāraṃ smāraṃ kā gatiritīvordhvadṛkkāgatapatrādilopena tanniṣkrayamūlyadāridryeṇa tadarthaṣaḍadhvaparyavasitapādheyalopādinā ca mahate duḥkhāya bhājanībhūyaiva paramadāridryapūrṇapīṭhīkṛtaśca yāvadvirajye, iyatyantarāle yathāvadanubhūyamānadeśaparyaṭanādikaṃ kāgatapatradāridryaprabhṛti pātheyalopāvadhikaṃ ca sudussahaduḥkhagrāmamacaturakṣīṇamapi caturākṣacāturyeṇādhyakṣaṇakṣīkṛtya śikindrābād nagare śāstrimahodayāḥ `tṛṇāni bhūmirudakaṃ sūnṛtā vākturīyakā | satāṃ geheṣu satataṃ nocchidyante kadācana' ityuktamānavamahāsmārtadharmasya svayamādarśībhūya svāvasadhyapradānapūrvakaṃ vakṣyamāṇakrameṇāsyā divyacakorikāvyākhyāyāḥ mahāsītāsāvitryāḥ maithilo rājarṣiriva kālidāsīyahṛdvyomni cidgaganacandrikāyāssaubhikayajñavāṭe vedīkarṣaṇādiyāvajjagatyā rāmapāṇigrahāntakhaṇḍakathāyāṃ pāśupatadhanurākarṣaṇapaṇabandhena yāvadbhāravahanārthamupanāyakaḥ pīṭhamarda iva yāvatpratyabhavan, teṣveva dineṣu śauṣkala iva rāvaṇapurodhāḥ dharmadhvajadharmāṇāmapi drogdhā tādṛśasyaiva virūpadharmābhāsācāryapīṭhasya vyavasthāpakaḥ kila śāṅkarāpaśado nāma kvacinmahārātre "ādilābādnagarīyadharmaviplavasamīkaraṇārthamagnihotraśāstriṇo mayāsākaṃ māsadvayaṃ yāvatpīṭhasannidhau dharmavyavasthāpanādinā kāryakrameṇopavāsārthaṃ preṣaṇīyā" iti jagadgurupīṭhamiti nāma mātraśravaṇenaiva prākṛtadharmadhvajeṣu kalyantajagadgauraveṣu pīṭhadharmadrohiṣu dhanāgamārthaṃ śikṣitadharmadhvajarahasyeṣvananubhavadoṣeṇājāgarūkatayā sulabhapratāraṇān śāstrimahodayān dhārvāḍanagarīyāṇāṃ nāgeśaśāstriṇāṃ vāraṇasīyānāṃ rājeśvaraśāstriṇāṃ ca mahopādhyāyānāṃ svalithipatrikāṇāṃ pratisamādhānakalpitapatrādimiścadmanā sānunayamiva suguptaśāṭhyaliṅgairatigāḍhaṃ vipralabhya yāvadayācayat tāvataiva tadānīṃ svīyaṃ divyacakorikāsammudraṇādimahākāryakramabhāraṃnivedyāpi parapratāraṇapaṇḍitena tena sudūraṃ vipralabdhatay svopakrānte mudrāpaṇādikāryabhāre labdhamiva hastāvalambaṃ matvā tatsarvaṃ niśvasyāpyacitathaṃ viśvasya yāvadāviḥśvasan -- tāvadayi bhoḥ ! `virūpākṣaṃ khalu pīṭhamakulaṃ kulavidyāsu saṃhāraikatatparaṃ mayānubhūtapūrvameva' ityādyasmadīyaṃ hitavacanamapyavigaṇathya sugāḍhavipralabdhatayā māṃ tena śāṃkarāpaśadenasākaṃ pīṭhasannidhau dharmaviplavasamīkaraṇacchamanā tādātvikavidvallopapūrtaye bahudhā'bhyacodayan | pūrvānubhūtaduścaritopyahaṃ śāstrimahodayānāṃ pīṭhagauraveṇa dākṣiṇyena cobhayataḥ pāśabaddhaśśakuntaiva tatpīṭhasannidhau `upabhravṛṣaṇaṃbhraṃśabhramālasasṛgālavat | rājanbahūnyahānyatra yāpitāni vṛdhāmāyā' iti kenacidvipaścitā subhāṣitameṣāṇḍapatanabhramālasasṛgālanyāyena tatraiva pratyahaṃ śikindrābādnagarācchāstrivaryaiḥ patrādibhirāhūto yiyāsureva `śvaḥ paraśva' ityutsavavigarhairguruvaryaiśśāṃkarāpaśadena ca kubārenujāyamānena vāryamāṇa eva kiṃcinnyūnaṃ māsacatuṣṭayamanaiṣam | atrāntare svīyagrāme putrapratirūpaḥ śrīmān svadharmādapracyutajīvito dhanāḍhyo madīyo bahiścaraḥ prāṇaiva vaṇiṅmaṇiḥ śiṣyaḥ mumūrṣurāste satvaramāgamyatāmiti didṛkṣātiśayena yadāsmi tantrīvārtayā''hūtaḥ tadānīṃ śāṃkarāpaśadena pātheyenāpyanupakṛtya vṛthaiva prahitaḥ | madīyaḥ pātheyabhāropi yadā śāstrivaryāṇāṃ mūrdhnyapatat | tadā khalu mayā sākaṃ śāstrivaryāṇāmapi pūrvoktaśāṅkarāpaśadīyā urabhravṛṣaṇabhraṃśabhramanivṛttiśśuktimukteva muktāphalena sākaṃ saprayāsaṃ saṃpāditetyādīni bahūni śāṃkarāpaśadīyāni svecchānicchāparecchāprārabdhānyantarāyasahasrāṇi daivecchāprārabdhānīvottīryāpi divyacakorikākhyā vyākhyā jagatyarāmapāṇigrahotsavānande prathamadvitīyavimarśakhaṇḍotsavānandapātratāmetāvatīmapyanuvamūveti mahadidamānandasthānam | atra mūlakṛtāvāgamānusandhānasamitīyenetareṇavāṃglapaṇḍitavareṇa vilikhya vyākhyātecāgotravitpravaratulye āṃglavāṅmayopoddhāte `pūrṇapīṭhakṛtaṃ siddhe'rityānuṣṭubhīṃ kaveraṃtimaślokapratijñāmudāhṛtya prabandhasyāsyaśrīvatsāparanāmā coritakālidāsamahārthavaranāmā ca kaścitpaṃḍitavaraḥ karteva pratibhātīti śrīvatsavāgarthārthakalāyā apyavivekenājñānenavā vilekhanapāṃḍityasāhasaṃ paṃḍitānāmiva prācīnānāṃ muṃḍitānāmapi mahāṃtamupahāsamāpādayatītyalaṃ kāleyavijṛmbhaṇavimarśapāṃḍityena | sthālīpulākanyayenāpyetat ślokavyākhyāvimarśakāṇāmaparokṣameva pratibhāsata ityanavamāpi navamīva lekhinī tadaṅkanātsvāṃkācca pratinivṛttā svarūpe viśrāmyatītyato na tatrāpyagrahaḥ || itīyamupoddhāte pūrvapīṭhikā || śrī śrī śrī ||
athaitasyāścidgaganacandrikāyāḥ prameyagurukramapraṇālikayā
svaprakṛteriva saprameyastadāgamo nāma -- 
upoddhātottarapīṭhikā |
athaitasya mūlaprabandharājasya rājamahendravaranagaryāṃ bahudhānyādivarṣe cāśvine śuklapakṣemāsi kāraṇāntareṇānivṛttagatau vartamāne mayi mannikaṭamāgatebhyo yadṛcchayā brahma śrīyutebhyaḥ karropāhvajogayyaśāstrimahodayebhyassakāśātsaṃkṣepataḥ kiñcidiva ṭīkāriracayiṣayā tīrthīkaraṇārthamivāgamaḥ | tatra yadihyahamadakṣiṇopi dākṣiṇyena pravarteyam, gaṇḍasyopari piṭakā saṃvṛtteti laukikīṃ nītimanusṛtyā'kāmayānopi tanmudraṇādau mahatāḍambareṇa saṃsārāḍambareṇeva gale gṛhīto bhaveyamityāgāmicitritagūḍhābhisaṃdhito bhiyācānuttarasrotaḥ kāṭhinyaprayuktayā dvitrivāraṃ niṣidhya virajyamānenāpi, kiṃcinnyūnapaṃcāśadvārṣikādhyayanasaṃpāditānāmanātmakalānāmiva sthitānāṃ catuṣṣaṣṭikalānāmāvidyakārthakāmadohanāya, mānuṣavittārjanādāvanupayuktatvena, daridramanorathavaddurviniyogabhayānnu, bhāratīyavāgarthaparibhāṣā'nāntarīyakāyāṃ bhāṣāvāṅmayapūjāyāmadhītakalānāṃ mantrapuṣpāñjalipradānalobhānnu, vāṅmayamahāyāgīyāvabṛdhasnānalobhānnu, svātmadevatāpreraṇabalānnu, ajñātamūlāt bhavitavyatābalātkila balātkāreṇaiva pravṛttirūrīkṛtā | tato gṛhānāgatena grahagṛhīteneva, māsaṣaṭkamananyadṛśā, rātriṃdivaṃ maṣyākṛteriva mahākṛtyā, lāṃchiteyaṃ cakorikānāmābhāsacakorikā vyākhyā | tadanu tadanuttarasiddhāntatantragranthadāridryeṇaṃ talliphsayā puraiva paricitānāmasmatsagotratvena sodarāṇāmadyayāvadekahāyanādarvākkīrtikāṃtāyā ākalpasaṃsthāpanenālaṃkṛtaśrīnagarāṇāṃ brahmaśrīyutānāṃ karropāhvasubrahmaṇyaśāstrīmahodayānāṃ paṭṭabhadratayā ballāriḍi || hospeṭa ḍi || musaphkorṭunyāyasthāne prāḍvivākānāmapyanuttarasiddhāntapāraṃgatānāṃ savidhamavāpya, uṣitvāca sārthamāsaṃ, ? tantrālokatantrasāralaghuvivṛtimālinīvijayavārtikahṛdayacandrikāsaubhāgyavāsanāpaddhatimahānayaprakāśānandakalpalatikādiṣu tattadupayuktagranthabhāgamupalabhyamaṣyākṛtilāñchitāmābhāsacakorikāṃ yāvadāśrāvayiṣaṃ tāvataivānandollāsabharitaistairdvāviṃśativarṣakalpebhyaḥ prāk vilekhya sampāditā saṃrakṣitā ca, mūlakṛtimātrākalpā, cidgaganacandrikā'dya yāvadanvarthanāmā, sālaṅkārāśramapūrṇā, viśramaṇīyā kalpā ca samajanītyāmūlacūḍamasaṃlakṣitakramacakorikābhāsā sahāyenaiva saṃtṛptijyotsnāritāntaraṅgatayā viniśvasitamiva | tataḥ svīyamānandollāsabharaṃ patrikāmukhena prakaṭayya ca kṛtakṛtyaṃ manyamānā iva divaṃ yayuḥ | paścāttāṃ paścāttāpalāñchitāṃ tadanutāpānubaddhahṛdvyomā divyacakorīkaraṇakautukena svadyota iva taissākaṃ divamutpatiṣyanvāgarthaṣaḍadhvapathena nākṣatraṃ vyoma yadā niraṇaiṣam, tadāsmacchārīrakaikasambandhinopi vinītāntevāsikalpāḥ triliṅgasaṃskṛtāṃglabhāṣātritayepi rājakīyabhāṣāniyamānuruddhavidyāśākhāyāṃ vidvān ityādipadatritayottīrṇapaṭṭabhadratayā haidarābād nagare nijāṃkālejīnāmakāṃglavidyāśālāyāṃ saṃskṛtāndhrabhāṣādhyāpakeṣu prophesara padābhiṣiktā apyanuttarasiddhāntamahānandasāmrājyapadepi kṛtābhiṣekatayā rāmānandanāthadīkṣānāmaśālinaśca śāstrītyāṃglavyāvahārikanāmnā lalitāśatakahaṃsatārāvalī -- kāmavilāsādinaikakhaṇḍakāvyakalpanirmātārassaripella viśvanāthaśāstrimahodayāssānandasambhramamāṃglapaṇḍitalokamahopakārāyāsyāḥ kṛtiprakṛteḥ ślokavākyārthamātrasyāṃglabhāṣāparivartanepi śraddhāpurassarametadīyaṃ divyacakorikāvyākhyāmudrāpaṇādibhāraṃ svaskandhayoryugandharavat svayamevāsiṣajannityaho mahatsāhasam | yāvadevaṃ gurutrayamukhyayā nādhaparamparayā'yamasyāgamaprakāraḥ | atrāntare adya gurudvayasya manavasiddhaughamithunasyeva kulasahasrārapatitasya karropalāñchitatvena madīyasyātrivaṃśasamudbhūtasya vidyāvīryaikaprabhavasyātrigotrakulasyāpi satriṃśapūraṇena lokapratyāyanārthamiva tādṛśatvena tatravai tricatuḥ pañcāvāhakramabhedepi tallāṃchanārthamiva karrāśabdasya prākṛtāndhrabhāṣāparibhāṣayā daṇḍaśabdaparyāyatāprayuktadaṇḍakramānuvṛtyā ca uktalakṣaṇatricatuḥpañcavāhakramabhedabhinnadaṇḍakramānuvṛttāvapitryarthamahākramalobheneva tridaṇḍakramānuruddhena daivānītena tridaṇḍasanyāseneva śiṣyaughapaṃktipatitena mayāpi purā maṣyākṛtilāṃchiteva bhāsamānā''bhāsacakorikā kulākulasahasrārasāmarastyenaiva divyacakorīkṛtā cetīyatā kālakrameṇa samūlakalpaḥ, prabandhāgamopi, āgamaiva dhāmasaṃvidvarṇakramestripuṭīkānubaddhastrivṛtkaraṇeneva prakāśānandamūrticakratrayānubandhenāpi, kalikātānamarasthāgamānusandhānasamitīyatrivikramatīrthapādapreritopyatrivikamakramānuvṛtyā turīyakulakramānuruddhe, vaṃśadaṇḍaparvaṇi, madīye mayi vā, turyātītamahānandamūrticakrīkṛtaścetyanena mahānandavīcīsaṃrambharasollāsabhareṇaitatkṛtau nirvāhāyakilātītaturyakramopi turīyānandamūrticakramātrarūposmi saṃvṛttaḥ | "muṇḍanaṃ jñānarūpaṇe daṇḍanaṃ ca kriyātma(nā)tā | muṇḍadaṇḍakramau tena matau jñānakriyātmakau || iti mahārthamañjarī parimalādiṣūllikhitatantrāntaravatanoktanītyā'smadīyakriyāmayānandacakradaṇḍakramasya pūrvanirūpitadiśā saripella viśvanāthaśāstrimahodayaiḥ svayaṃsvīkṛtatvenāvaśiṣṭamuṇḍakramamātre vicitrabhānāviva citrabhānāvanuvatsare'kulasahasrāreca svabhānunā viśrāṃtiścopalabdhā | atraivāntarāle purā pūrvapīṭhikāyāṃvilikhya vijñāpita prakāreṇa virūpākṣapīṭhābhosa'kāryavyavasthāpakasya śāṅkarāpaśadasya svātmadharmadroheṇa kṛtaṃ mahāpratāraṇamapyanubhūya iti kartavyatāmauḍhyena śrīśāstrimahodayairvijayanagarasaṃsthānapaṇḍitaprakāṇḍānāṃ śrīmahāmahopādhyāya kalāprapūrṇādivirudāṅkitānāṃ śrītātā subbarāyaśāstrimahodayānāṃ śrī `nyāyabhūṣaṇa'"peri lakṣmīnārāyaṇaśāstrimahodayānāṃ ca saṃśodhanepi preritaḥ purā kalpīyāmābhāsa cakorikāmevādāyāgamam | tatra tayossaivāśrāvitā | tacchravaṇasaṃtuṣṭayostayoparitoṣapatrikāpradānamapi tasyā ekata na divyacakorikāyāḥ | tathaiva hariharaśāstriprabhṛtīnāmiti punaruktaprāyam | tasmāttattadīyapatrikā''nayanottaramevābhāsacakorī divyā kṛteti pāṭhakamahodayebhyo vinivedya viramyate prabandhāgamodantalekhanādeḥ ||
atrāsmin kalikātānagarasthāgamānusandhānasamitīye cidgaganacandrikākhye mūlaprabandhe ślokapañcakaṃ nopalabhyate | tacca -- "khe nirastasakalakriyākrame" `ādimāntimagṛhītavarṇarāśyātmikā' `ambike! cidamūtāmbucinmayīm' `yatra tatra militā marīcayaḥ' `amba! tāvakapadadvayārcaka' ityākārakam | tatra prathamatṛtīyaturīyaślokatrayaṃ mūlakośasampādakaiḥ svāmi trivikramatīrthapādairmūlagranthasamāptyanantaraṃ yoginīhṛdayasetubandhādinibandhagrantheṣu tattadācāryaiḥ pramāṇatayā parigṛhīteṣūllekhanena pradarśyāpi mūlaprabandhe svopalabdhe tadanupalambhātkila sthānāntaraprakṣepasāhase saṅgatipracyutibhayena vā hetvantareṇa svamudrite mūlakośe mudraṇāparityaktam | dvitīyaḥ ślokaḥ granthāntare pañcamopi vandārāśramapūjyapādakṛtasaundaryalaharīvyākhyāne ca pramāṇīkaraṇenopalabdha iti krameṇa `khe nira'stetyādiślokadvayaṃ dvitīyavimarśe aṅkāṅkite sthānadvaye `amba keci' dityādyavaśiṣṭaślokadvayaṃ, tṛtīyavimarśe krameṇa sthānadvaye ca pañcamamapi, turīyavimarśe sthāne ca tattatsaṅgatilobhenaiva prakṣipya vyākhyātam | ata etatpañcakaṃ teṣu vā sthānāntareṣu vā vimarśakaiḥ paṇḍitavaraiḥ prakṣepaṇārhatayā boddhavyamityapi nivedyata iti śivam ||
atha cidgaganacandrikāviṣayādyanubandhacatuṣṭayādiprameyasārāṃśaḥ -- 
seyaṃ mahākṛtiḥ vimarśacatuṣṭayopetagahvaraviṣayarājasaṅkalitaśabdārthaśarīratayā kālidāsasamāhākavicandrasyaiva mahākṛtirnānyasyetyapi suvacā | tatra kīdṛśī mahākṛti, kovā gahvaro viṣayarāja ityasyottaraṃ prabandhanāmaiva vivṛṇotīti brūmaḥ | kathaṃ? cidgaganaṃ nāma jñānakāśaḥ dāharābhinnaḥ | candrikā khalu candrakāntiḥ | sā ca svasvadharmādharmaviṣayakarāgadveṣabhedabhinnamatobuddhikalātulyena, trivṛtkatapañcākṣarīmayena, pañcadaśākṣarīmayena vā, vāgarthaṣaḍadhvagarbhitārthādhvatrikamayena, tādṛśenaiva nādabindukalātripuṭītrivṛtkṛtapañcākṣarīmayena, pañcadaśākṣarīmayena vā, vāgarthaṣaḍadhvagarbhitenaivākṣamālāsu śābdādhvatrikatulyena ca, sāṅketikaparibhāṣayā deśakālābhinnaprakṛtipuruṣaikarūpeṇeti saṃgrahavākyamiti bodhyam | tādṛśena śuklakṛṣṇapakṣatithyādibhedena tattadīyacandrakalāprabhedanena vā "pūrṇatākṛśatayoryadantaram" ityādinā prathamavimarśānte, "vāsareśarajanīśayoḥ kalā" iti dvitīye, evameva tṛtīyaturīyavimarśayośca tatra tatra vakṣyamāṇena deśakālamayamānavapitrādivyāvahārikakālamānaprabhṛtideśakālātītatvena śuddhapāramārthikadaivapitrantakānlamāna kalpanāprabhedena "maharṣayatsapta pūrve catvāro manavastathā | madbhāvā mānasā jātāḥ yeṣāṃ loka imāḥ prajāḥ" iti gītācāryoktalakṣaṇacaturyugādicaturdaśayugāntamahāmānavapāramarṣasāstrīyavyavahārepi chandaḥ kalākālakarmatripucīkalpaprabhṛti caturyugasahasrabrāhmaparyantaṃ tṛtīyavimarśe vakṣyamāṇabrahmavigrahakalādhirohaṇārthaṃ chandaḥkalākālakarmatripuṭīkalpabhedena ca, tattadādhikārikavidyā'vidyāsampadabṛhaṇe krameṇa kramākramaśabditābhyāṃ vṛddhikṣayābhyāmantataḥ kulākulasāmarasyātmakaṃ kālīkālamithunatādātmyāpādanameva pāryantikaṃ brahmavigrahakalāvirohaṇaṃ vaidikam | tāntriko vā mahārthaśca taccādhirohaṇaṃ mahārtho vā | yāmalāditantrāgamadṛśā sattātrayāṅgīkāreṇa prakṛtipuruṣavibhedasya vyavahārasattāyāmantarbhāvena cottamādyadhikāraprabhedābhinnaṃ paścimādyūrdhvāmnāyāntatricatuḥpañcārthamahākramaikarūpaṃ vā pramātrāditripuṭīsāmarasyātmā ahaṃgrahopāsanārūpaḥ pāryantiko mahārthaḥ | vedadṛśā tu, vyavahārasattāyā anaṅgīkārātprātibhāsikapāramārthikasattādvayaśabditaprakṛtipuruṣamātrasāmarasyātmā turīyaḥ paramapuruṣārthaḥ | mahāpuruṣasvarūpameva vā | iti tāntrikavaidikadṛgvivekena kālaśivasya kāraṇopādheḥ karmamayaśivasya kāryopādhervā brahmavigrahakalādhirohaṇaṃ nirṇeyam | tatā hi ekaikasmin brāhme dine tadīye karmamaye vā kalpe caturdaśamanvantarāṇi khalu yāpayitavyāni | tatra catasraḥ daśā avasthāḥ yeṣāṃ manvantarāṇāṃ tāni caturdaśāni, caturdaśasaṅkhyākāni ca | ityādi vyutpattibhedena turīyavimarśe "yā daśāsu nikhilāsu sarvataḥ" ityādinā vakṣyamāṇaguptibhāṣitajagadvijṛmbhaṇalakṣaṇena gāyatryādijagatīchandaḥparyantatayā krameṇa caturakṣarottaraṃ vaidikaṃ saptachandomayamūrtikaṃ, tāntrikaṃ ca pañcapañcadaśākṣa?rīprabhṛtisaptadaśāṣṭādaśākṣaraparyantaṃ vaisargikadaśāmithunasāmarasyena svarākṣarakadambamūrtikaṃ mahāṣoḍhaśīkalāmūrtikaṃ ca jāgataṃ māntravīryameva, vāgarthaṣaḍadhvaśarīreṇānyonyatripuṭīgarbhitena śiṣyaughādiparaughāntagurvaughaṣaḍadhvaśaktirūpeṇa pariṇatā, mātṛkāmayī, vāgarthaṣaḍadhvaśaktireveti mahākavinā prabandhesmin uccaiḥ kaṇṭharavaṇaivoddhoṣitam | etadevāntaḥ puruṣe kālīprakṛtiḥ kulavisagamithunātmanā bahiśca kālī, kālaprakṛtiḥ puruṣātmanā ca "antarbahiḥpūruṣakālarūpaṃ"? ityādinā vedavedāṅgapurāṇetihāsakāvyaparyantaṃ kalpapañcakabhāratavāṅmayecoddhoṣya guptibhāṣitajagadvijṛmbheṇa māntravarṇalakṣaṇena sadyojātādipañcabrahmamayamukhapañcakātmanāpi varṇitamiti prakāśavimarśaikarūpayā susūkṣmadṛśākalanīyam | atra parantvanāvṛttacakṣuṣkatādoṣeṇa puruṣāpacāreṇāparakālasarpadaṣṭānāṃ tadviṣavegasahakṛtena digdeśakālakalātmikāmātmīyāṃ brāhmīṃ tripuṭīmanātmatayā bahiḥsṛṣṭāviva paśyatāṃ ataeva svatejodarśināmavikṛtāvapi vikṛtacetasāmācāryamanyānāṃ tatra tatra dṛgdoṣanibandhanāmatibhedātsamupasthāsyantītyapi sārvajanīnametat | tatrāpi vimarśakādhikāratāratamyena kecana manubhānavādimānabhedeṣu vivekāndhyena tattadīyakalākālakalpaparibhramaṇādivimarśagocarāḥ | kecana tatrāpi durāgraheṇa jaḍabāhyasya bāhyajaḍasya vā bahirvastuno vimarśagocarāścetīyatī gamanikā | manuśabdasya mantratadarthavimarśaikabodhakatvāditi | tatra cidacinmithunameva kalpaḥ | tallakṣaṇe tu `rogaiśca kṣayito dehaḥ kalpa ityabhidhīyate | tatra tatsūcanātsūtraṃ kalpasūtraṃ vidurbudhāḥ' iti bhaviṣyottarapurāṇavacanam | tatra -- `ādyacitprasaramūrtirambike' iti tṛtīyavimarśādya ślokavākyārthena | tatraiva brahmavigrahakalādhirohiṇītyādimahākavisūktyā ca, brāhmaḥ kalponāma brahmaviṣṇurudreśvarasadāśivātmakapañcabrahmamūrtiṣu yathādhikāraṃ sādhakādhikāradaśāsvanyatamadaśāvatī anyatamā mūrtireva | tatra caturdaśamanavaḥ pūrvoktavigrahavyutpattibhedena manvantarāṇi vā pravṛttinivṛttidharmayorhetuphalabhedadṛṣṭyā tatsṛṣṭyā ca bījakāryamanusaptakayordvaitarūpeṇa vā dṛṅmātrakalpitabhedena hetuphalayorābhāsabhedacāturyeṇa vā | anuśabdasya mantramananārthasādhāraṇyena dehendriyasaṅghātasahabhāvitatvādyayādhikāraṃ, yathāyogaṃ ca manumānavāntaravibhāgo boddhavyaḥ | evameva tadīyāyuḥkalākalākalpepyekasaptatimahāyugamite paurāṇikepi kalpe yena kenacidgaṇanāmārgeṇa manvindrasaptarṣidivyaṣitryaprājāpatyabrāhmāntāni yugānyapi vyāvahārikena sauracāndrādimāsapañcakena sākaṃ yathāyogaṃ bodhyāni | sarveṣvapi mānavādinādibrāhmadināntaṃ dinakalpagaṇanāmāśrityaiva yathā kālamānaṃ `śatamānaṃ bhavati śatāyuḥ puruṣaśśatendriyaḥ' ityādiśrautamāyuḥparimāṇādi ca | evaṃ sthite prakṛte vāgarthaṣaḍadhve tatprakṛtau vā vaivasvatamanvantarīyeṣu tadīyāṣṭāviṃśatitamakaliyugīyeṣu vā'vatārapuruṣeṣu kālidāsamahākavicandrakalpīyāḥ matabhedāḥ bahudhā vasevartanta ityalaṅkāra eva tattadīyakalākālavigrahākṛteḥ kṛtādau dyūteva vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇena dyūtakarmaṇā vijitakṛtāyanāmakadurodarāṇāṃ vidvadvarāṇāṃ kālidāsīyakarmadṛśā tadīyakarmavigrahādhirohe tadīyāyā eva svasvaprakṛtervā | nanvāstāṃ tadīyakalākālavigrahamahākṛteradhirohaṇam | tattu -- `yena khelanasarohaṇārṇavaśśaśvadāviśati yena māṃ śivaḥ' iti prabandhānte svenaivoddhoṣitam | prakṛtau tadīyakarmavigrahakalādhirohasya prakṛtatvāttatra prabandhanāmarūpayorvāgarthavigrahavyutpattyā sākaṃ tadīyanāmarūpayorapi tadaikyapraṇālyā viṣayādyanubandhacatuṣṭayanirṇayena sākaṃ ca prameyahārthasārāṃśanirṇayopi kriyatāmiti cettarhi cidgaganaśabdārthavigrahasvarūpanirṇaye viṣayeciti gaganamiti vā cideva gaganaṃ gaganameva cidityabhedasāmānādhikaraṇyane vā athavā, cit gagane yatreti janmādyuttarapadena bahuvrīhimayena vā | tathaiva candrikāvāgarthavigrahasambandhaviṣayepi -- cidgagane candriketyādhikaraṇakatatpuruṣeṇa vā āhosvit, cit gagane yasyāssā cidgaganā, sā ca sā candriketi śāktena karmadhārayavigraheṇeti vā utāho, pūrvavat cidgaganameva candrikā candrikaiva cidgaganamityabhedapratipattirvā, ityevaṃprakārakaṣaḍvidhavigraheṣu vigrahadvayasyābhedapratipattyā vigrahapañcakāvaśeṣe, kenavā vigraheṇa? kathaṃ vā vigrahasvarūpe mahākṛteḥ prakṛtervā tattvanirṇayaḥ? kathaṃ vā tatsahāyena gahvaraviṣayarājādyanubandhacatuṣṭayasya vā tattvanirūpaṇam? ityādi praśnarāśessaduttaratayā mahākavicandreṇaiva gahvaro viṣayarājaḥ, prabandhātmanā, prabandho vā, viṣayarājātmanā kārakādhvavigrahaṣaṭkasāmarasyenaikayoktyā divākaraniśākaravadvipariṇamayya prasādhanīkṛtya ca prasādīkṛta ityeva prati samādhiriti nirvācyam | tatkathamiti cet `āśīrnamaskriyā vastunirdeśovāpi tanmukham' ityuktaprabandhamukhalakṣaṇe āśīrādyanyatamena sākamanubandhacatuṣṭayasya sūtraṇameva khalu prācīnamahākavisamayaḥ | tadatra ṣoḍaśīśabditamahāvaisargikīkalope taṃ puruṣasvarūpameveti `kṣīroda'miti maṅgalaśloke vacasaiva niruktam | taccāntardṛśā hṛdayodyogarūpaṃ, bāhyadṛśā tadupayuktadigdeśakālatripuṭītattvaikarūpaṃ, yadyapīdaṃ dvayaṃ rathūladṛśāṃ taccāturyeṇa mahāpativrataikapatnīvrasthamahāmānavaṃ mithunamivaikameva śrautaṃ vāgarthamithunaṃ, svarūpaeva susūkṣmavastudāmpatyarūpaṃ sphuraratīti lodavedayoḥ jīveśvarau, jagajjīvau, naranārāyaṇau, pārvatīparameśvarau, kālīkālau, guruśiṣyāvityādiśrautabhāratyā svāvinābhūtatattatparyāyanāmabhiḥ vyāvahārikadṛśā kalpanācāturyaparyantayā svātmadharmaprabhedakaṃ, prabodhakaṃ ca yathādhikāraṃ kramākramaśabditena vāgarthaṣaḍadhvakrameṇa ṣaṣṭhaṃ, saptamaṃ vā, pramātṛpramāṇāṃśadvayānubaddhamaṣṭamaṃ cetprameyāṃśena sākaṃ saprameyatripuṭīkaṃ navamaṃ, daśamaṃ ca vāgarthātmakameva mithunam | evaṃ ca pūrvoktaprabandhanāmanirūpepi triṣuṭīdvayātmake, ṣaḍvidhaśābdavigrahaparibhāṣayā kārakādhvaṣaṭkasaṅketitaiṣṣaḍaṅgaṣaḍdarśanādibhiḥ paryāyanāmabhiḥ paryāyanāmabhiḥ tattadabhidheyārthaṣaḍadhvavigrahaparibhāṣayā manobuddhiyutadaśendriyacakre puruṣasvarūpe pumarthasampadi ca tricatuḥpañcavāhavāgarthatripuṭīṣu samaṣṭiyaṣṭibhyāṃ vāgarthasantantyāṃ ṣaḍdvādaśoḍaśānteṣu mahāvindvekarūpatvāt, tathā digdeśakālatripuṭītattvepi sāyanasya vatsarasya malamāsasya vā śiśiravasantayoranyatarasyārtośca vā kalākālavāgarthatripuṭyossamaṣṭivyaṣṭibhyāṃ dvibhinnaikarūpatvācca | tadetattripuṭīdvayaṃ trayaṃ vā prākṛtaṃ kālapuruṣamithunaṃ, anyataraṃ vā mithunasya tātvikaṃ turīyameva rūpamiti ca | tadutkṣipyopāsyakoṭau, upakṣipya vā upāsakakoṭau, avaśiṣṭatripuṭīpañcake dvicatuḥpañcavāhaikarūpe kalākālapravartakamagnīṣomasūryamaṇḍalatrayasamaṣṭivyaṣṭibhyāmapi turīyamahārthasaṅketitaṃ svarūpameveti | śaivaśāktatantrāgamādihakramaparyantaḥ kṛtyapañcakavyavahāraśca | tadatraivaṃbhūtabhautikadṛgviparyāyaśabditena sṛṣṭidṛṣṭivādasiddhāntakramāvalambena śaivaśāktāditantrāgamasampradāyopāsakāḥ, pañcakodṛśamavalambyākramamārgeṇa prātibhāsikadṛksaṃhāraśabditena dṛṣṭisṛṣṭivādasiddhāntakrameṇa vaidikopāsakāśca, pāramārthikasadvastusvarūpasyā śābdatvena svarūpasattāmeva pāramārthikaṃ vyāvahārikaprātibhāsikasattayorviparivartya bahudhā(')nyaprapañcīkṛtya ca svarūpameva tatvāvabodhāya varṇayāmāsuriti | "akramakramavimarśalakṣaṇam" ityādinā dvitīyavimarśe dvitīyaślokena kaṇṭharaveṇa mahākavirevāsūtrayat | tadāstāṃ tāvat | prakṛtau pūrvoktahṛdayodyogavāgartharūpayoḥ cidgaganacandrikāśabdārthayoṣṣaḍdarśaneṣvanyatamena darśanena vigraheṇa vā'ndhagajanyāyenāpi svarūpasāmarasyāvabodhāya vaidikyā antardṛśā saccidānandāparanāmakaṃ guṇatrayameveti | vaiṣayikyā tantrāgamādisiddhyā ca bahirdṛśā tejobannāparaparyāya pramātrāditripuṭīti śāktaśāmbavāṇavavisargatrayāparaparyāyā agnīṣomasūryadhāmatrayatripuṭīti vā turīyasvarūpatatvasyaiva trivṛtkṛtya nirūpaṇe vibhajyālam | tathāpyasyāḥ kṛtiprakṛtestantraprabandharūpatvena tāntrikī paribhāṣaiva tattadabhidheyārthavarṇanāyopayujyate na vaidikīti prāyeṇa saivāśrīyate | vaidikyapi tattadghaṭeṣu tadupāsakalokopakārāya prāsaṅgikī nirūpyata eveti vyākhyāparipāṭī | tadatra tantraparibhāṣayā śāktavisargo nāma sṛṣṭisthitisaṃhārānākhyābhāsākhyānulomakṛtyapañcake caramasya virodhānānugrahāparanāmakānākhyābhāsākhyakṛtyamithunasya vyutkrameṇa saṃhārasthitināmake kṛtyadvaye sṛṣṭyādikṛtyatrayamadhyakendre vā mahāsthitau antarbhāvya, evamanākhyābhāsāgarbhite sṛṣṭyādikṛtyatrayepi ādyantasthityā kramākramaśabditaṃ sṛṣṭisaṃhārākhyaṃ vyutkrameṇa saṃhārasṛṣṭiśabditaṃ vā kṛtyadvayaṃ kṛtyābhāsatayā svātmano mahāsthitau cāntarbhāvya, tādṛśakṛtyacatuṣṭayagarbhitamahāsthiteśśaktipūrṇatayā pūrṇaśaktirūpatayā vā nirantaramithunībhūtaprakṛtipuruṣātmakaśivaśaktimithunapūrṇasvarūpākāṃkṣitayā udayadyauvanāyāṃ tasyāṃ mahāsthitau mahāśaktyāṃ vā `daśāsyāṃ putranādhehi patimekādaśaṃ kṛdhi' `karāṃgulinakhotpannanārāyaṇadaśākṛtiḥ' ityādiśrutismṛtiprasiddhe, ṣaḍaṅgapūrṇe, daśendriyadaśopete vidyāvīryavaṃśoddhārakacidacitsamarasaputrotpattidvāreṇa svasya svaśakteśca ṣaḍaṅgatāpūrṇamāvāya ṣaḍaṅgapūrṇa iva svayamapyavāptaśaktitādātmyabhāvena cittaprabodhaikalakṣaṇatvādaṣṭādaśaśśaktivisarga ucyate | śāmbhavavisargastu ānandabhūmirātmākhalu śambhuśabdābhidheyabhūtaśśakyārthaḥ | saca pūrvoktasṛṣṭyādyanulomatricatuḥ paṃcārthakṛtyeṣu vijñānapūrṇatayā tadīyabāhyānandānubhave tṛpyopyantarmukhasvaśāktānandepi pūrṇānubhavatātparyeṇa bahirmukhatayā cittāvaśiṣṭānāṃ naṣṭārthacaṣkāvabhāsādinirvikalpakameyāvabhāsāntānāṃ vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇānāṃ vāsanābījānāṃ bījāṃkurāṇāṃ vā saṃhārakriyayā ābhyantaracidagnimūrtyā svātmaśaktau svayameva hotṛtayā cittapralayaikalakṣaṇatvātsaptadaśaśśambhava iti vyavahriyate | athāṇavopi visargaḥ svayamasampūrṇajīvāṇurūpeṇārthāṇavabindurūpatayātmaviśrāntimevāvidyakīṃ svarūpapūrṇatāṃ vā kāmayānaḥ pūrvoktamahāsthitilābhaparyantaṃ śāktaśāmbhavavisargayo rantarāle praveśaniveśāmyāṃ śāktavisargagarbhapāṭhena ca pūrṇākṣatāmakṣapūrṇatāṃ cāpekṣamāṇaḥ svayaṃ tayaiva viśrāmyati nāhantayā'nāhatavisargapūrṇayeti cittaviśrāntilakṣaṇoyaṃ pañcadaśaṣṣoḍaśo vā visarga iti tantrālokatantrasāralaghuvivṛttyādiṣu visargatrayalakṣyalakṣaṇasamanvayo vistareṇa draṣṭavyaḥ | etadeva visargatrayaṃ lokepi vāgarthaṣaḍadhve liṅgatrayaparibhāṣayā strīpuṃnapuṃsakaliṅgatrayātmanā prathamamadhyamottamapuruṣatrayātmanā ca vyavahriyate | ityevamuktalakṣaṇopetaṃ visargatrayamanuttarākṛtyā vedituṃ yadi camacakṣuṣāpi didṛkṣettarhi mātṛkāsvarakhaṇḍānte śāktavisargadāmpatyaṃ tailasārpirbindudvayatulyenāgnīṣomīyavisargātmanā, śāmbhavaṃ ca sauryaṃ mahāsaumyaṃ vā'dvitīyapūrṇānusvārātmanā, āṇavaṃ cārthībhūtamarthānusvārārthavisargānuttarākārādidisthūlarekhākāritaṃ varṇamayakulaśarīreṇa ca kaulānuttarasiddhāntamaryādayā trikasiddhāntarahasyavedibhiḥ spandasiddhāntarahasyavādibhi rvā vimarśakadhaurayairmātṛkāvāgarthaparibhāṣāsamanvayasadbhāvapūrṇabhāvābhyāṃ gurumukhena vā vivicya jñātavyam | lokepi mātṛpitṛputralakṣaṇaiḥ kāryaśarīraiḥ sṛṣṭyādikṛtyatrayakriyāśarīraiḥ dharmāditrivargamahārthaśarīrairagnīṣomādidhāmatrayakāraṇaśarīraiśca vivicya vijñāya dvādaśānte mahāpramātrādipāryaṃtikapañcadaśyāṃ mahākāraṇatripuṭyāmante ṣoḍaśyāṃ ṣoḍaśānte ca yathādhikāramupāsitavyaṃ ca | atrāyamāṇavo visarga eva vāgarthaṣaḍadhva jīvasaṃsāre ca sādhakasvarūpamityapi | śāktaśāmbhavavisargadvayaṃ tu bindutrayanirvācyaṃ sat suvyaktaśāktaśāmbhavanādanavakabhedena | mātṛkāyāṃ svakhaṇḍānte cāntarbahistripuṭīṣaḍadhvayoryayopacaritavyaṣṭisamaṣṭibhyāṃ dvādaśaṣoḍaśāntanādavāgarthākṣanavakāntamunmanīmahānādabinduparyantaṃ pāryantikamidhunabhedena ca yāvanmahārdhasidhyavadhikaṃ sādhyarūpameveti ca | sphuṭoccāraṇena vyaktidaśāyāmapi nādavāgarthaṣaḍadhvātmanā kartṛkārakādikriyākāryāntākṣamākāsu bhedasphuraṇepyāṇave vāgarthakrame vyaṣṭisamaṣṭibhyāmagnīṣomādidhāmatrayatripuṭīrūpeṇāpi kāraṇābhinnameveti sūcitapūrvam |
tatrāṇavavisargo jīvaṃ evānubandhacatuṣṭaye mūlabhūtodhikārī | saca kiñcidāṇavamalamātrāvaśeṣeṇa janmani yatra kālāvayave vā jananamaraṇapravāhe kālavegenohyamānaśśāktavisargagarbhe patati | tadeva tasya jīvasaṃsāre caramaṃ janma tadīyaḥ kālanāśo vā | sa ca tadā prapañcapañcapetāsanāsīnāyā mahākālasaṅkarṣiṇīparadevatāyāḥ mañcapādeṣu prathamaḥ "brahmaṇāṃ brahmaṇaspate" ityādi māntravarṇikagāṇāpatyavāgarthaṣaḍadhvarūpatvādeva śaktiputratvena vyavahṛto gaṇeśāvatāratayā, śāmbhavavisargapatitaścet ṣaḍānanaśivakumāratayā vā, karmabhūmāvavatarati | idameva rahasyam | "śaktijanmā gaṇeśaḥ" iti granthādau maṅgalaślokacaramapādena, dvitīyavimarśe "yāvadambapuruṣasya śānta" tetyādinā ca rūpitaṃ, tadatra pūrvoktanādavāgarthapramātrādiśaktiṣu yasyāḥ kasyāścidvā śaktergarbhasamudgakādviśvottīrṇassanneva svātantryādbhūmāvavataran tṛtīyavimarśe "akṣacakranivahokrasaṅkhyayā" ityetat śloke vakṣyamāṇaprakāreṇāntarbahirakṣārthaviṣayagaṇānābhīśitṛtayā gaṇeśa iti vyavahriyate | idamevādhikāriṇaḥ svarūpam | evaṃ sambandhopi | uktagaṇeśāvatāraprabhṛti sādāśivamañcaphalakaparyantaṃ pañcabrahmāvatāreṣu tattadavatārakrameṇāntato mahākālīkālamithunatādātmyenānabhedenavā pañcavāhamahārthakramāparaparyāyasya sādhakahṛdayodyogasyeva yathādhikāraṃ gahvaraviṣayarājasvarūpatayā tasya prabandhena sākaṃ pratipādyapratipādakabhāvaḥ, sāvakena sākaṃ prāpyaprāpakabhāvaśca sambandha iti sambandhaviṣayayoḥ svarūpam | tathā prayojanaṃ | sādhakasya maṅgalaśloke caramapādasūcitaśaktigarbhapraveśadvāreṇa viṣṇurudrabhāvādikramagrāsamukhena ca `īśvaro bahirunmeṣo nimeṣontassadāśivaḥ' iti tantrāntarokteśvarabhāvasyaikānekajanmānubhavakrameṇavā, pañcamabrahmabhāvamapyatītya `cillocanena satataṃ śivamīkṣitu'miti granthādau turīyaślokapratijñātasamanvayasadbhāvapūrṇabhāvābhyāmantataḥ parasadāśivamithunasvarūpāpattiḥ | tayā `hṛdguhāmabhilaṣannahanta' yeti granthādau pratijñātaviruddhasakhyahāniśca prayojanamiti prayojanasvarūpanirūpamaṃca | evaṃ granthādau ślokacatuṣṭayena, pañcakena vā anubandhacatuyaṃ mahākavinaiva sūtritam | tatsvarūpavistarastu sādhakasya tantrāgamaparibhāṣayā māyīyakārmaṇāṇavāparanāmakaṃ malatrayaṃ krameṇa sthūlasūkṣmakāraṇopādhitrayamāśritya tadīyanaiśreyasābhyudayayoḥ pratibandhakamiva pramādālasyādidṛgdoṣeṇa sphuraṇaṃ tadīyajaḍaprakṛterlakṣaṇam | tasyāḥ bhrāntyekaśarīratvānubhavāditi brūmaḥ | tathāhi -- `māyikaṃ nāma yoṣotthaṃ malaṃ bhedāvahaṃ matam | vā, bhedaprathāmāyīyaṃ' iti lakṣaṇātpāṃcabhautikadehādāviva bāhyepi jagati svarūpeṇāsato bhedasya sphuraṇāpādanaṃ bhrāntireveti bāhyairmādhyamikādibhirapyabhyupeyate | tathā kārmaṇamapi `śarīravāṅmanobhiryatkarmaprārambhate naraḥ | nyāyyaṃ vā viparītaṃ vā paṃcaite tasya hetavaḥ || tatraiva sati kartāramātmānaṃ kevalaṃ tu yaḥ | paśyatyaketubuddhitvānna sa paśyati durmatiḥ' ityādibhagavaduktyā yaugikīṃ kartṛtāmātmani kevalāsatīmadhyāropya "kimahagai sādhunā karavaṃ ! kimahaṃ pāpamakaravamiti" ityādinā taittirīyabrahmavalyuktaśubhāśubhakartṛtā'kartṛtāprayuktatāpatrayātmakamidamapi bhrānteḥ prakārāntaram | evamāṇamavamapi -- "svātantryahānirbodhasya svātantryasyāpyabodhatā | dvidhāṇavaṃ malaṃ proktaṃ svasvarūpāpahānitaḥ" ityuktadvividhaliṅgayā bhrāntyā svarūpavibhutvādisampado vismṛtirevetīdaṃ tritayamapi bhrāntirūpamevetyatra naviśayaleśopi | tadetattritaye `kṣīroda'mityādiślokatrayakrameṇaikaikaślekenaikaikamāśīḥ pūrvakaṃ nirasya turīye nirmalajñānanetreṇeśvarasādāśivamithunarūpasyātmaśivasya sākṣātkāreṇa taccikīrṣayā vā saṃsāradāvānaladagdhasya tatsamanvayasadbhāvapūrṇabhāvābhyāṃ viśvottīrṇatāmayapūrṇānandānubhavāya svasvacidgaganacandrikāprabandhavimarśasyeva svavitīrṇasya mukhyopāyatāmāvedya samaṣṭiprabandhena varṇayitavyeṣu nādavāgarthaṣaḍadhvabhedeṣu saccidānandatripuṭīsamaṣṭirūpayornādanādhanavakayordvitīyavimarśe vyāvahārikamātṛmānameyātmakatripuṭīvarṇanena tṛtīye pāramārthikapramātrādi mahātripuṭīvarṇanena turīye ca phalībhūtamahāturīyatripuṭyāḥ cinmayanāmarūpāṃśayoḥ svarūpe kramākramaśabditayoḥ sṛṣṭisaṃhārakramayoḥ paryāvartanena ca krameṇavyāvahārikamātṛpramāṇatripuṭīśabdite kriyākāryākṣanavakadvaye'pi viśvottīrṇayoḥ viśvābhāsacinmayaprakṛtipuruṣayoraikyavimarśena sūtraprāye prathamavimarśe "hṛdguhāmabhilaṣa"nnityādibhiraṣṭādaśabhiḥ sūtrātmaprāyaissūtraślokairvarṇitasvātmaikyasiddhaye svīyamahākṛterādinādhāparaparyāyanāmnasiddhanādhabhaṭṭārakasya kramastutyaparaparyāyakṛteḥ pañcikākhyayā viśvarūpavivartaprakṛtyā vyākhyārūpatānavottaratriśatatame" "muktameva na maye" tyākārake śloke kaṇṭharaveṇoddhoṣitetyanubandhacatuṣṭayena sākaṃ siddhasyeva sādhakasyāpi kālidāsasya pūrroktaṣaḍvidhavigrahaikyaṃ jñānavarṇavamahānayaprakāśādisūcitaṃ ṣaḍvidhaṣaḍadhvagurvaughakramaikyena sākaṃ śiṣyaughānujighṛkṣayā svānandānububhūṣayā ca mahākavicandrasya vāgarthaṣaḍadhvaprakṛtyā varṇitamiti sudhiyo vibhāvayantu | turīye vimarśe "tvaṃ navārṇamanucakravigrahe" ityetasmin ślokecedamevaikyaṃ vitatya vyākhyātaṃ tatravai draṣṭavyam | iti prabandhārthaprameyāṃśasārarāṃśaśceti dik | iti nivedayitā -- granthakṛt ||

vyākhyopakramaślokāḥ ||
oṃ namaḥ parānuttarasiddhāntavidyāsampradāyapravartakebhyo vaṃśarṣibhyo namaḥ paraughādibhyo mahadbhayo munibhyo namo gurubhyaḥ | sarvopaplavarahitaḥ prajñānaghanaḥ pūrṇapratyagartho brahmaivāhamasmi ||

svaranādāvivānyonyasaṃpṛktau svopalabdhaye |
sadānandasvarūpau tau vande(')kāra(la)yugātmakau || 2 ||
śrīkāmākṣyāndanāthamithune praṇato(vo)smi kim |
yadanugrahatastantramārgo me gahano'sphurat || 3 ||
ānandanāthāparadaikṣanāmā kāmākṣibhāmāhitapūrṇadhāmā |
śrīdeśikendre'cyutapūrṇasomaḥ(ṣṭamavīrarāmaḥ)ṣaḍadhvadhāmā pihitasvasīmā
parāprāsādamārūḍhassauvarṇopavane caran |
mānasānandanārāmo vyākurve kṛtimanvaha (hamimāṃ kṛtim) || 5 ||
atrivaṃśasamudbhūtiragnimūrterdvināmadhṛt |
edhīkurvannupapadaṃ daṇḍakramasamudbhavam || 6 ||
paradṛgdoṣaśāntyarthamidhmavāhaṃ kalebaram |
gṛhākhyānendhanairgotre dahannasmi (sti)sunirvṛtaḥ || 7 ||
āśāyāmuttarāyāṃ hi kuherosmi (sti) yatassvayam |
dhanadānandanādheti gurumūrtiprasādi(ri)tam || 8 ||
vidyādhane'nvarthapadaṃ dhṛtvā syāṃ prerito balāt |
kriyārsiddhirmahīyaṃ cetsatyā(tve)syāditi vi(niḥ)śvasan || 9 ||
kālidāsakṛteḥ kurve(ryādvyā)vyākhyāṃ divyacakorikām |
cadrikāsvādanānmattāmunmattāṃ tadrasādṛte || 10 ||
namaskaromi satataṃ karma kiṃ tannakathyate |
kiṃ dampatimiti bṝyāmutāho dampatī iti || 11 ||
arthaicityepi śabdasyānaucityādādimaṃ na cet |
śabdaucityepyanarthasvabhāvādevāntimaṃ na hi || 12 ||
tasmānnamaskriyākarma kathituṃ naiva śakyate |
tadbhāvāpattirevātra tṛtīyā gatiriṣyate || 13 ||
turye tādātmyasaṃvittiḥ sā kāle nopalabhyate |
mahānivedanaṃ tatra turyātītāmiti smṛtam || 14 ||
kvāhamantarma(haṃ mandama)tirbālaḥ kva mahākavivāg‌jharī |
titīrṣuṃ dustarāṃ tāṃ māṃ nayetpāraṃ guroḥ padam || 15 ||
kṣantumarhatiṃ me santaḥ sāhasaṃ(ṣoḍaśaṃ)bālacāpalam |
yā jātā ityoṣadhayayo devebhyastriyugaṃ purā || 16 ||
ityoṣadhīśasūktādau yogastraipura īritaḥ |
parokṣapriyatā ceti pratyakṣadveṣitā tathā || 17 ||
vedeṣvapica deveṣu śabdaprathānyajṛmbhaṇāt |
tadeva tricatuḥpañcavāgarthānāṃ yugaṃ matam || 18 ||
tadevānuttare dhāmni deśakālādhvanorapi |
tadrahasyaprakāśena sādhakānujighṛkṣayā || 19 ||
kṛtā nu kṛtiratraiṣā ṣaḍadhvavivṛtichalāt |
tadarthavivṛtau sākṣātpravṛttāpi prasaṅgataḥ || 20 ||
vādajalpakathākṣuṇṇā trayīṣaḍdarśaneṣu ca |
yathāmati madīyāpi tadviveke(kā)kṛtirbhavet || 21 ||
atra māmanugṛhṇātu yenāsmi kila coditaḥ |
kālidāsakavernāma sūpālabdheva dīrghikā || 22 ||
hrasvaṃ māmavajānantī kalidāsye nyamajjayat |
trayyante tatra sūrttīrṇastraivikramagiraṃ smaran || 23 ||
tripādyānnāchalātpañcavāhatāṃ balisampadam |
āruhya gurave devarājāyā'dāṃ guroḥ śriyam || 24 ||
paricchado mamahyatra bhūcārī daṇḍataḥ kramaḥ |
nāma(mūrti)mālā varṇamālā yasyaiśvaryamanantakam || 25 ||
tadantasthaḥ svairacāraḥ svecchā('')cāraniyantritā(śāstraniyantritaḥ) (trirāmaviniyantritaḥ ) |
svabhāstatra mokṣassyādanuttaramahārthavān || 26 ||
kalyantasiddhimārme'sminvaikharī pañcavāhatā |
evaṃ khaṇḍakathā yāsau kathā parikathā'stu me || 27 ||
kṛte tu mānavā dharmāstretāyāṃ gautamāḥ smṛtāḥ |
dvāpare śaṅkhalikhitāḥ kalau pārāśarā matāḥ || 28 ||
kṛtetu reṇukā śaktiḥ tretāyāṃ janakātmajā |
dvāpare draupadī śaktiḥ kalau śaktistu kālikī(kā) || 29 ||
kalyante nāsti tacchāstraṃ śaktirvā kali (kāla)vigrahā |
tasmātsarvaprayatnena kalyante kālikīṃ yajet (kalimudvahet) || 30 ||
vaidikaṃ kālamithunaṃ (vaidikaṃ maithunaṃ kāla(pāpa) kalanāśamanaṃ hi tat |
śrutaṃ tadvāmadevye'pi sāmanīśamataṃ bhavet || 31 ||
anyathā dvādaśāntoktasantaternāśasambhavaḥ |
jñānārṇavādyuktadūtīyāgayogavithau smṛtaḥ || 32 ||
gurūṇāṃ puṣpasaṅkocakramopyatra nidarśanam |
so'pi tatraiva paṭale ṣoḍaśe niyamācchṛtaḥ || 33 ||
tade(sae)vehāpi niyatamanubandhacatuṣṭaye |
yathādhikāraṃ vijñeyaṃ nādavāgarthasantatau || 34 ||
śrutipāṭhe kramajaṭāghanārohāvarohavat |
triṣaḍadhvadvādaśāntaniyamo nādavartmani || 35 ||
aidranīlamaṇiklṛptavigrahaṃ klīṃkarāṃghrikṛtacidviśākhajam |
sauryasaumyarasanetradūṣitaṃ(kaṃ)kālikālayugamūrti(mārti)daṃ śiraḥ || 36 ||
arthairalamalamiti śābdikasumanaḥstragunmanāpāṅgī |
bindutrayarasacarvaṇasambhramāvirutā cakāstu bhramarāmbā(samarembā) || 37 ||
mātṛkāśābdavijaye yo vāgarthaikaniṣṭhitaḥ |
digdeśakālatripuṭīparārthaikaprayojanaḥ || 38 ||
śabdabrahmasuniṣpattyā paraṃ brahmādhigacchati |
evameṣa mahārtho'pi vāgarthaikaṣaḍadhvavān || 39 ||
prasṛtābhyantarajyotsnaḥ śaratkālendumaṇḍalaḥ |
ṣaṭ‌triṃśatā ślokajālaiḥ saṃdṛbdho vipulakramaḥ || 40 ||
kudeśikamahāgharmasaṃtaptaissevyatāṃ ciram |
kṣipraṃ hi mānuṣe loke siddhirbhavati karmajā || 41 ||
tadidaṃ karma sahajamāgantukamiti dvidhā |
sahajaṃ śābdikaṃ karma janmanā saha śabdanāt || 42 ||
āgantukaṃ tvārthikaṃ syāttasya cāgantukatvataḥ |
tatra pūrvatarā'dhīnā siddhirmānuṣalaukikā || 43 ||
śābde brahmaṇi siddhiḥ syādbrāhmī kṣātrī tu laukikī |
vaiśyānāmarthasiddhirhi gatireṣā sanātanī || 44 ||
kṛtāvasyāṃ prameyādisūcinī suvimṛśyatām |
mahāpramātṛtāmātravimarśaikanibandhanaḥ || 45 ||
sūtraprāyetra purataḥ tricatuḥpañcavāhakaiḥ |
mahākramairgarbhitastu saṃkṣepeṇātra sūtritaḥ || 46 ||
dvitīye śāktavijñānakriyāsaṃsṛtigarbhitaḥ |
pramātā vyavahāropayuktanādaikaviśramaḥ || 47 ||
vicāritastṛtīye tu mātṛkāgarbhaniṣṭhitaḥ |
vāgarthaikaṣaḍadhve'sya tricatuḥpañcakṛtyake || 48 ||
dhāmasaṃvidvarṇagaṇakramaprāmāṇyajṛmbhaṇāt |
mūlahṛtphālakaviladvādaśāntaikaviśramāt || 49 ||
pīṭhaśmaśānamelāpakṣetrayāgakramasthitau |
dhāmamudrāvarṇakalāsaṃvidbhāvasvabhāvataḥ || 50 ||
pātāniketadṛṣṭyā (sṛ)ṣṭyāca bṛndacakraprakāśanāt |
visargatritatayasyākṣamālāvirbhāvahetukaḥ || 51 ||
antarniriṃdhanajyotirbahirmūrtyaṣṭakātmavān |
mūrtyānandaprakāśeṣu bṛndacakrāhamakṣavān || 52 ||
jñānakriyopāsanāsu sakalāsu vyavasthiteḥ |
pramāṇāhaṃbhāvayuktaḥ śāmbhavo'tra vivecitaḥ || 53 ||
turīye tu turīyāhamakṣamatrāvalambanaḥ |
mahāpramātrahaṃbhāve svarūpe vinivartitaḥ || 54 ||
mahāprameyāhamakṣavicāro vipulakramaḥ |
lolitākhi(ku)laviśvāhamakṣakramavidīpanāt || 55 ||
viśvatādātmyatassarvaviśvottīrṇasvabhāvavān |
kramāṇāṃ vaiparītyena śāktaśāmbhavasargayoḥ || 56 ||
guptisambhāṣitajagatsarvasvātmavijṛmbhaṇāt |
kulākulānusandhānasāmarasyasvabhāvataḥ || 57 ||
mahākau(kā)lasvadharmeṇa dvādaśāntaikaviśramāt |
mahāprakāśaciccandraścidvimarśaprakāśakaḥ || 58 ||
navākāśavikāsena nādavarṇakramaikyataḥ |
mahāprameyāhamakṣakramaḥ pīṭhe vicāritaḥ || 59 ||
vāṅmayo'yaṃ mahādūtīyāgaśśāmbhavanāyakaḥ |
sāṅgopāṅgo mahāhantāhavanānta iheritaḥ || 60 ||
anuttaramahāsampadadhikāryatra pūjyate |
mahāprameyāhamakṣasādhakaiḥ suvimṛśya(gya)tām || 61 ||
anuttaramahākārapañcārthaiḥ kṛtyapañcake |
ārthiko'pi mahāyāgaṛ svādhikārānurūpataḥ || 62 ||
arthibhiḥ satu sampādyassvānarthavinivārakaḥ (ṇe) |
tatrānarthotpādahetuḥ svadṛgvailakṣyamevahi || 63 ||
evamarthānarthasāmye yeṣāṃ nirduṣṭamaṃtaram |
nirdoṣaṃ hi samaṃ brahma tasmādbrahmaṇi te sthitāḥ || 64 ||
na prahṛṣyetpriyaṃ prāpyetyādigītānuśāsanam |
pārthāyopadideśātra bhagavāndevakīsutaḥ || 65 ||
sarvavedāntasiddhāntasāro'yaṃ hṛdayaṃ mahat |
anuttaramahāsaṃvitsiddhānto'trāpi (traiva) dṛśyatām || 66 ||
athāpyanuttare dhāmni gītācāryānuśāsanāt |
pratyabhijñāpanādduṣṭapravṛtterduḥkhahetutā || 67 ||
parāprāsādanādoyaṃ vāgarthaikamahākṣavān |
janmamūlādhārahetuḥ pañcāśaddeśabhāṣitaiḥ || 68 ||
anuttaramahāmūrtiṣṣaḍadhve pañcavāhake |
mahāprāṇaspaṃdarūpaṣṣaḍadhvaikaikalakṣaṇaḥ || 69 ||
pañcavāhasvabhāvena śākto'nyo nāda uttaraḥ |
uttamaśca mahāprāṇanādo (dho) nyaśśāmbhavopi saḥ || 70 ||
iti viracitamārānnādavāgarthakṛtyaiḥ
prakaṭitaparamārthairjñānavijñānagarbhaiḥ |
pratiyajanavidhānaiṣṣaḍvidhaikyena yuktaiḥ
praṇavaparabhadevyāḥ tṛptimāpādayennaḥ (dvaḥ) || 71 ||
ślokaprameyasūcī tu vākyairatra vilikhyate |
sāpyatropakarotveṣā bhuvane'pi vilokyatām || 72 ||
nādavāgarthatattvasya vyākhyāne mārgaśodhanam |
atattvaviduṣāmatra svājñānasya vijṛmbhaṇam || 73 ||
idaṃ hi śāstramāhātmyadarśanālasacetasām |
apabhāṣaṇavadbhāti naca saubhāgyamujjhati || 74 ||
manvindrāderivaikaikakālamāne yugakrame |
ślokānāmekasaptatyā yugasaṅkhyāpi rūpitā || 75 ||
kalāvāgārthatattvepi yugasaṅkhāpramāṇataḥ |
sa ṣaḍadhve brahmakalpe manusaṅkhyāvabuddhyatām || 76 ||
evaṃ kalākālayoge yugaprastāra iṣyatām |
vāstutadvarṣakhaṇḍāderekāśītipadāvadhi || 77 ||
parārthāvadhikāpīha saṅkhyā vāgarthaśaṃsinī |
arbudāyetyādimantre svāhāntena suyojitā || 78 ||
prakaraṇehyaśvamedhetra vāgvārbudamitīraṇāt |
nyarbudāyetyādike tu svāhāṃtena tathā punaḥ || 79 ||
yovai vaco yadbhūmāsyāttanyarbudamitīritam |
tasmāttadubhayādhīnā mantrasiddhirhi laukikī || 80 ||
mātṛkāyā laukikīsyādvarivasyā satāṃ mude |
ityayaṃ mātṛkāvāstumaṇḍapo viśvabhāvanaḥ || 81 ||
iha khalu paramakāruṇikaḥ kavikulatilakaḥ kālidāsanāmā mahākaviraviśeṣeṇa mandamadhyamādisakalabhaktajanoddidhīrṣayā pratīkādyupāsanāmedeṣu dhāma saṃvidvarṇakramatrayopetavakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇaprakāśānandamūrtināmabhistrividhabṛndacakropāsanāyāṃ devatānāntarīyakaṃ "antarbahiḥ pūruṣakālarūpaṃ" iti śrīmadbhāgavate bhagavaduktiprakāreṇāṃtardaharātmanā bahiḥkālarūpeṇa carācarasakalajaganniyāmakaṃ, kaladhātvarthakālaśivaśaktyāḥ svābhinnamahākālīpañcavāhamahārthakramākhyamanuttarasiddhāṃtatattvarūpaṃ, "haṃta! rahasyaṃ bhaṇāmo mā bhramata garbhagoleṣu | atyāsannaṃ hṛdayaṃ paryālocayata tasya codyoyaṃ" iti mahārthamañjarīparimaloktalakṣaṇahṛdayodyogarūpaṃ cidgaganacaṃdrikāprabandhamiṣeṇa kāvyamaryādayā prakaṭayitukāmastatra vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇaparadevatāpīṭhamañcapādeṣu śiṣyaughādimahāparaughāṃteṣu gurvaugheṣu vā prathamamañcapādagurvaughādirūpaṃ "vāsanāmātralābhe pi yo'pramatto na jāyate | tamanityeṣu bhogeṣu yojayanti vināyakāḥ" iti mahārthamañjarīparimaloktiprakāreṇa nirvighnasiddhyaupayikā'pramādahetutayā satsampradāyaparamparāgataprathamaspāṃdakhyayā puryaṣṭhakādhinetṛgaṇeśatattvatayā ca prathamārādhyaṃ, "oṃkāraścāthaśabdaśca dvāvetau brahmaṇaḥ purā | kaṇṭhaṃ bhitvā viniryātau tasmānmāṅgalikāvubhau" ityuktaprakāreṇa brahmagranthivibhedakamahāpraṇavavāgarthaṣaḍadhvaikaśuṇḍaṃ, śaktiputraṃ, mahāgajādhirājānanamādhikārikamaṅgalamūrtimanubandhacatuṣṭayasūcakaṃ svānubhavadidarśayiṣayā sābhiprāyaviśeṣaṇairādau graṃthato nibadhnāti -- 
<1-1>
kṣīrodaṃ paurṇamāsīśaśadhara iva yaḥ prasphurannistaraṃgaṃ
cihyomasphāranādaṃ rucivisaralasadbinduvaktrormimālam |
ādyaspandasvarūpaḥ prathayati sakṛdoṃkāraśuṇḍaḥ kriyādṛk
dantyāsyo'yaṃ haṭhādvaḥ śamayatu duritaṃ śaktijanmā gaṇeśaḥ || 1 ||
kṣīrodamityādi || atrādāveva yadyapi "śubhado mo bhūmimayaḥ" ityukta chaṃdogaṇalakṣaṇānusāreṇa bhūdevatākamagaṇasya maṅgalakaratvena gaṇaśuddhāvapi kaṣayoryogātmakakūṭārṇakṣakāraprayogābhisaṃdhinā yaugikī varṇaśuddhi rna vivakṣiteti dyotitam | tathāpi jaḍajāgraddaśāprakṛtivācakeṣu kādimāvasānakasparśavarṇeṣu kādimāvasānakapratyāharasiddhasya pāśukajīvagatasthūlakāmapuruṣārthavācakatvena taṃ vihāya, śaṣasahānāmūṣmavarṇānāṃ krameṇa citprakṛtitādātmyāpannaśuddhavalidyeśasādāśivaparaśivamithunāvasānakatayā paraśivamithugatāntamukhahṛdayodyogatattvacatuṣṭayasya pañcakasya vā vācakatvena śaktijanmeti gaṇeśaviśeṣaṇāvagataśāktavisargavilāsasya, tadnabhapatitāṇavavisargavilāsasya vā, nididhyāsayiṣitaprathamaspandātmakagaṇeśatattvaśivakumārasya kūṭārmabodhyatvavivakṣāṃ manasi nidhāya jāgraddaśādisparśabodhakasyādyasparśākṣarasya kakārasya, turīyadaśātattvabodhakeṣūṣmavarṇeṣu dvitīyavarṇaparāmarśatyeśavācakasya ṣakārasya ca pratyāhāreṇāntarmukhe hṛdvyomni kaṣaṇakriyāprātipadikavāgarthapratīteḥ tadubhayapiṇḍībhūtakṣakāreṇeśajāgṛtivācakena pravṛttinivṛttyubhayadaśāsāmarasyātmakameva puryaṣṭakādhinetṛgaṇeśatattvamiti sūcayati | taduktaṃ mātṛkācakraviveke turyakhaṇḍe -- "mātmā paśuḥ prakṛtireva tayā nigīrṇo hātmāpi citprakṛtireva nivṛttirūpā | itthaṃ dvidhā prakṛtivikramabhūḥ kṣakāraḥ śambhuḥ paraḥ parata eva bhavetprakṛtyāḥ" `yadvā śivaḥ kṣa iha mapratiyogibhāvātsauṣuptameva paraśambhupadaṃ cidātmā | yaccakramadhyaparatā tadadhīśatā ca śambhoḥ tadasya guṇagandhitayārcyabhāvāt ||' iti ca | nistaraṅgaṃ kṣīrodaṃ paurṇamāsīśaśadhara iva prasphunnādyaspandasvarūpo yo gaṇeśaḥ nistaraṅgaṃ cidvyoma sakṛdeva prathayati | svātmānubhavatya sakṛdvimātarūpatvātsakṛtprathanamitibhāvaḥ | etena puryaṣṭakādhinetṛgaṇeśasya paurṇamāsīśaśadharasāmyavarṇanena candrasyāvināśisādākalāparyatavṛddhikṣayāviva gaṇeśasyāpi puryaṣṭakāvayavapañcadaśakalānāṃ vṛddhikṣayasadbhāvepi candravadeva nāmakalāparyaṃtatayaiva vṛddhikṣayāvityapi sūcitam | yadvā, upasakānāṃ yuṣmākaṃ duritaṃ māyīyabhedaprathārūpaṃ sakṛdekaprayatnena śamayatviti vā sakṛtpadasyānvayaḥ | sphāranādarucivisarādiviśeṣaṇe dehalīdīpanyāyena kṣīrodapakṣepi yojye | kṣīrodamiti | kṣakārameruvarṇaṣaḍadhvarūpamīrayatīti kṣīraṃ daharākāśaḥ | yadvā, kṣaratīndriyavṛttibhya iti vā | tadudayate'treti vā udīyate'smāditi vā kṣīrodamiti cidvyomno viśeṣaṇam | taccādau kṣārodamapi paścātkṣīrodamiti rahasyaṃ | kṣīrodadhiśceti viśeṣyatā ca | ubhayadhāpi hṛdayodyogabodhakaṃ padamityarthaḥ | ādyaspandasvarūpa iti | sṛṣṭikrame ādāvanuttarākārādāvaspandasvarūpo vāgarthayo racalasvarūpaḥ | saṃhārakrame tvādye'kṣapratyāhāre spandasvarūpaśca | akṣaspandasyaiva saṃhārakramārambhatvādidaṃ gaṇeśaviśeṣaṇam | tadārucīnāṃ visareṇa lasanto bindavo vaktreṣu yāsāmūrmimālānāṃ tāṃ ūrmimālā yasmintamiti vigrahaḥ | cidvyomapajñe tu, rucivisaralasadbhindavo vāṅmādhuryarasānusandhānena tadīyaśabdārthālaṅkārādirasānusandhānena vā prakāsāmānatālurasākhyabrahmavindavaḥ śabdoccāraṇakālikāḥ mukheyāsūrmimālāsu vāgvyāpāraparamparāsu tādṛgvākparamparā yasmiṃścidvyogni vimarśamayadaharākāśe taditi vigrahaḥ | yadvā, antaḥ sphāranādamapi bahirvaisvarīrūpeṇa rucivisaralasadbinduvaktrormiṣu mālaṃ vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇāṇavādimalatrayekiñjinmalaśeṣeṇāropitena vā'nubaddhavigrahamiti vā'rthabhedenānvayabhedaḥ |
oṃkāraśuṇḍaḥ, kriyādṛk, dantyāsyaḥ, śaktijanmeti catvāri gaṇeśaviśeṣaṇāni | tatroṃkāravācyaṃ śābdaṃ puruṣatattvameva śuṇḍā yasya sa oṃkāraśuṇḍaḥ | tathā ca śrutiḥ -- `ātmanamaraṇiṃ kṛtvā praṇaṃva cottarāraṇim | dhyānanirmathanābhyāsātpāśaṃ dahati paṇḍitaḥ iti | ata eva kriyādṛk, dantyāsya, śaktijanmeti viśeṣaṇatrayeṇa jñānecchākriyāmayakāryabrahmarūpatāpi sūcitā | tatrāpi kriyādṛgiti bahirmukhakriyāmātravimarśena dantyāsya iti tadīyaphalabubhukṣātmakecchādyotakayauvanāvasthāsaṃpannamukhaviśeṣaṇena, śaktijanmeti kriyāmayaśarīrajanmabhūmidyotakena ca -- "kāryaṃ viṣṇuḥ kriyā brahmā kāraṇaṃ tu maheśvaraḥ" iti brahmavaivartoktatrimūrtimayatvamapi sūcitam | pūrṇopamālaṅkāraḥ | snagdharāvṛttaṃ | tallakṣaṇaṃ tu -- "mnabhrairyānāṃ trayīṇāṃ trimuniyatiyutā sragdharā kīrtiteyam iti || 1 ||
<1-2>
evaṃ sādhakānāṃ māyīyamalanirāsaphalakagaṇeśastutirūpamaṅgalānantaraṃ cikīrṣitacidgaganacandrikāstotraupayikakārmaṇamalanirāsahetuka mākāśaśarīraṃ śivatattvamapi parāmṛśati -- 
sthūlaṃ sūkṣmaṃ paraṃ ca trividhamiha jagadhatprathāveśasiddhyā
yuktaṃ sattā yadīyā sphurati ca parataḥ svaprathaikasvabhāva |
bhāmūrtiṃ yaṃ vimarśakriyamanupatitā lakṣyate lokavṛttiḥ
sanmārgālokanāya vyapanayatu sa vaḥ tāmasīṃ vṛttimīśaḥ || 2 ||
sthūlamiti | sthūlasūkṣmaparanāmakaṃ vyāvahārikaprātibhāsikapāramarthikasattātrayāśrayaṃ, vāgarthanādātmakaṃ tadrūpaṃ vā sthūlaṃ vaikharīrūpaṃ| sūkṣmaṃ madhyamārūpaṃ | paraṃ paśyantīvāsanāmayaṃ ca | trividhamapi jagat | gacchatīti jagaditi vyutpattyā siṃhādigaṇapāṭhasiddhavarṇavyatyāsena varṇāgamena vā trividhajīvaśaktirūpamityarthaḥ | yasya paraśivasya | prathāveśasiddhyā pṛthūbhāvāparaparyāyavyāptisiddhyā yuktaṃ sadeva sphurati | tasmādevanāmamātrabhedasphuraṇahetoḥ, svaprathaikasvabhāvā svīyamukhyavyāptisvabhāvā | yadīyā yatparaśivātmavastusambandhinī sattāca parataḥ bhedeneva vartamānājjagataḥ sakāśācca sphurati | etena svasiddhyavīnasiddhimattvarūpānyonyāśrayo'laṅkaro'smākamabhedavādināṃ na doṣāvaho bedavādināmivetyapi sūcitam | tena ca bhedasṛṣṭiḥ prātibhasikaśābdaśarīramātretyapi vyākhyātaprāyā | tathā vimarśamātrameva kriyā yasyataṃ bhāmūrtiṃ prakāśamātravigrahaṃ | tathāca śrutiḥ -- "na tatra sūrro bhātī" tyādyā | tathāvidhaṃ yamanu anusṛtya | lokavṛttiḥ lokasya vartanamapi | jīvikāpītyarthaḥ | lakṣyate | sa īśaḥ vaḥ sādhakānāṃ yuṣmākaṃ | sanmārgālokanāya -- sanmārgaḥ paramārthasattāsākṣātkāramārgaḥ | mṛgyate mārga iti vā, mṛgyate vastvanena mārga iti vā, krameṇa svarūpaṃ tatsākṣātkaramārgo vā yathāvikāramartho veditavyaḥ | tasyālokanāya sākṣātkārāya | tāmāsīṃ vṛttiṃ śubhāśubhānuṣṭhānakarmaṇamalarūpāṃ,svarūpāvagamāya karmaṇaḥsādhanatvabhrāṃtirūpāṃ vā vyapanayatu | `upāyairna śivo bhāti bhānatyamī tatprasādataḥ | īśvarānugrahādeva puṃsāmadvaitavāsanā||, ityādinā'napāyasthiteranupāyasthitervā'jñānanāśasyeśvarānugrahaphalatvokteriti bhāvaḥ | bhūtakāśapakṣe -- satāṃ nakṣatrāṇāṃ mārgaḥ tasyālokanāya | tāmasī vṛttiṃ tamisrārātrigatāndhatamovyāpāraṃ, meghāvaraṇādirūpāṃ vā sa dikkālākāśamayaśarīraka īśo nāśayatviti | etena tarkikāṇāṃ mate tamaso dravyatvamiva, vedāntināṃ mate mithyā'jñānasyāpi dravyatvaṃ, vastutvaṃ vā nirastamiti veditavyam | avṛtatejasastamastvamivāvṛtajñānarūpasyaiva mithyā'jñānatvavyavahārāt | tamistrārātriṣvākāśaprakāśenaiva nakṣatrasphūrteranu bhūyamānatvācceti bhāvaḥ || 2 ||
<1-3>
evaṃ śivatattvaṃ parāmṛśya śleṣavidhayā, pāṭhabhedena ca svasya sādhakānāṃ ca vidyā'vidyāsvarūpānandasāmrājyasaṃhartṛtayā'sampūrṇaśāmbhavavisargarūpāṇavamalāpakaraṇaṃ prārthayansvasya kṛteśca nāmanī api darśayati -- 
iha kālidāsacandraprasūtirānanandinī stutivyā(rvyā)jāt |
cidgaganacandrikābdheḥ śamayatu saṃsāradāvadavadhuṃ vaḥ (naḥ) || 3 ||
ihetyādinā | ihāsmin cikīrṣitaprabandhe kālidāsanāmā yaścandras‌sa eva prasūtirutpattisthānaṃ yasyāḥ stutessā tathoktā | ānandinī cidgaganacandrikānāma stutiḥ chandaḥpādādiniyamabaddhavāgarthavyāpārarūpā bāhyā vā ābhyantarā vāgarthakṛtiḥ | abdhervyājāt bhrāntasaṃsārābdhimiṣādityarthaḥ | vaḥ naḥ pāṭhakānāṃ yuṣmākaṃ karturmama ca | saṃsāradāvadavadhuṃ saṃsārānalasantāpaṃ kiñcidjñatvāṇavamalaprayuktatāpatrayātmakamityarthaḥ | sphurantamiti śeṣaḥ | śamayatu nāyaṃ saṃsārābdhiḥ kintvānandābdhireveti śāṅkaramārgasvarūpajñānadānenānandayatvityāśayaḥ | stutivyājāditi pāṭhe stutimiṣādityarthaḥ | stutirityapahnavamātraṃ vimṛśyamāne neyaṃ stutiḥ kintvanuttarasvarūpaparatvena cidgaganacandrikaiveti bhāvaḥ | ata evānandinī satyabdheriva saṃsāradāvadavadhuṃ vaḥ yuṣmākaṃ, naḥ asmākaṃ ca kiñcidjñāṇavamalarūpaṃ bāḍabānalatāpaṃ yathā śamayati iyaṃ cidgaganacandrikāpi vakṣyamāṇapadapadārthasamanvayasaṃsāraṃ vardhayitvaiva tadīyadāvadavadhuṃ śamayatvityāśīriti bhāvaḥ | kṛtipakṣe kālīti saṃbuddhiḥ | hekāli ! dāsacandraprasūtiḥ tavāvināśisādākhyapuruṣacandrakalāsvarūpatayāyaṃ paridṛśyamānagaganastha vyāvahārikaścandraḥ tvaccheṣatvena dāsabhūta eva | tatprasūtiścandrikā''nandinī yathā tvaddāsī iyamapi cidgaganacandrikā tathaivānandinī satī śāṅkaramārgapradānena dāsabhūtānāṃ naḥ mama, sāvakānāṃ cāṇavamalātmakasaṃsārānalasantāpaṃ śamayatvityanvayaḥ | taduktaṃ mahārthamañjarīparimale || "duḥkhānyapi sukhāyante viṣamapyamṛtāyate | mokṣāyate ca saṃsāro yatra mārgaḥ sa śāṅkaraḥ" iti || yadvā, ihāsminvedaśastrādividyāprapañce cidgaganacandrikābdheḥ samudratulyacidgaganacandrikākhyaprabandhāt | prabandhasya sṛṣṭyādibhāsāntakṛtyapañcakābhinnedavatarupañcakapañcaratnādyākaratvena samudrasāmyamiti bhāvaḥ | kālidāsacandraprasūtiḥ kālidāsanāmakakavicandraprabhaveti śliṣṭarūpakam | cit kṛtyapañcakābhinnadevatarvādisvarūpayāthātmyacidvṛttiḥ | gaganacandrikevānandinī satīti padāvṛttyā stutivyājāt stutivyāpāramiṣāt | vaḥ yuṣmākaṃ śrotṝṇāṃ, naḥ asmākaṃ paṭhakānāṃ ca saṃsāradāvadavadhuṃ āvidyakasaṃsārānalasantāpaṃ śamayatvitivānvayaḥ | asminpakṣe pāṭhakoktiriti jñeyā | atra pūrvataraślokadvaye va iti pāṭhena, śloketvasmin vaḥ na iti pāṭhabhedena ca sādhakānāṃ yathādhikāramāṇavādimalatritayasya, dvitayasya vā'nuvṛttyā svasya vinaṣṭaprāyāṇavamalamātrānuvṛttyā ca sādhakānāṃ yathādhikaraṃ vijñānākalapralayākalasakalaniṣkalākhyacaturvidhādhikārabhedaḥ, svasya niṣkalopāstyadhikārabhedaśca tena svakṛtau sastrīkacāturvarṇyādhikārikatā niṣādapaṃcamapañcajanādhikārikatā vā svasya mahākālīkālaśivamithunāvatāratā ca sūkṣmekṣikayā sūciteti maṅgalaśkalatritayarahasyam || 3 ||
<1-4>
tathoktacaturvidhasādhakopāsakānprati "hanta! rahasyaṃ bhaṇāmo mā bhramata garbhagoleṣu | atyāsannaṃ hṛdayaṃ paryolacayata tasya codyogam" iti mahārthamañjarīparimaloktaprakāreṇa sādhakahṛdayodyogarūpāṃ svacikīrṣitagranthasandarbhastutiṃ "saritāmivapravāhāstucchāḥ prathamaṃ yathottaraṃ vipulāḥ | ye śāstrasamārambhā bhavanti lokasya te vandyāḥ" ityuktakāvyamīmāṃsanayena prathamaṃ sūtraprāyatayā yathottaravaipulyena ca vartanādyathādhikāraṃ vimarśakaphalapradāṃ ca svavāktripuṭīprameyatṛtīyacidgaganacandrikāṃ turīyacidgaganacandrikāmiva savimarśamupāsituṃ śleṣamaryādayātmānamevā''darśatvena darśayanvidhatte -- 
ci(tva)llocanena satataṃ śivamīkṣitumanvaye ca sadbhāvam |
labdhuṃ ca pūrṇabhāvaṃ yadi cetassamupādhva (dya) mimām || 4 ||
cillocaneneti || cideva locanaṃ netraṃ jñānanetramityarthaḥ | tena satataṃ śivamīkṣitum | tvallocaneneti pāṭhe sādhakasya tava locanenetyarthaḥ | avicchinnatailadhārāvadaparokṣavṛttyā sākṣātkartṛm | anvaye sadbhāvaṃ pūrṇabhāvaṃ ca labdhum | asya granthasandarbhasya saralamārgeṇātmābhinnaparaśivatātparyakapadapadārthasamanvayaṃ, taddvāreṇātmanaḥ pūrṇabhāvaṃ ca prāptumityarthaḥ | yadi cetaḥ sampravṛttamitiśeṣaḥ | tarhīmāṃ stutiṃ samupādhvaṃ samyaktātparyanirṇayena vimṛśadhvam | iti vidhau prārthanāyāṃ vā loṭ | svapakṣe cillocanena jñānanetreṇa | tvallocaneneti pāṭhe yuṣmatpratyayārthatattvajñānameva locanamasmatpratyayārthasvasvarūpatattvāvedakaṃ tṛtīyanetraṃ tenetyarthaḥ | satataṃ śivamīkṣituṃ prakṛtiviviktātmaśivamaparokṣayitum | anu -- aye -- ca -- sadbhāvamiti chedaḥ | tameva yuṣmatpratyayārthalocanamasmatpratyayātmaśivaṃ vā anu -- anusṛtya pūrṇabhāvaṃ labdhuṃ ca | cakārotra vyavahārasadbhāvapūrṇabhāvayorapi samuccāyakaḥ | sadbhāvaṃ aye ayaṃ kālidāsassatpuruṣaḥ pūrṇaśceti sārvajanīnavyāvahārikasattvabhāvamapi prāptavānasmi | yūyamapi tādṛśasadbhāvaṃ prāpnuyetyarthādākṣipyate | jñānanetreṇa śivasākṣātkārāya taddvāreṇa pūrṇabhāvāpattayeca satyapi vidyādhanādau ca tadīyapūrṇabhāve satpuruṣoyamiti vyāvahārikasadbhāvāpattirapi sarveṣāmāvaśyakīti bhāvaḥ | tatsūcitaṃ vidyākarmādisādhanena dvināmasampatsampādanabodhake brāhmaṇopaniṣadādivākyajāte "tasmāddvināmā brāhmaṇorthukaḥ" iti | "sa gurumevāmigacchetsamitpāṇiḥ" tametaṃ vedānuvacanena brāhmaṇā vividiṣanti" ityādike | yadvā vyāvahārikaprātimāsikasattādvayatyāgapūrvakaṃ trikālābādhyacetanarūpohamiti pāramārthikasadbhāvaṃ prāptavānasmi | yūyamapi tathā prāpnutheti vā yathādhikāramathokṣepo boddhavyaḥ | yadi sārvajanīnasadbhāvādyāpattyā pāramārthikasadbhāvāpattyapi vā na pūrṇabhāvāpattisākṣātkāralābhaḥ | tarhimāṃ cidgaganacandrikāmapi svīyacittavilāsarūpatayā samyagupādhvamityāha -- yadīti | yadīmāṃ cidgaganacandrikāṃ -- cet iti | svīyacetovilāsa eva cidgaganacandrikāstutirūpeṇa kavinibaddha ityupādhvam | tarhi tvallocanena śivasākṣātkāraṃ tena pūrṇabhāvaṃ ca prāpsyatheti bhāvaḥ | cetassamupādyamiti pāṭhe śivamīkṣituṃ pūrṇabhāvaṃ ca labdhuṃ yadi pravṛttistarhīmāṃ stutimanucetassamupādyam | stutivimarśanakṣamaṃ cittaṃ sampādanīyamityarthaḥ | yadvā "adabhakṣaṇe" iti dhātoradanīyamalatrayopādhirūpamātmānaṃ imāṃ ca stutiṃ samupa samyagupakurudhvamiti vā | vimarśamayajñānanetreṇa jāte'pi prakṛtiviviktātmasākṣātkāre cidgaganacandrikāvimarśajanyahṛdayaśāntiṃ vinā aupaniṣadapuruṣasvarūpasākṣātkāre cidgaganacandrikāvimarśajanyahṛdayaśāntiṃ vinā aupa niṣadapuruṣasvarūpasākṣātkārālābhena sārvātmyāpatyaparaparyāyapūrṇabhāvāpattistu tatopi dūrataratvānna labhyata iti rahasyārthaḥ || 4 ||
<1-5>
evamanubandhacatuṣṭayātmakaślokaṭatuṣṭayopodghātena malatrayānubaddhamukhyatrividhādhikārasampadamupavarṇya tacchodhakamahāvirāṅgāyatryarthamekadvitricatuḥpañcapācchaktirūpaṃ "pṛthivī pādaḥ | antarikṣaṃ pādaḥ | dyauḥ pādaḥ | diśaḥ pādaḥ | parorajāḥ pādaḥ virājyeva pratitiṣṭhati" iti taittirīyaśrutyuktavirāṭpratiṣṭhāphalaprāpakaṃ, pañcārthakramāparaparyāyamahākālīpañcavāhakramatayā paramasthūlarūpeṇa pariṇatastrīkacāturvarṇyarūpaṃ, niṣādapañcamapaṃcajanamānavajīvaśaktīnāṃ trivargaścaturvargapaṃcavargātmake trividhamahāpuruṣārthakrame prathamacaturthapaṃcamapuruṣārthasāmarasyātmakāmuṣmikabuddhimaihikatvadṛṣṭyā bhāvavikāreṣu dvitīyatṛtīyayoriva dvitīyatṛtīyārthakāmapuruṣārthayugalasyāmuṣmikatvadṛṣṭyā ca viparivartanamapyupajīvyopāstikrame puruṣārthapaṃcake paṃcapādvibhūtivistāreṇa ṣaṣṭhādinavāntasāṅkhyakramepi ṣaṭsaptasaṅkhyayorantarbahissāmarasyopāstibhedena ṣaḍṛtuṣaṇmukhasaptamanu grahadvīpasamudrāditattadvāsanānukūlakalpitabhrāmaryādiśāktasampadrūpāṇāṃ tathā trivargacatuḥpaṃcavargavyaṣṭisamaṣṭibhedopāstitaratamabhāvena saptāṣṭamātṛkānavananādhanavayonikālaśaivacakrādirūpāṇāṃ ca nādasaṅkhyānāṃ śaivaśāktabhāgayorjñānakriyayornāmarūpāṃśayośca jāgradāditurīyātītāntamānavāvasthā'paraparyāyapaṃcabrahmakṛtyapaṃcakena saha sṛṣṭisthitisaṃhārānākhyābhāsākhyakālīpaṃcakābhedasamanvayaṃ sarvatomukhena vitatya vyācikīrṣuḥ pūrvoktapṛthivyādiparorajontagāyatrīpādeṣu yathākramaṃ ahaṅkāraḥ prāṇa manaḥ buddhi indriyaiḥ pratīcīnagatyā niyamyatve sarvadā śrutigocarātmasampadabhivṛddhyā "bṛhi bṛṃhi vṛddhau" ityevaṃ śabdaniruktātmasampadbṛṃhaṇenānvarthakaṃ brahmatvam | kṣatāttraṇanātmakaṃ kṣatratvam | dharmadharmiveśasthityā''tmānātmasampadveśasthityā vā vaiśyatvam, cetyevaṃrūpatraivarṇikāparaparyāye vaidikasaccidānandātmakajñānakriyāmayatrayīmukhyaśābdaśarīratve "rogaiśca kṣayito dehaḥ kalpaityabhidhīyate| tatsūcanena tasyāpi kalpasūtratvamiṣyate" ityādibhaviṣyottarapurāṇaniruktyā bhautikābhautikaśabdārthasāmarasyena trayīmayamukhyavedaśarīrakakālīkālaparaśivamithunakalparūpatvaṃ, vaidikaṣaḍadhvāparaparyāyaṣaḍaṅgaśabdaśarīratve, śīkṣāvyākaraṇachandorūpeṇa śābdādhvatrikapradhānaṃ, vedāṅgaśābdajñānaśarīrakaṃ, kālakālīmithunajñānatripuṭīrūpaṃ, niruktajyotiḥ kalpātmakārthādhvatrikapradhānaṃ, kalātattvabhuvanarūpaṃ, kriyātripuṭīmayaṃ, mukhyavedāṅgaśarīrameveti, mukhyaṃ, trividhaṃ vedavedāṅgatraivarṇikanavacakrātmakaṃ, śrīcakraṃ mukhyavedādhikārikaṃ siddhacakraṃ | anadhikārikantu vyutkrameṇa pūrvoktapaṃcasaṅkhyākavirāṅgāyatrīpādairahaṅkārādīndriyāntātmāvayaveṣu parācīnagatyā niyamyatve viraḍṝpakāryabrahmamātrasthityā, satyapi "brāhmaṇo bṛhaspatisavena yajeta, rājā rājasūyena, vaiśyo vaiśyastomena, varṣāsu rathakārognīnādadhīta, etayā niṣādasthapatiṃ yājayet" ityādiśrutyuktavaidikakriyāmātrādhikāre vedatātparyāviṣayakatvenāvidyakavaikharīkriyāmātraparatvāt "strīśūdradvijabandhūnāṃ trayī na śrutigocarā | iti bhārata mākhyānaṃ kṛpayā muninā kṛtam" ityuktanāmamātradhāritraivarṇikatvarūpaṃsādhakacakram | tāvatsampanmaryādāyā apyabhāve tattatsaṅkīrṇavṛttyupalakṣitapaṃcajanatādirūpaṃ, vakṣyamāṇaśiṣyaughalakṣaṇānugatyā sādhyaśrīcakrarūpaṃ, jāgataṃ bṛndacakrapīṭhaṃceti, trivṛttriṇamahāvirāṭsampadaṃ, pūrvoktasvarāṅgāyatrīsampatsāmarasyena "daśa hastyā aṃgulayo daśa padyā ātmaikaviṃgśaḥ | dvādaśamāsāḥ paṃcartavastraya ime lokā asāvādityaekaviṃg‌śaḥ" iti śrutyuktakālapuruṣasampatsāmarasyābhedena bhāratapaṃcamavedasamanvayamārgeṇa ca krīm, krūm, khphrem, hsauḥ0 , shauḥ0, hraskhphrem, ityādikālīprāsādaparāprāsādādipañcasaptanavādisaṅkyākabījapiṇḍamantrādibhiḥ pañcāmnāyasārabhūtamahākālīkālamithunacijjaḍamayanādanavakavibhūtisāmarasyameva sakalāgamatantraśāstraprameyamiti cidgaganacandrikāstutyā'vyākṛtapīṭhotpattyādikālyupāsanākramarahasyātmakaṃ, vaidikāvaidikasādhakahṛdayodyogarūpātmadṛgunmeṣamanujighṛkṣurādau svasya dṛkkriyāmayaśarīratāṃ, mahākālīkālamithunātmakārthanārīśarīrasya tadatītapadatvaṃ copadarśayan "dvā suparṇā sayujā sakhāyā samānaṃ vṛkṣaṃ pariṣasvajāte | tayoranyaḥ pippalaṃ svādvattyanaśnannanyo'bhicākaśīti" iti mantroktaviruddhasakhyahāniphalalābhāya mahānādamayapūrṇāhantayā hṛdguhābhilāṣavarmanapūrvakaṃ, svasya tadanupraveśena pramātṛpūrṇabhāvaṃ ca darśayan "strīśūdradvijabandhūnāṃ trayī na śrutigocarā" ityuktastrīśūdrāditrayyāmanugraheṇa sakalatantrāgamapurāṇetihāsādiprameyabhūtamahākuladharmarūpaṃ, bhārataṃ svakuladharme, mahāvirāṅgāyatryarthatvenaivābhinīyāpi darśayantaṃ, mahākaulikamarthanārīśvaraṃ śabdārthobhayaśarīreṇa svayamapyanuviśati -- 
hṛdguhāmabhilaṣannahantayā
dṛkkriyāvapuradṛkkriyāpadam |
santame(no)mi samabhāvadaṃ (ginaṃ) śivaṃ
saṃkhyahāniramunā hi sūyate || 5 ||
hṛdguhāmityādinā | ahantayā mamatāspadagauṇāhantāṃ, prādeśikātmamithyāhantāṃ ca vihāya mukhyayā | mātṛkāpratyāhāreṇāhaṃśabdanādatripuṭīvṛttirūpayā, "trivṛdvai jyaiṣṭhyaṃ | mātā pitā putraḥ" ityādiśrutyuktasarvajyeṣṭhapitrahamartharūpayā, uktaśabdārthasāmarasyātmakānāhatanādapūrṇabhāvarūpayā cetyevaṃ trividhayāpyahantayetyarthaḥ | hṛdguhāṃ | "guhūsaṃvaraṇe" iti dhātoḥ saṃvṛtasamaṣṭiśabdārthahṛdayaguhāmityarthaḥ | abhilaṣan ābhimukhyena śabdārthahṛdayāguhāmicchan | dṛkkriyāvapuḥ | dṛkkriye upyete'sminniti, aneneti vā vyutpatyā vahirmukhāvasthāyāṃ vṛttimayajñānakriyāśarīroham | adṛkkriyāpadaṃ vṛttimayajñānakriyābhyāmapadyarūpam | kartṛkarmakriyāmayatripuṭīrahitamityarthaḥ | anuttarākāraviṣayakadṛkkriyābhyāṃ padyarūpaṃ ca | vāgarthaśarīratvādityapi dhvanyate | ataeva nādabindukalāmayamahānādarūpaṃ cetyanuraṇanamaryādayā | santaṃ pāramārthikasadrūpam | yadvā, ahaṃ hṛdguhāmabhilaṣan | tayā hṛdguhayaiva | dṛkkriyāvapuḥ, adṛkkriyāpadaṃ santamiti vā | ataeva samabhāvadam | śivaṃ maṅgalarūpam | "brahma vedabrahmaiva bhavati | brahmaiva sanbrahmāpyeti | brahma tanmaṅgalaṃ param" ityādiśruteḥ | "vaś‌ kāntau" iti dhātoricchārūpaṃ cetyapyarthaḥ | yathādhikāraṃ, yathecchaṃ ca sārūpyādipradātāramiti bhāvaḥ | santaṃ śivamityanyataraviśeṣaṇavivakṣayā sādākhyapuruṣacandrakalārūpaṃ śivaṃ cetivā'rthaḥ | emi prāpnomi | anuviśāmītyarthaḥ | yadvā, samīcīno'bhāvaḥ samabhāvaḥ | adhikaraṇātmako bhāvāntararūpovā | taṃ dadātīti samabhāvadam | tārkikābhyupagatasaptamapadārtharūpābhāvasyāsamīcīnatvadyotanārthamatratyābhāvasya samityupapasargaviśeṣaṇasārthakyamunneyam | taduktaṃ vārtike -- "bhāvāntaramabhāvastu kayācittu vyapekṣayā" iti | bhaṭṭapādairapyuktam -- "adhikaraṇātmako'bhāvaḥ" iti | santanomi samabhāvadaṃginamiti pāṭhedṛkkriyābhyāmapadyarūpamapi samānasphuraṇavantam | ataevāṅginamātmānaṃ samyaktanomi vistārayāmi | vitatya vidhimukhabodhāya sṛṣṭyāditaṭasthalakṣaṇadvāreṇa varṇayāmītyarthaḥ |
nanvīdṛśātmavistṛtiśivabhāvāpattirūpānupraveśasya kiṃ phalamityāśaṅkya viruddhasakhyahāniṃ phalatvena darśayati -- sakhyahāniriti | hi yasmāt | amunā śivena sakhyahāniḥ dvāsuparṇeti śrutyukta viruddhasakhyasya hāniḥ | prasūyate dīyata ityarthaḥ | dvayorahamamoraikyaṃ labhyata iti bhāvaḥ | atastaṃ emīti phalopanyāsaḥ | tenādvitīyātmābhedasākṣātkārāvasthāyāṃ, tādṛśāhaṃgrahopāsanāvasthāyāṃ ca, svasvarūpatayā, viśiṣṭādvaitabhedāvasthāyāṃ śeṣatayā, sevyasevakabhāvarūpadvaitapratīkopāsanāvasthāyāṃ sevakatayā ca, āvidyakasakhyahānessamatvānmatatrayepi sakhyahāniphalaṃ samamiti veditavyam |
yadvā `han hiṃsāgatyoḥ' iti dhātupāṭhātsakhyahānirvidyāprayuktasakhyagatirdīyata iti vā, upāsakapakṣe -- vidyārthināṃ vaiṣayikāṇāṃ yā mate samanvayo veditavyaḥ | tena prakṛte sa eva śivaḥ kṛtikartā nāhamityāśayaḥ | taduktaṃ mantrarāmāyaṇakāraiḥ -- "viśevotpattivipattibhūssa bhagavānatra pravaktā śivastasyāhaṃ pratiśabdaparvataguhā yanme na garvagrahaḥ | taddoṣostu guṇostu vā na khalu tatsvāyattamitthaṃ sthite mātsarye mahatā mudetu yadi vā vātsalyamujjṛmbhatām ||" iti | atra sarvatra radhoddhātāvṛttam | lakṣaṇantu -- "ro narāviha rathoddhatā lagau" iti | evaṃ vṛttaracanāmiṣeṇāpi "ātmānaṃ rathinaṃ viddhi śarīraṃ rathameva ca" ityuktapratyakśivavirāḍdehasya cidgaganacandrikākhyaprabandhātmanā uddhatarathavṛttasya vāgarthavilāsamayatvapratyabhijñājñāpanena ca svaśarīratādātmyaṃ sūcitamiti saṃkṣepaḥ || 5 ||
<1-6>
evamātmanaḥ paraśivatattvānupraveśena pūrṇabhāvabharitasya paraśivasya prakāśavimarśobhayātmakatvāparaparyāyavāgarthādyātmakārthanārīśvararūpatāṃ sūcayan, "namo vāce yā coditā yā cānuditā tasyai vāce...pitaronumadantu" iti śukrīyakāṇḍaśāntimantroktasvasvarūpavāca uditānuditadaśābhedenāvidkalokavyavahārasiddhavaikharīparāvāgarthadṛṣṭibhedaṃ ca viparyāsayan, uditānuditavāgarthābhinnavidyāmayaparāvaikharīvāgarthabhāvena vakṣyamāṇadvitricatuḥpañcārthānuttarapañcavāhamahārthakramopayogitayā vāgarthamayaṣaḍadhvasuṣṭhyādihetutā pratijānīte -- 
yāhamityuditavākparā ca sā
yaḥ prakāśalulitātmavigrahaḥ |
yau mithassamuditāvihonmukhau
tau ṣaḍadhvapitarau śraye śivau || 6 ||
yā'hamityādinā | yā vāk | ahamiti | mātṛkārṇapratyāhāravimarśenābhyantarakriyāśaktimayo'haṃ śabda iti | uditavāk | uditaṃ sajjātodayamātmānaṃ vaktīti uditavāk | bhavati, saiva pareti sambandhaḥ | tadvat, yaḥ ahamarthopi | prakāśalulitātmavigrahaḥ | prakāśena śabdābhinnārthasphuraṇena | ahaṃśabdaprayoktrabhinneneti yāvat | taduktamupadeśasāhasrayāṃ śaṅkarabhagavatpūjyapādaiḥ -- "sambhāvyo gocare śabdaḥ pratyayo vā na cānyadhā | na sambhāvyau tadātmatvādahaṃkartṛstathaiva ca || " iti | lulitaḥ śabdānupraviṣṭaḥ ātmavigrahaḥ svasvarūpaviśeṣajñānaṃ yasya saḥ | sopi paraeveti liṅgapariṇāmovā śeṣo vā | yattadornityasambandhāt, parācetyatra cakārārthasamuccayasvārasyācca " ||
nanu tahuryabhayorapi paratve dvaitāpattessvasiddhāntavyāhatiḥ prasajeyetetyāśaṅkya vakṣyamāṇavāgarthasṛṣṭyādiṣaḍadhvakṛtyapañcakakramopayogitayā "parasparatapassampatphalāyitaparasparau | prapañcamātāpitarau prāñcau jāyāpatī stumaḥ|| " ityuktaparasparānupraviṣṭavāgarthayugalātmakatvadyotanāya gajavṛṣamacitranyāyena bhedanirdeśavarṇanaṃ vācoyuktimātramiti pariharati -- yau mitha iti | yau vāgartharūpau mithassamuditau uktaprakāreṇa parasparānupraviṣṭasvarūpau santāveva | iha pūrvaślokoktāvidyakasakhyahānirūpavilāsārthabhuvanasṛṣṭau | unmukhau āvidyakavāgarthayorliṅādivāgarthavidhiniṣedhavyāpāropayujyamānaliṅgādikalpanākramatyāgena "na padmāṅkā na vajrāṅkā yataḥ prajā | liṅgāṅkā ca bhāgāṅkā ca tasmānmāheśvarī prajā ||" ityuktasahajāvimṛśyavāgarthaliṅgādisphuraṇapāṇḍityena cordhvamukhau | ataeva ṣaḍadhvapitarau | varṇapadamantramayavimṛśyaśābdādhvatrayātmanā, mātā | `māṅ māne' iti dhātoḥ | mātyaparicchinnaṃ svarūpaṃ paricchinattīti mātā | svasvarūpaparicchetrī, svarūpapramātā śivaśca | kalātattvabhuvanamayaprakāśanīyārthagatā'rthādhvatrayātmanāpāti pālayati svarūpayāthātmyamiti pitā | svarūpapālakaśśivaḥ pālayatrī śaktiśca | evaṃ mātā ca, pitā ca pitarau | ṣaḍadhvapitarau | śivau | śivā ca śivasca śivau | `vaś kāntau' iti dhātoḥ parasparavācyavācakānukūlecchāsvarūpau | nāmāṃsaparicchedapālanābhyāṃ rūpāṃśaparipālanaparicchedābhyāṃ ca sakalajagatprasūtirakṣādidakṣau kālīkālau śraye | svasvarūpābhedenāhaṃ kalaye ityarthaḥ | parākpratīcoḥ kālīkālayordigdeśakālādhvaṣaṭkamevamuktaṃ saubhāgyahṛdayāmarśastave -- "varṇaḥ kalāpadaṃ tattvaṃ mantro bhuvanameva ca | ityadhvaṣaṭkaṃ devaśi ! bhāti tvayi cidātmani ||" iti | etena tattvamasīti mahāvākyagataśabdārthaparokṣāparokṣānubhūtāvapi yathā vāgarthasṛṣṭau varṇaśabdārthabhūtabrāhmaṇādicāturvarṇyamūrtimūlaparijñānamātramevāvidyakaprameyatvaṃpadārthajñānavatkalāmātrameva bhavati, tādṛgvarṇādikalpanāhetukasvasvavarṇadharmaparijñānaṃ padyate svarūpatattvamaneneti vyutpattyā parokṣavartitatpadārthajñānavat padam, tādṛśaparokṣatatpadārthabhūtasvarūpātmakasakalavedaśāstrāditātparyārthaphalajñānaṃ yathā svarūpābhinnaṃ; tathaivaitacchāstramaryādayāpi nādabindukalāmayākṣarārthajñānāparaparyāyakalājñānadvāreṇaivākṣarasamudāyātmakapadārtha tatvajñānaṃ, tādṛkpadarthatattvajñānadvāreṇaiva padasamudāyātmakavākyārthāparaparyāyamantrārthabhūtabhuvanasvarūpatādātmyajñānamapīti veditavyam | uktaṃ ca kārikāvalyām -- "padajñānaṃ tu karaṇaṃ dvāraṃ tatra padārthadhīḥ | śābdabodhaḥ phalaṃ tatra śaktidhīḥ sahakāriṇī || " iti | etena "śabdāvarṇātmakā nityāśca | mantrāṇāmananyādṛśaṃ sāmarthyam" ityādiparaśurāmakalpasūtreṣu traipurasiddhāntoktasūtrārthastātparyeṇānūdita ityapi veditavyam |
nanu kimidaṃ viruddhamucyate, yatotra śabdabuddhikarmaṇāṃ dvikṣaṇāvasthāyitayā tṛtīyakṣaṇanāśaḥ tārkikairabhyupagamyate | bhagavatā paraśurāmeṇa tu varṇātmakānāṃ śabdānāṃ nityatā ceti kathamupapadyata iti cediha tadubhayābhyupagatanityānityatvavirodhābhāsayorvakṣyamāṇasṛṣṭyādilakṣaṇakṛtyatraye, pañcake vā tattanmātṛkākṣarārṇavakalpāvasānakakṣaṇalakṣitatvena sāpekṣatvādavirodha iti brūmaḥ | taduktaṃ vedāntapañcadaśyāṃ śrīvidyāraṇyagurusārvabhaumaiḥ -- "upādāne vinaṣṭepi kṣaṇaṃ kāryaṃ pratīkṣate | ityāhustārkikāḥ tadvadasmākaṃ kiṃ na sambhavet | tantūnāṃ dinasaṅkhyānāṃ taistādṛkkṣaṇa īritaḥ | bhramasyāsaṅkhyakalpasya yogyaḥ kṣaṇa iheṣyatām" ityādinā || 6 ||
<1-7>
evaṃ pramātṛvāgarthamayātmanaḥ pramāṇapade "prameyasya svarūpatvaprakāśohi pramā tṛtā | pramāṇanāṃ vimarśaścetyarthanārīśatā''tmanaḥ || " ityuktapramātṛpramāṇasāmarasyāparaparyāyaprakāśavimarśamayatāṃ vibhāvya, dhūmārcirmārgādhikāriprameyavimarśakasya jīvasādhakarudrasyāpi tadavāptaye tasya prameyavāgarthābhinnarūpatvena "dravyayajñāḥ tapoyajñāḥ" ityādinā bhagavadgītoktasaptavidhayajñeṣu dravyayajñasyaivā'hantāvidyā'vidyayoḥ svarūpepi vā kṛtyatrayaśirorūpeṇa prathamasādhanatāṃ dyotayan, vaidikasomayāgaviśeṣeṣu -- "rudro vā eṣa yadagniḥ" iti śrutyuktoṣṇavīryaśirassandhānātmakapravargyādeḥ "aharahaḥ pravṛjyate | arthamāse'rthamāse pravṛjyate" ityādi śrutyuktattatsthānāpannāgnihotradarśapūrṇamāsādeśca yatā nityatvena prāmukhyaṃ, tathā tāntrikayāgaviśeṣeṣvapi śītavīryarudraśirassandhānātmakapañcapadārthakaraṇakapūjāviśeṣasya prāmukhyamabhidhotayituṃ prameyajīvarudrasya mahāmanthānabhairavasya pramāṇapadasthapāṃcabhautikadehendriyādisaṅghātaṃ jīvayātrāmayasṛṣṭhyādipañcakṛtyasamudramathanopayogimanthadaṇḍatayā pramātrabhedena stuvannamaskaroti -- 
śaktinetrapariṇaddha (dva) māsthitaṃ
pūrṇabhāmaya (vama) payodhimantha(thna)tām |
devi ! tarpaṇasudhākalāśrayaṃ
rudramandaramahaṃ sphuṭaṃ bhaje || 7 ||
4śaktinetretyādinā | hedevi! prakāśavimarśātmike | śaktinetrapariṇaddhaṃ | vidyākṛtyapaṃcakapakṣe -- śaktireva netraṃ locanaṃ tena pariṇaddham | āvidyakakṛtyatrayapakṣe -- śaktinetā śakteḥ nāyaka ātmātenā'pariṇaddhaṃ | svarūpapakṣe -- śaktireva netraḥ mantharajjuḥ tena pariṇaddhaṃ | pūrṇabhāmayapayodhimanthatāṃ | pūrṇabhāmayaḥ pūrṇaprakāraśamayaḥ | yaḥ payodhistasminmanthatām | mandaratāṃ manthadaṇḍatāṃ ca | āsthitam | śaktinetrapariṇadvamāsthitamiti pāṭhe -- śaktinetraṃ netrasadṛśī śaktiḥ pariṇīyate'neneti śaktinetrapariṇaditi kvippratyayāntavivakṣāyāṃ netrasadṛśaśaktipariṇetā daharākāśaḥ | taṃ vamati prasārayatīti śaktinetrapariṇadvamaḥ | sarvaśaktiprasārakapāṃcabhautikadehendriyasaṃghātaḥ | tamāsthitaṃ śaktinetrapariṇadvamāsthitam | sakalaśaktisametadehendriyādiyuktamityarthaḥ | pūrṇabhāvamapayodhimanthatāmiti pāṭhe -- apayodhimanthatām | viditaveditavyatayā'kāramātrasvarasamudram | "a ityaṣṭādaśānāṃ saṃjñā" iti śabdānuśāsanamaryādayā'kārātmakamūlādhārasthasvaramātṛkākuṇḍalinīmātravimarśena manthānabhairavarūpāṇāṃ siddhānām | sṛṣṭhisamudramanthane pravṛttirahitānāṃ vā | ataeva taittirīyabrāhmaṇe dvitīyakāṇḍasthaturīyapraśne ṣaṣṭhānuvāke śrutirapi śrautamantraliṅgena vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇanādavāgarthaṣaḍadhvalakṣite prakāśānandaśaktinetrapariṇaddhavigrahe ca vaiśvānaramūrticakre pūrvadevānāmeva gūḍhaliṅgacāranyāyena manthānabhairavarūpatāmāha -- śru || "na hi spaśamavidannanyamasmāt | vaiśvānarātpura etāramagneḥ | athemamanthannamṛtamamūrāḥ | vaiśvānaraṃ kṣetrajityāya devāḥ" ityādinā | pūrṇabhāvamāsthitaṃ vā | pūrṇo bhāvo yasya taṃ pūrṇabhāvaṃ vetyarthaḥ | yadvā pūrṇabhāṃ vamatīti pūrṇabhāvamaḥ | sacāsau payodhiḥ | taṃ madhnantīti pūrṇabhāvamapayodhimanthānaḥ | teṣāṃ madhye śaktinetrapariṇaddhamitivā'nvayaḥ | etenāgnīṣomātmakajagatsṛṣṭāvagneḥ śaktyātmakatvena somasya śaktimaddravyarūpatvena ca śaktimaddravyasyaiva manthadaṇḍatvoktyā'śaktimaddravyāṇāmakiṃcitkaratvaṃ kṛtyapaṃcakakriyāyajñeṣu | tena "haviryajamānayoḥ saumyayorna pratinidhiḥ" iti mīmāṃsānyāyasyānugṛhītatatvaṃ cārthādāpatitamiti veditavyam | tathā devi ! tarpaṇasudhākalāśrayaṃ devī cicchaktiḥ tṛpyatyanayeti devitarpaṇā | saṃjñāyāṃ puṃvadbhāvaḥ | sā ca sā sudhā amṛtātmikā jīvaśaktiḥ | saiva kalā vidyā | tadāśrayaṃ | yadvā, devī tṛpyatyaneneti devitarpaṇaḥ | sa cāsau sudhākalaḥ candraḥ | cicchaktyabhinnabrāhmīlakṣmyāḥ sudhāyā api vāruṇyā indusahodarītvāditi bhāvaḥ | tadāśrayaḥ sa āśrayo yasyeti vā devitarpaṇasudhākalāśrayaḥ | tamasaṃstutadvijarājalakṣmīkamapi | dvisaṃjñayāpi dvijarājaśabditamityarthaḥ | yadvā, devīti sambuddhiḥ | tarpaṇasudhākalāśrayam | hedevi! tava tarpaṇārthā yā sudhā tasyāṃ yāḥ kalā ardhyadravyasaṃskārārthāḥ | amṛtādipūrṇāmṛtāntāṣṣoḍaśa somakalāḥ tantroktāḥ | caturnavatikalābhimantraṇapakṣe -- agnisūryasomakalā aṣṭatriṃśat, brahmaviṣṇurudrāṇāṃ daśa daśa, īśvarasya pītā śvetā aruṇā asitā ceti catasraḥ, sadāśivasya nivṛttyādivyomarūpāntāṣṣoḍaśa kalāḥ haṃsaśśuciṣadityādyāśca pañca dravyābhimantraṇārthāḥ, mūlamanumūrtikalayā saha caturnavatirityādi ca | evaṃvidhāḥ kalā āśrayo yasya taṃ, kalānāmāśrayaṃ vā | rudramandaram | sphuṭaṃ sphoṭarūpeṇa carmacakṣurvedyadravyarūpeṇa ca pratyakṣam | paṭukaraṇendrirūpaṃ ca | taduktaṃ rudraprapāṭhakepi -- "namo rudrebhyo ye pṛthivyām" ityādinānnavātavarṣarūpāṇāṃ sthūladravyāṇāmeva yathākramaṃ pṛthivyādilokatrayasthitarudragaṇānāmāyudhatvoktyā rudragaṇānāmapi nityapratyakṣarūpatvaṃ paṭukaraṇendriyatvaṃ ca | evaṃvidhaṃ rudramandaramahaṃ bhaje | pañcapadārthakaraṇakadravyamayayajñasya paṃcakarmajñānendriyakaraṇakavaitānikasomayāgasyeva vicchinnarudraśirassandhānātmakatvabhāvanayā pravargyādikarmavatpañcakṛtyātmikāyā mahāsthitestatrāpi sākṣātpradhānāṅgatvaṃ nirantaramanusandadhāmīti
