bhāvaḥ | taduktamabhīṣṭakāpradhānāruṇaketukāgnibrahmavidyāprakaraṇāruṇaprapāṭhakepi -- "divyasyaikā dhanurārtniḥ | pṛthivyāmaparāśritā | tasyendro vamrirūpeṇa | dhanurjyāmacchinatsvayaṃ | tadindradhanurityajyam | abhravarṇeṣu cakṣate | etadeva śaṃyorbārhaspatyasya | etat rudrasya dhanuḥ | rudrasyatveva dhanurārtniḥ | śira utpipeṣa | sa pravargyau'bavat | tasmādyassa pravargyeṇa yajñena yajate | rudrasya sa śiraḥ pratidadhāti | nainagaṃ rudra āruko bhavati | ya evaṃ veda" iti | pañcapadārthakaraṇakapūjāmayadravyayajñasya pravargyāruṇaketukasomayāgādiṣu cityagnyādisāmarasyaprakārastu gurumukhādevāvagantavyaḥ | granthavistarabhayānneha pratanyate || 7 ||
<1-8>
evaṃ ślokatrayeṇa svātmanaḥ pramātrāditripuṭīrūpatayā sārvātmyasamunmīlanamukhena dravyamayayajñasyāpi prathamasādhanatāmanusandhāya tatra "patanti karmaṇā yena jantavo narake dhṛvam| utpatanti ca tenaiva trikālajñā bhavanti ca ||" ityādikālikāmatoktarītyā yāni dravyavākkarmādīni pātakāni tānyevābhisandhibhedena mocakatvenāpi śrutānīti bhāvavākkriyādravyamayasādhakābhisandhitraiguṇyena tāmasasyādhaḥ patanamapi pratyabhijñāpayan sārvātmyaṃ samunmīlayati -- 
yo'bhavadbhavati yastathetaro
yo bhaviṣyati pṛtha[kkriyo] kkramo janaḥ |
tattrayodaramahanta(yai) thaikayā
saṃtamasmi hṛdi randhramānataḥ || 8 ||
yobhavadityādinā | yaḥ sādhakaḥ, manthānabhairavarudraśca | pṛthakkriyaḥ pṛthagbhūtāḥ lokavilakṣaṇāḥ kriyā yasya sa tathoktaḥ | ataevābhavat | prāgavidyāvasthāyāṃ pūrvasminvā janmani nādānuttarākārātmanāsattāhīna ivābhavadityarthaḥ | pramātṛrudrapakṣe -- "asadeva somyedamagra āsīt" iti śrutyā'nabhivyaktasattākanāmarūpādimānapi sattāvānevābhavaditi | tathaiva bhavati | bhavatīti laṭā jagadrūpeṇa vartanāditi bhāvaḥ | yaśca tathaivetaropi | atra itaraśabdamahimnā pravāhanitye jananamaraṇasaṃsāre nābhavannabhavaticetyapi dhvanyate | sādhakapakṣe -- "pūrvasminniva vartamānepi janmani lokavilakṣaṇakriyatvena jñānakriyādipuruṣārthavāsanāhainyena vā saṅkhyāpūrakasaṃjñāmātrasattāvānapi na bhavatīti dhvanyarthaḥ | rudrapakṣepi -- "asanneva sa bhavati | asadbrahmeti veda cet | asti brahmeti cedveda | santamenaṃ tato viduriti" iti śrutininditātmaśūnyatābhayamapīdṛśādhikāriṇo'navabhāsayannabhūtabhautikavastuni bhānāyogānna bhāsata eveti bhāvaḥ | etadapyuktaṃ gauḍapādakārikāsvalātaśāntiprakaraṇe ātmamānasya catuṣkoṭyavabhāsanam "asmi nāstyasti nāstīti nāsti nāstīti vā punaḥ catasraḥ koṭaya" iti | atha yaśca pṛthakkriyaḥ | kāścanā''stikya uttamajanipradāḥ kāścana nāstikya adhamajanipradāśca kriyāḥ puṇyapāpavyavahāreṇa pṛthagbhūtāḥ kriyā yasya saḥ pṛthakkriyaḥ | pṛthakkrama iti pāṭhe -- pūrvoktottamādhamajanāpekṣayā pṛthagbhūtasaṃsārakrama ityarthaḥ | tiryagūrdhvādhassrotastrayātmakasyā'hamo nādavāgarthaṣaḍadhvakṛtyatrayātmakatvāditi bhāvaḥ | etena uttamaśivasaṃsārāpekṣayā adhamapaśusaṃsārāpekṣayā ca tṛtīyaprakārakasādhakajīvasaṃsārassūcitaḥ | ataeva janaḥ bhāvinyapi janmani saṃśayātmatvājjanimānevetyarthaḥ | bhaviṣyati | rudrapakṣe ajana ityapi padacchedaḥ bhavitumarhati | yataḥ kālatrayepi lokaśāstravilakṣaṇakriyo'to janmatrayepi janmasambandharahita eva bhaviṣyatītyartha śca | yadvā, yaḥ sādhakaḥ purā lokavilakṣaṇakriyo'bhavat, idānīṃ bhavati, agre bhaviṣyati ceti vānvayaḥ | tattrayodaraṃ teṣāṃ trayāṇāmudarabhūtam | ekayā'hantayā | ekatra, guṇatrayā'hantayā anyatra, pūrṇasattārūpayā ca | hṛdi randhraṃ santamānatosmi | ahaṃ tathaikayeti pāṭhe -- yathā trayāṇāmudarabhūtastathaivaikayā'hantayā hṛdi randhra santamahamānatosmīti vā, santaṃ prati randhrapramāṇataḥ hṛdyasmīti vā'rthaḥ || 8 ||
<1-9>
evaṃ svātmaśivasyaiva trividhasādhakābhedaṃ pratyabhijñāpya sadvidyāsampradāyamārgapravartanāya, tasyaiva guruparamparārūpabhedena svīkṛtānekavigrahatyadyotanāya ca, śābdayogavaidyanāmakaprasthānabhedena vāṅmanaśśārīramalatrayanivartakagurvaughatrayagurupaṃktisamaṣṭimūlabhūtaṃ "yogena cittasya padena vācāṃ malaṃ śarīrasya ca vaidyakena | yo'pākarottaṃ pravaraṃ munīnāṃ patañjaliṃ prāṃjalirānatosmi" ityuktaśeṣaśivavatārapataṃjalirūpeṇa śabdabrahmavatāratvaṃ ca dyotayan paraughaprabhṛtiśiṣyaughaparyantāṃ gurupaṃktiṃ namaskaroti -- 
viśvasadmani sadātmanīśvare
pūraṇātpuruṣatāmupeyu(ṣe) ṣi |
ye manaḥ praṇidadhurmaharṣayaḥ
tān pataṃjalimukhānupāsmahe || 9 ||
viśvasadmanītyādinā | viśvameva sadma yasya tasmin | viśvasya samanīti vā | ubhayadhāpi sadātmani | parāparavāgarthayorupādhyupahitarūpeṇa kālatrayābādhyarūpatvāditi bhāvaḥ | ata eveśvare "yaḥ pṛthivyāṃ tiṣṭhan pṛthivīmantaro yamayati" ityantaryāmibrāhmaṇāt, "īśvarassarvabhūtānāṃ hṛddeśe'rjuna! tiṣṭhati" iti bhagavadvacanācca | śabdārtharūpeṇa sarvabhūtabhautikaniyantari | pūraṇāt cijjaḍāṃśayossārvatmyaikīkaraṇāt | puruṣatāmupeyuṣi "puri śayanāt, puramoṣati, puraiva śete" ityādiniruktibahutvepi prakṛte vāgarthapūrṇabhāvasyābhilaṣitatvātpūraṇāditi niruktyāśrayaṇamiti veditavyam | puruṣatāmupeyuṣe iti caturthyantapāṭhe -- sadātmani viṣaye jīveśvararūpeṇa, guruśiṣyastrīpuruṣādirūpeṇa vā vāgarthayoravāptapuruṣabhāvasya śabdabrahmaṇo vidyāmārgasampradāyasaṃrakṣaṇadvārā parasparaśreyaḥ praṇidhānārthatvena tādarthye caturthīti jñeyā | vakṣyati cāgrepi -- "yaḥ pramajjayati mūḍhacetasaḥ tārayatyapi sa eṣa dhīmataḥ" iti parasparapramajjanapratāraṇābhyāmatiśreyaḥ praṇidhānārthitvaṃ guruśiṣyayoḥ | ataḥ purauṣatāmupeyuṣe | vāgarthānyatarātmane tadīyaṣaḍadhvātmane vā puruṣāyetyarthaḥ | ye maharṣayaḥ | paraughamahaughadivyaughātmakavāgīśvarīvāgīśvarāvatārarūpāḥ, siddhamānavaśiṣyaudhajīvāvatāravāgarthagururūpāśca ye maharṣaya ityarthaḥ | manaḥ praṇidadhuḥ anyonyasmai manaḥpraṇidhānaṃ cakruḥ | patañjalimukhān tānupasmahe ityupasanāyāṃ sādhakajīvasāhityadyotakaṃ bahuvacanam || 9 ||
<1-10>
evaṃ sakalaughasamaṣṭimayapataṃjalyādigurunamaskārottaraṃ vāgarthasamaṣṭipañca vāhamayaviśvotpattivipattihetusveṣṭadevatāṃ divyādyaughatrayarūpiṇīṃ cidvyomatayā'nusandhatte -- 
khecarīmukhamarīcijālakaṃ
vyomaśaktitanusaṃśra [yaṃ] ye sadā |
śambhudakṣatanujāvabhāsa[nā] yā
yatra viśvamanukartumīhate || 10 ||
khecarīmukhetyādinā | khecarī mukhe prārambhe yāsāṃ marīcīnāṃ vakṣyamāṇānāṃ khecarībhūcarīdṛkcarīdikcarīgocarīnāmakānāṃ tāsāṃ jālakam | siddhānāṃ dehendriyaprāsāde gavākṣarandhrasamudāyarūpam, sādhakānāmaindrajālikajīvadhīvaranirmitendriyagolakayutānāyapaṭarandhrarūpaṃ ca yasminvyomni tatkhecarīmukhamarīcijālakam | `jālaṃ gavākṣa ānāye' ityubhayatrāpyamaraḥ | śaktitanu | siddhānāṃ satyasaṅkalpatvāt śaktestanurūpam | śarīrātmakaṃ śaktireva tanuśśarīraṃ yasyeti vā śaktitanu | sādhakānāmadhikārabhedena śaktistanyate vistīryate kṣīyate vā atreti śaktitanu | yadvyoma daharāparaparyāyaṃ cidgaganam | tadahaṃ sadā saṃśraye | sārvakālikāvismaraṇena svasvarūpatayā kalaye | śaktitanusaṃśrayamiti pāṭhe -- śaktitanvossaṃśrayamitisiddhapakṣe, śaktitanū saṃśrayo yasyeti sādhakapakṣe ca vigrahabhedena vyomna eva viśeṣaṇaṃ jñeyam | asminpāṭhe tādṛśaṃ vyomāstīti śeṣaḥ | yatra vyomni śambhudakṣatanujāvabhāsayā | śambhorānandaparamāvadhisvarūpasukhabhūmerātmano dakṣatanujāyāḥ tattatkāryasamarthadehendriyādisvābhinnavyavahārasatpuruṣakārāntaḥkaraṇavṛtteśca | avabhāsayā | prakāśavimarśasāmarasyarūpeṇeti yāvat | śambhudakṣatanujāvabhāsaneti prathamāntapāṭhe śambhussukhabhūmirātmā | dakṣatanuḥ jñāne karmaṇi vā samarthaṃ śarīram | tābhyāṃ tayorvā jāyata iti śambhudakṣatanujā | sā ca sā'vabhāsanaiva | śambhudakṣatanujāvabhāsanā | yadvā, ādihāntakāmakalātmakamātṛkārṇamālāyāmādyantavarṇapratyāhārasampaditāhamakṣa mātṛkārṇapratyāhāraṃ vāgarthamaryādayā khaṇḍīkṛtya aḥ kathetyātmavisargakathābodhakāthaśabdārtharūpeṇa "oṃkāra ścāthaśabdaśca dvāvetau brahmaṇaḥ purā | kaṣṭhaṃ bhitvā viniryātau tasmānmāṅgalikāvubhau" iti smṛtipratipāditamaṅgalavāgartharūpe, akathādiṣoḍaśārṇamayarekhātrayatripuṭyāṃ trayastriṃśadvarṇapratyāhārasiddhakālīkālamithunarekhādvayaṃ, trikoṇavarṇarekhāsvanantarakartavyajanmādivācakāthaśabdarūpamupāsya tatsiddhyanantaramādikṣāntasameruvarṇamālāyāṃ thakārānantaradakārādikṣāntavarṇapratyāhāreṇa dakṣaśabdarūpaniṣpatteḥ jāyamānatvādantarakṣaśabdārthāhaṃpratyayasyaiva dakṣatvamapi siddhyati | tadānīmakṣameva dakṣamapyahaṃpratyayavacchabdārthaikarasaṃ bhavitumarhati | tathāca, śambhunā khaṇ‍ḍitajīvaśivena, drakṣeṇa samarthendriyeṇa, tanvā śarīreṇaca, jāyamānā'vabhāsanā, śambhudakṣatanujā ityucyate iti vā'rthaḥ | tādṛśī avabhāsanaiva | viśvaṃ vyāvahārikaṃ jagat sat anukartum | śabdārthāveva parasparākāreṇa parasparamanukartumiti bhāvaḥ | īhate icchadiva vartate | tadvyoma saṃśraye | astīti vā pūrveṇānvayaḥ | imamevārthaṃ śabdārthasāmarasyātmakaṃ kālīkālasvarūpatatvaṃ śrībhairavīparābhaṭṭhārikāyai -- `athādyāstithayassarve' ityādinā upadeṣṭumathaśabdaḥ śrītrikaśāstrārambe śrībhairavabhaṭṭārakeṇa prayukta iti tantrasāre laghuvivṛtau śrīmadācāryāminavaguptapādaistopapattikaṃ prapañcitastatraiva draṣṭavyaḥ | akathādiṣoḍaśaṣoḍaśārṇamayarekhātripuṭīpratyāhārādikathā tu prakriyāmātraparāṇāṃ bālānāmavabodhanāyāsmatkapolakalpitakathācamatkāramātramityalaṃ yuktiprastārapallavitena || 10 ||
<1-11>
evamiṣṭadevatāspandātmakaṃ, daharāparaparyāyaṃ, cidvyomasattāsvarūpamanusandhāya tatra prakāśavimarśātmanośśivaśaktyorakhaṇḍakhaṇḍātmano śca siddhamānavaughayośśāktāṇavavisargayoḥ dyotyadyotakabhāvaṃ vivinakti -- 
khaṇḍasatprasaragarbhayā yayā
lakṣitaṃ sphuraṇapūrṇasattayā |
tattvamaiśvaramakhaṇḍamambikām
tvāṃ prāṇaubhyakhilatatvanāyikām || 11 ||
khaṇḍetyādinā | khaṇḍasatprasaragarbhayā | khaṇḍātmanāpi sadrūpāṇi yāni ṣaditraśattatvāni teṣāṃ prasaraḥ garbhe yasyāstayā ataeva sphuraṇapūrṇasattayā tatvākāreṇa khaṇḍasvarūpatvepi sphuraṇākāreṇa pūrṇākhaṇ‍ḍasvarūpayā yayāmbikayā'khaṇḍamaiśvaraṃ tattvaṃ | svarakhaṇḍarūpeṇa śabdātmanā'rthātmanā ca nādanādhasāmarasyarūpeṇākhaṇḍamakārasvarakhaṇḍaṃ khaṇḍākārabhinnaṃ | tadīyārtharūpaṃca tattvamityarthaḥ | lakṣitaṃ bhavati | lakṣaṇayā pratipāditaṃ lakṣaṇena pradarśitaṃ vā bhavatītyarthaḥ | akhilatattvanāyikām | uktaprakāreṇākhilaṣaṭ‌triṃśattattvānāṃ vāgarthatattvasya ca nāyikāṃ tādṛśīṃ tvāṃ praṇaumi | prakṛtināmnā sakalatattvagrahaṇasyānubhūyamānatvāditi bhāvaḥ | yadvā, khaṇḍasatprasaragarbhayā yayāmbikayā sphuraṇapūrṇasat lakṣitaṃ lakṣaṇayā bodhitaṃ, tayā'khaṇ‍ḍamaiśvaraṃ tattvamapi lakṣitaṃ | tasmādakhilatattvanāyikāṃ khaṇḍākhaṇḍayāvattattvanāyikāmityarthaḥ | ata evāmbikāṃ tvāṃ praṇaumītivānvayaḥ|| 11 ||
<1-12>
evaṃ śivaśaktyoḥ dyotyadyotakabhāvaṃ vivicya parāprāsādavagarthātmanā siddhavastutvepi śleṣamaryādayā śiṣyaudhe pārokṣyamāpādayankālapalitaśirasīva jñānapalitaśirasyapi ṣaḍadhvātītavāgarthayugalātmanā vyāvahārikamāśrayāśrayibhāvamapi vivecayati -- 
yaḥ kilāsa sa kilāsa īśvaraḥ
khyātasatva (udi)luli)to(dhva)rdhvamūrdhani |
pratya(sau) haṃ janani! yatra tiṣṭhati tvaṃ
sa (tatva) satvavapuradri (ka) rasya yaḥ || 12 ||
yaḥ kilāsetyādinā | ya īśvara āsakila | sa āsakileva | ubhayatrāpi vidhyanuvādasthānīyapuraḥpaścādvādayoḥ kileti pārokṣya eva | ajñānināmalakānāmni nagare parokṣatvena vartanāditi bhāvaḥ | yadvā, ya āsakila sakilāse tannāmni girau āsakileti vā | hejanani! asau īśvaraḥ | khyātasattvalulitordhvamūrdhani khyātasattvena lulitaṃ yarddhvaṃ śuddhasattvaṃ sattvapradhānenāpi sattvena, sattvapradhānena raja upasarjanenāpi rajomiśritasatvena, sattvapradhānenaiva rajastamomiśritena ca evaṃ tribhāgīkaraṇepi pratibhāgaṃ sattvasyaiva prādhānyāt khyātasattvena lulitasyordhvamūrdhnaḥ dvādaśāntaśirobhāgasya śuddhasattvatvam | evaṃ khyātasatvalulitasyordhvamūrdhnaḥ dvādaśāntaśirobhāgasya kailāsasthānāpannasyordhvamūrdhatvaṃ ca | khyātasatvalulitodhvamūrdhanīti pāṭhe -- adhvamūrdhani vāgarthaṣaḍadhvoparibhāge khyātasatvalulitaḥ | khyātasatvena pūrvoktatribhāgīkṛtasatvena lulitaḥ viśeṣaṇīkṛta itīśvaraviśeṣaṇam | evaṃ uditodhvamūrdhanīti pāṭhe -- adhvamūrdhani vāgarthaṣaḍadhvadvādaśāntaśikhāyām | uditaḥ sañjātodayaḥ | iti | uditordhvamūrdhanīti pāṭhepi sañjātodayaśirasa ūrdhvabhāge khyātasatvaḥ khyātasatvevā kilāse | īśvaraḥ pratitiṣṭhatītīśvaraviśeṣaṇam, kilāsaviśeṣaṇa vā | asau īśvaraḥ yatra pratitiṣṭhati | pratyahamiti pāṭhe -- pratyahaṃ sadā yatra tiṣṭhatīti sparṣṭorthaḥ | asya pratitiṣṭhata īśvarasya | yo'driḥ nivāsabhūtakailāsādriḥ | sa satvavapuḥ pūrvoktatribhāgīkṛtacaśuddhasatvaśarīraḥ | tvameva | tvameva kailāsādrirūpetyarthaḥ | evaṃ tvaṃ sa tatvavapuradrikasyeti pāṭhe -- alpaḥ adriḥ adrikaḥ ityalpārthe kapratyayaḥ | adrikasya parimitatatvamayasya kailāsagireḥ | tvaṃ satatvavapuḥ parimitatatvasahitaśarīramapi tvamevetyanvayaḥ | īśvarabhogāya tadicchānukūlaśarīrātmikā tvamiti bhāvaḥ | evamaviduṣāṃ parokṣaśivaśaktisamanvayānantaraṃ viduṣāṃ siddhavastutvenāparokṣasamanvayapakṣe tu "brāhmaṇosya mukhamāsīt" ityādiśrutyuktacāturvaṇyotpattihetubhūtavirāṭcharīre bhāgakalpanavadeva pādādiśiraḥparyantaṃ paricchinnaśarīrepi mūrtimayavāgarthacatuṣṭayabhāgakalpanena śirasaḥ parāvāgarthamayaparamaśivabhāgatvam | vayomānabhāgeṣvapi vārthakyasya śivābhedaṃ cādhyāropya "kilāsaṃ palitaṃ ca yat" asthijasya kilāsasya | tanūjasya ca yattvaci | kṛtyayā kṛtasya brahmaṇā | lakṣmaśvetamanīnaśam" iti mantroktakālikavārthakyarogādikṛtapalitaśvetādilakṣmaṇaḥ kilāsaśabdārthasyāyurvedādiśāstroktakāyakalpādyaiṣadhasevanacikitsādibhiḥ pratīkāryatvepi kālapakvaśatavārṣikaśiraḥpalitādilakṣmaṇaḥ apratīkāryatvenānubhavaikanivartyatvavatkālīśabdārthakālaśaktyupāsanāvaibhava pakvaparaśśatajanmādyanubhavāgatajñānavṛddhābhinnavāgarthasiddhanādanādhayugalābhinnaṃ śira evānna kailāsārthabodhakakilāsaśabdārtharūpeṇa kavinā vivakṣitamiti niścitya vyākriyate | tathāca, yaḥ kilāsaḥ vāgarthasiddhādinādanādhayugalaśirorūpaḥ | saḥ saevakila | āsaḥ anuttarādisāntapratyāhāraḥ | śāktavisargarūpaḥ | kilāsaśca | śrutismṛtipurāṇāgamādyārṣānārṣavāṅmayavarṇitaḥ kailāsādriśca | kuta iti cet, khyātasatvalulitaḥ | āsamantātprakhyātasakārabījatvadharmeṇānuttaraśāmbhavavisargagarbhitena lulitaḥ | tādṛśaśāktavisargadharmeṇa … āsaḥ ityākāreṇasaṃjātalolaścetyarthaḥ | lulitaḥ udito vā | adhvamūrdhani vāgarthaṣaḍadhvaśirasi | yatra śāmbhavānuttarabindudharmeṇa, śāktānuttaranādadharmeṇa vā krameṇāntarbahi śca lulitaścet antaḥ aṃ iti nādadharmeṇa bahiḥ aḥ | ahamiti ahaṃ śabdārthaśāktanādayutaśāmbavabindurūpeṇa ca lulitaḥ | asau ityākārakaparāśāktanādabindūprati | pratinidhitayetyarthaḥ | tiṣṭhati | tathaiva śāktānuttaranādasakārabījadharmeṇa śāmbhavānuttarabindusakārabījadharmeṇa ca bahirantaścoditaścedvāgarthaṣaḍadhvaśirasyeva bahiḥ | iti vicchinnasvarākhyānuttarānandecchā īśanonmeṣormyākhyavakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇaṣaḍ‌vidhaparāmarśaparāśāktanādarūpeṇa āsa iti vicchinnanādopādhibahutvavāciśabdarūpeṇa | antaśca | asāvityākārakādinādhaśāmbhavabindusvasvarūpeṇa ca piṇḍībhūtānuttarasākṣibhāsyamahamityākārakaṃ paraśāmbhavatripuṭībindutrayaṃ prati… etadbindutrayapratinidhitayetyarthaḥ | tiṣṭhati ca | khyātasatvalulitordhvamūrdhanīti | uditordhvamūrdhanīti vā | pāṭhāntaradvayepi vā | lulitātyantoparibhāge "bindvarthacandrarodhinyo nādanādāntaśaktayaḥ| vyāpikā samanonmanyo navanādāḥ prakīrtitāḥ" ityuktanādanavakoparibhāge sāṃkhyayogaśāstroktadaśamanādamahābindurūpe ityarthaḥ | antarnādasvarūpe ādinādhādinavanādhasamaṣṭisvarūpādinādhevā tadabhidheye'ntaḥ | ahamiti vā | asāviti vā pratitiṣṭhatīti | uditordhvabhāge pūrvoktaśāmbhavabindunādatrayaparāmarśatrayeṇa ṣaṭkena vā śāktanādatrayādirūpeṇa vā nādanavakasya tatsamaṣṭeścavā gaṇane dvādaśāntaṣoḍaśāntādimahābindurūpe unmiṣitadvādaśaśāntādisthāne ca pratitiṣṭhati | bahiścāsau svarakhaṇḍanādaityākāreṇāhaṃcāmukānandanāyomukāmbā vā | ityākāreṇa ca pratitiṣṭhatīti cārthaḥ | evaṃ yatra ahaṃ pratyasāvityākāreṇa pratiyogitayā, asau pratyahamityākāreṇānuyogitayā ca antarbahiśca vā pratitiṣṭhatīti | hejanani! asya parāvāgartharūpasya | ahamityākārakātmamantre'sāvityākārakaparāmantre ca vidyamānasyākārāṃśasyeti ca dhvanyate | yo'driḥ | pūrvoktāhamartharūpo'sāvartharūpo vā nādotpattibhūmiḥ | nādhanivāsabhūmirvā kailāsādrirityarthaḥ | satatvavapuḥ pūrvoktaparāmantre sakārabījākṣaratatvasya śarīram | tvaṃ śāktavisargarūpiṇī | `s au, ityuktaprāṇaspandātmikā tvamevetyarthaḥ | adrikasya ya iti pāṭhe -- satatvavapuriti dvitīyapāṭhamāśrityānvayaḥ | adrikasya jyotiśśāstramaryādayā'kāramātradrekkāṇarūpasya vakṣyamāṇabhāgatrayasamudāye pradhānabhāgasyetyarthaḥ | yadvā, akāranādāt adas ityākārakaparokṣaśabdārtharūpādricyamānā'kārasyeti vā | arthāt sakārākhyaśāktānuttaravisargabindubhyāṃ nādādvā viviktarūpasyeti labhyate | tādṛśasyādrikasya śāktavisargaikadeśeparamantraikadeśasyavā satvavapuḥ satvapradhānaśāmbhavānuttaranādanāthasāmarasyaśarīram | saḥ ahaṃ | parokṣatacchabdārthaśarīro aparokṣāhaṃśabdārthaśarīro bhavatītyarthaḥ | asāvahamiti pratyabhijñayā, sohamityākāreṇa pratyabhijñātasvarūpeṇa vā svarūpānandasākṣātkāravānbhavatīti tātparyārthaḥ | sa evāhaṃ satvavapuścetyarthopi dhvanyate viduṣāṃ siddhavastutvenāparokṣasamanvayapakṣe | ataeva paraudhādidivyaughāntapatañjalyādīśvarāvatāragurvaughatraye patañjaliriva siddhaudhādiśiṣyaughāntakālidāsādijīvāvacāragurvau ghatrayasya kūṭasthādigurunādharūpaḥ kālidāsa ityatra na kasyāpi vivāda iti sarvamavadātam || 12 ||
<1-13>
evaṃ svātmābhedena vāgarthakālīkālayorvāgarthanādatripuṭīśarīreṇa ca gurvau ghaṣaṭkagatamātmāśrayāśrayibhāvamunmīlya, atīndriyatvenānirvacanīyatāṃ vadan pūrṇabhāvamapyunmīlayati -- 
kalpanātigamatīndriyaṃ ca ya
llakṣyamujhjati bahirmukhaṃ (tridhā) tathā |
antarālagamabhāvabhāvayo
ste natosmi sahadaṃ nirākṛ (tiṃ) ti || 13 ||
kalpanātigamityādinā | yat sohaṃ pratyabhijñārūpapūrvaślokoktakailāsādrimayaṃ vapuḥ | atīndriyaṃ dvādaśāntādirūpeṇa vāgarthanādalayasthānatvāditi bhāvaḥ | ataeva kalpanātigaṃ ca | vāgarthakalpakasvātmāhaṃkāra kuleśvarasyāpi layasthānatvenatatra kalpakābhāvena kalpanāyā apyayogāditi bhāvaḥ | tathaiva bahirmukhaṃ lakṣyamujhjati | adhokṣajajñānatatvarūpatayā parāṅmukhendriyavṛttilakṣyatāmapyujhjatītyarthaḥ | taduktaṃ śrutyā "parāṃ cikhāni vyatṛṇatsvayaṃbhūḥ tasmātparāṅpaśyati nāntarātman" iti | tridheti pāṭhe -- divyasiddhamānavaughatrayagataparāmahassiddhimayaśāktatripuṭīrūpeṇa| pramātrādi tripuṭīrūpeṇāpi vā śāmbhavena | bahirmukhaṃ lakṣyabhujhjatītyarthaḥ | ṣaḍadhvātmakavāgarthanādatripuṭyāḥ svasyāpi lakṣyāntarāpekṣāyāmanavasthāpātena turīyasākṣirūpeṇa svasyaiva parasparalakṣyatvāditi bhāvaḥ | ataeva bhāvabhāvayorantarālagam | bhāveṣu ghaṭādiṣu bhāvabodhakavāgartharūpeṇa, abhāveṣu vandhyāputrādituccheṣvapi tadbodhakasadasadvilakṣaṇaśabdānupātitṛtīyatucchavāgartharūpeṇa turīyasākṣyātmanā ca vā svasyaiva sthitatvāditi bhāvaḥ | taduktaṃ mahārthamaṃ jarīparimale -- "sadasatvena bhāvānāṃ yuktānāṃ dvitayī sthitiḥ | tāmullaṅghaya tṛtīyasmai namaścitrāya śambhave" iti | ataeva nirākṛti yathā tathā ātmetaranirākaraṇaṃ yathā bhavati tathā | sadahaṃ | pāramārthikāhaṃśabdartharūpaṃ taṃ kailāsādriṃ vā natosmi| etadeva pāramārthikasadahaṃvāgartharūpamātmatattvamupadeśasāhasryāṃ śrīśaṅkarācāryagurupādairapyuktaṃ -- "sambhāvyo gocare śabdaḥ pratyayo vā na cānyadhā | na sambhāvyau tadātmatvādahaṃkartustathaiva ca" iti | nirākṛtimiti pāṭhe -- tamityasya kailāsādrerviśeṣaṇam | nanvatra svātmana eva namyanaṃtṛbhāvaḥ kathamiti na śaṅkanīyam | svasminneva vāgarthāṃśakalpanenatadupapatteḥ | tathācoktam -- "dikkālādyanavacchinnānantacinmātramūrtaye | śātṛjñeyasvarūpāya mahyameva namo namaḥ" iti || 13 ||
<1-14>
tasyaiva vyāpakavidyā'vidyābhuvanātmanā brahmasvarūpatāmapyāha -- 
amba! yadbhavati tattadātmanā
sveritāmajahadadvayodayam |
tāvakaṃ pada (ma) mupākṛtakramaṃ
brahma tatsaditi (hi) vā śrutaṃ bhaje || 14 ||
ambetyādinā | he amba ! bhārate śāntiparvaṇi 266 adhyāye "aṅgānāṃ vardhanādambā" iti nirukteḥ | yadvā, "ambe ambālyambike" ityaśvamedhaprakaraṇamantroktamahāprāṇasatvaprakṛtirūpiṇīti vā aṃ pūrvauktākārasvaranādātmakavāgartharūpaṃ bamativabayorabhedevivakṣayā vātīti vā ambā | tasyāssambuddhiramba daharātmike ityarthaḥ | yat pūrvaślokoktakailāsādrimayaṃ vāgarthaśarīramayaṃ vā tāvakaṃ padam | tattadātmanā deśakālapadārthādyātmanā bhavati | taduktaṃ saubhāgyahṛdaye -- "deśakālapadārthātmā yadyadvastu yathā tathā | tattadrūpeṇa yā bhāti tāṃ śraye'haṃ vibhoḥ kalām" iti | "sthitirnāma svarūpasyatattadrūpatayā dhṛtiḥ" iti śrīsaṃvitstotre ca | punaḥ kīdṛśaṃ? sveritāṃ svasvarūpābhedeneritāṃ parātmikāṃ vācamajahat | ataevādvayodayamadvitīyatvenodīyamānasphuraṇātmakam | taduktaṃ śivasaṃhitāyām "akārassarvavavarṇāgryaḥ prakāśaḥ paramaśśivaḥ | hakārontyaḥ kalārūpo vimarśākhyaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || anayossāmarasyaṃ yadahamityeva nirmalaḥ | ahantābhāsitaṃ bhāsyamidaṃtākhyaṃ ca tanmayaṃ | anayossāmarasyaṃ yatpūrṇāhantā parā matā" iti | ataeva śrāpādukodaye vākchabdaniruktirapyevameva darśitā -- "tvāmupāsitagurūttamāḥ parāṃ vācamāhuravibhaktaviśvakāṃ | svaprakāśanavimarśanātmikāṃ vakti vāmiti niruktimāsthitāḥ " iti | punarapi kīdṛśaṃ ? apākṛtakramaṃ | apākṛtaḥ tyaktaḥ kramaḥ āvidyakasṛṣṭyādikramaḥ yasmin tat yena vā tat | upākṛtakramamiti pāṭhe -- ārabdhollaṅghanātmakakramamityarthaḥ | sa ca kramaḥ evaṃ darśitaḥ | śrīsaṃvitstotre -- bhāvāvṛttiṣu tāścitte cittaṃ saṃvidi sā pare | yomnyastaṃgamitā yatra krama ullaṅghanātmakaḥ" iti | mātṛkāstavarāje kālidāsenāpi darśito yatā -- "jagatkāye kṛtvā tadapi hṛdaye tacca puruṣe pumāṃsaṃ bindusthaṃ tamapi viyadākhye ca gahane | tadetat jñānākhye tadapi paramānandagahane mahāvyomākāre tvadanubhavaśīlo vijayate" iti ca | etādṛśaṃ tāvakaṃ padaṃ | brahmeti suprabuddhaṃ pravṛddhaṃ vā mahadānandarūpamiti | taditi | parokṣamiti | sat pāramārthikapratyakṣarūpamiti ca śrutaṃ hi | prasiddhaṃ śrutipratipādanamiti bhāvaḥ | tatsaditi vā śrutamiti pāṭhe -- vā vikalpena | nāmarūpāṃśarāhityenetyarthaḥ | brahmādipadajātena śrutaṃ śrutipratipāditaṃ bhaje ityanvayaḥ || 14 ||
<1-15>
evaṃ brahmasvarūpatāṃ nirūpya tasyaiva pāṭhabhedena adhyāropāpavādābhyāmapi svasvarūpatvabodhanāya karmahetuvidhiniṣedhamārgeṇāpi taddhitabahirvijṛmbhaṇaṃ darśayati -- deśakāletyādinā |
deśakālakalanāviśeṣato
yadbhavāni vihitaṃ (nigadyate) niṣidhyate |
vyaktijātitanusaṃśritakramaṃ
taddhitaṃ tava bahirvijṛmbhaṇam || 15 ||
he bhavāni ! bhūdhātvarthakajātisattāsāmānyādijñānapradānaśivasaṃsāravācakabhavaśabdārthabhūtabhavasya patni, pālayatrīti vā | sābhiprāyassambudvyarthaḥ | deśa kālakalanayorviśeṣataḥ | yathā "prācīnapravaṇe vaiśvadevena yajeta" "revātīre tapaḥ kuryānmaraṇaṃ jāhnavītaṭe" ityādideśakalanāyāḥ yathāvā | "vasante vasante jyotiṣā yajeta" "udagayanapūrvapakṣeṣu puṇyāheṣu kāryāṇi" "adhivṛkṣasūrye vācaṃ visṛjati" "graste snāyāt" ityādikālakalanāyāśca | yadvā, pūrvoktadeśakālayormadhye deśasya kalātatvabhuvanātmakārthādhvatrike kālasya varṇapadamantrātmakaśābdādhvatrike cāntarbhāvāttayoḥ deśakālayoḥ kalanānāṃ kriyājñānānāṃ "kṣepo jñānaṃ ca saṃkhyānaṃ gatirnāda iti kramāt" ityuktakalanāśabdārtharūpāṇāṃ vā | athavā kalanāyāḥ "vedaṃ kṛtvā vediṃ karoti" "jñātvā karmāṇi kurvīti" ityādikramaviśeṣajñānasya vā| viśeṣataḥ bhedavidhānaniṣedhamārge svavāgarthakriyākramabhedavidhiniṣedhamārgaviśeṣādityarthaḥ | vyaktijātitanusaṃśritakramaṃ vyaktisaṃśritakramaḥ "svargakāmaḥ,putrakāmaḥ, bhūtikāmo yajeta" ityādiḥ | jātisaṃśritakramaḥ "brāhmaṇo bṛhaspatisavena yajeta" ityādiḥ | jātisaṃśritakramaḥ "brāhmaṇo bṛhaspatisavena yajeta | rājā rājasūyena | vaiśyo vaiśyastomena yajeta | varṣāsu rathakārognīnādadhīta | etayā niṣādasthapatiṃ yājayet" ityādiḥ | tanusaṃśritakramastu "kṛṣṇakeśo'gnīnādadhīta" "śvetamālabheta" ityādiḥ | evaṃ vyaktijātitanusaṃśritakramam | vihitaṃ sañjātavidhivyāparam | yannigadyate | adhyāropavidhivyāpāreṇa kaṇṭharaveṇevocyate | niṣidhyata iti pāṭhe adhyāropakramavihitaṃ netineti vākyaiḥ "ātmā purastā" dityādideśaviśeṣataḥ; "bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ bhavacca sarvamātme"veti kālaviśeṣataḥ , `yasyāmataṃ tasya mata'mityādikalanāviśetaśca | tathā -- `na jātu kāmaḥ kāmānāmupabhogena śāmyati' `aśanāyā pipāsādirahitam' `pitā'pitā bhavati' `devā adevāḥ' `vedā avedāḥ' ityādivyaktyāśritakramaniṣedhamukhena, `apetabrahmakṣatrādibheda'mityādijātyāśrikramaniṣedhamukhena, `arūpamasparśaṃ' `asthūlamanaṇu' ityāditanvāśritakramāniṣedhamukhena ca | yanniṣidhyate | aikātmyapratyayasārakaniṣedhavākyairatadvyāvṛttyā apodyate | tattava bahirvijṛmbhaṇaṃ | vidhimukhena niṣedhamukhena vā bahirvilasanamityarthaḥ | hitaṃ yathākramaṃ sādhakānāṃ siddhānāṃ ca pathyamevetyarthaḥ | pratiṣedhasya prasaktipūrvakatvaniyamāt vidyākaraṇakaniṣedhasyāvidyāprāptipūrvakatvaṃ niyamitamiti bhāvaḥ | kiñcātra tava bahivrijṛmbhaṇaṃ vāgarthayorvidhimukhena niṣedhamukhenāpi | taddhitameva sāsya devateti prākṛtātmadaivatyataddhitapratyayavāgartharūpamevetyapi gamyate | detārūpe vidhiniṣedhāṃśayostaddhitapratyayavāgartharūpamevetyapi gamyate | devatārūpe vidhiniṣedhāṃśayostaddhitapratyayavāgarthavyāpārasyaiva balavattaratvācca | tathācoktaṃ vārtikepi -- "taddhitena caturthyā vā mantravarṇena vā punaḥ | devatāyā vidhistatra durbalaṃ tuṃ paraṃ param" iti | tena vidyāvasthāyāṃ vidvadbhirantarmukhaissiddhaiḥ śanaiśśanairvidyābhivṛddhivaśādvyaktyāśrito'dhyāsakramaḥ vidyāphalalābhāya virodhitayā yathā yathā nirasyate tathā tathā avidyāvasthāyāmavidvadbhirbahirmukhaissādhakaiśśanairavidyāvṛddhivaśātsa evādhyāsakramo'vidyāphalalābhāyāvirodhitayānuṣṭhātumādriyata ityayamarthaḥ anuraṇanadhvanimaryādayā siddhyati | "bahūnāṃ janmanāmante jñānavānmāṃ prapadyate | vāsudevassarvamiti sa mahātmā sudurlabhaḥ || kāmaistaistaiḥ hṛtajñānāḥ prapadyante'nyadevatāḥ | taṃ taṃ niyamamāsthāya prakṛtyā niyatāḥ svayā ||" ityādibhagavadvacanāni, "nītijñā niyatijñāḥ śāstrajñāssantu lokesmin | brahmajñā api bhavantu vedajñāssvājñānajñānino viralāḥ ||" ityādimahāmahopādhyāyanīlakaṇṭhādīkṣitasubhāṣitoktiścāsminnevārthe paryavasyantīti dik || 15 ||
<1-16>
evaṃ hṛdguhāmityādyekādaśabhiḥ sūtraprāyaiḥ ślokairekarūpeṇa prathamaślokoktavāgarthamayasvātmavastunaḥ vidhiniṣedhāparaparyāyasṛṣṭisaṃhārakramākramābhyāṃ vyarthakramamuupanyasya tayoradhiṣṭhānāsphuraṇaśaṅkākalaṅkitānāmavabodhāya tasyaiva kramākramapratinivartanena tripuṭīprabhṛtidvādaśaṣoḍaśāntapāryantikaprastārabhedena tricatuḥpañcavāhamahārthakramarūpatāmapi vaktuṃ chandogyopaniṣanniruktatrivṛtkṛtatejo'bannātmakabhūtatrayasthūlasūkṣmarūpatvena guṇatrayātmanā sṛṣṭyādikṛtyatrayasphuraṇarūpatāṃ ca pratijānīte -- 
satvamamba vihitastha vastunaḥ
tatprasādita [ta] dayodayaṃ vapuḥ |
syāttamaśca yadidaṃ niṣidhyate
(dve) dvairajaḥ sadasadanyarūpataḥ || 16 ||
satvabhaṃvetyādinā | he ambeti sambuddhiḥ pūrvavat | vihitasya upādeyatvena heyatvenānuṣṭhātavyena vā sañjātatrividhavidhipratyayavyāpārasya | vastunaḥ vasatyasminnātmasvarūpamiti vyutpattyā siddhadravyarūpasya sādhyasya vā vāgarthaikarūpasya | tatprasāditadayodayaṃ | tatprasāditaḥ tadvastuprasādīkṛtaḥ | dayāyāḥ bhūtadayāyāḥ udayaḥ utpattiḥ yasmin tat tādṛśaṃ vapuḥ upatesmin dayābījamaneneti vā vyutpattibhedenābhyantaraṃ bāhyaṃ vā dayāmayaṃ śarīram | satvaṃ nāma guṇo bhavati | ayamāśayaḥ "rasyāssnigdhāḥ sthirāḥ hṛdyā āhārāssātvikapriyāḥ" ityādividhānavat | yadvastu vāgarthānyatarākāreṇa sevyatvenānuṣṭhātavyatvena vā vihitam, tadvastusevanaprasādīkṛtasarvabhūtadayodayavatīyā'hantā tanmayaṃ vapuḥ pūrvoktakailāsāparaparyāyaṃ satvamityucyata iti | tatprasāditatayodayamiti pāṭhe -- tadvastukṛtuprasādatvenodīyamānaṃ vapurityarthaḥ | tathā yadidaṃ niṣidhyate tattamaśca syāt | idaṃ taiva tamoguṇasthūlarūpatayā niṣidhyata iti bhāvaḥ | etena vāgarthātmake svātmarūpe ahaṃtedantābuddhyoreva vidhiniṣedhaviṣayatvaṃ na svarūpavastuna iti siddham | taduktaṃ vedāṃtapañcadaśyāmapi citradīpe śrīvidyāraṇyagurupādaiḥ vāgarthayoḥ jīvajagadbhāvamabhisanvāya -- "jīvabhāvajagadbhāvabādhe svātmaiva śiṣyate | nāpratītistayorbādhaḥ kintu mithyātvaniścayaḥ ||" iti | evaṃ sadasadanyarūpataḥ | sadasatoḥ pūrvoktāhantedantābuddhyoranyarūpataḥ | rūpabhedāt | sadahaṃ | idaṃ buddhigocare nāsmi | asadidaṃ | ahaṃ buddhigocare na bhavitumarhatītyākārakavāgarthasvasvarūpabhedādityarthaḥ | dve ahantedante | militveti bhāvaḥ | rajaḥ nāma bhavati | dvai raja iti pāṭhe -- dvayo rajasorvāgarthaparamāṇvossamāhārau dvairajasī | tayossadasadanyarūpataḥ mithunīkṛtasatyānṛtavāgartharūpabhedāt rajonāma bhavatīti vigrahaḥ | etena ahantedantayorvāgarthaparamāṇvoḥ rūpabedamiśraṇe niyatyaparaparyāyanāmakalopetaṃ rajonāma guṇaḥ | abhedena svasvarūpaikye brahmātmatā ca svarūpasthityaparaparyāyetyuktamiti veditavyam | ataevātra manaḥ prāṇavācāmeva guṇatrayāhantāmayaśarīratvamabhisandhāya bhāvaprakāśe prathamādhikāre -- "yatsatvapariṇāmi syāddravyaṃ tanmana ucyate | īśvarasya ca muktānāṃ tatsaṅkalpo bhaviṣyati || saṃsāriṇāmahantvena pariṇamya pravartate | tatsatvapariṇāmitvātsatvamityucyate budhaiḥ || yadrajaḥ pariṇāmi syāddravyaṃ sa prāṇaṃ ucyate | īśvarasya ca muktānāṃ kriyāhetussaritaḥ || saṃsāriṇāṃ punarasau prāṇākāreṇa tiṣṭhati | yattamaḥ pariṇāmisyāddravyaṃ sā vāgudāhṛtā || īśvarasya muktānāṃ sā vāgbhavati śobhanā | saṃsāriṇāṃ pariṇamecchabdākāreṇa sā punaḥ || uktā krodhādibhirbhāvaistatphalā'vyabhicāriṇī | yā mukteśvaravāgutthā sā vāṇītyucyate budhaiḥ || ravissomaśca vahniśca tasya tasya yathākramam | adhiṣṭhātāra ityeṣā vyavasthā yogibhiḥ kṛtā ||" iti `sadvidyāhamidaṃdhiyoḥ' iti śuddhavidyāsvarūpe tantrāntarañca || 16 ||
<1-17>
evaṃ vāgarthayoreva guṇatrayasūkṣmarūpatāṃ vivicya bhūtatrayasya guṇatrayasthūlarūpatāmāha -- 
yattamonyadiha tatkṣi [te] tirvapu
satvamanyadiha tejasaśśive |
miśraṇotthamitaradrajaḥ tayoḥ
tvatkasatva (śa) jamato guṇatrayam || 17 ||
yadityādinā | heśive! maṅgalarūpe `vaś‌ kāntau' iti dhātoḥ varṇavyatyayādicchārūpe iti vā sambuddhiḥ | saptamī vā śivādhikaraṇavivakṣayā | yat anyattamaḥ pūrvoktasūkṣmarūpāt rūpāntaraṃ trivṛtkṛtasthūlaṃ tama ityarthaḥ | eva muttaratrāpi | tatkṣitarvapurbhavati | kṣitirvapuriti pāṭhe -- kṣitireva rūpāntaraṃ tava tamaśśarīramityarthaḥ | evamanyat satvaṃ saddharmasthūlarūpaṃ tejaso vapurbhavati | tayossatvatamasormiśraṇotthaṃ itarat sthūlaṃ raja āpyānāpyabhūtasya vapurbhavatītyarthaḥ | etena pārthivataijasorāgneyasaumyayośca tamassatvayormiśraṇotthaṃ itarat sthūlaṃ raja āpyānāpyabhūtasya vapurbhavatītyarthaḥ | etena pārthivataijasorāgneyasaumyayo śca tamassatvayormiśraṇotthasya rajasa āpyānāpyabhūtasammelanajanyatvoktyā kṣutpipāsadharmakasya tasya śukrīyaprāṇasūryamayaparamāṇavaśarīrasyāpi tryaṇukadvayasammelanena myamaparimāṇamapyuktaprāyaṃ veditavyam | evaṃ tvatkatvaśamataḥ vāgartharūpiṇyāstava satvaguṇaśamadharmāt | guṇatrayaṃ pūrvoktavidhiniṣedhodāsīnavyāpārehetukaṃ bhavatīti śeṣaḥ | tvatkasatvajamiti pāṭhe -- tvadīyaśuddhasatvaguṇajanyameva miśrajātīyavakṣyamāṇaliṅgatrayahetukaṃ guṇatrayarūpamityarthaḥ | atredamanusandheyam | evaṃ bhūtatrayasya guṇatrayasthūlakāryatvanirūpaṇena jalabhūtasya svakāryakāraṇabhūtakṣititejasormadhyakendre tanūnāpādapānaṃpānnamakhamedhyāmedhyasāmarasyarasātmanā | vāyvākāśasāmarasyasthūlopādhitvamiva mamatāparaparyāyarajoguṇasyāpi svakāryakāraṇātmakānnamayānandakośayostamassatvasthūlarūpayormadhye vidyā'vidyāmayaśābdārthādhvatrikadvayamaṣṭirūpavijñānamanaḥ prāṇamayakośatritayasamaṣṭyāṃ liṅgaśabdavyavahāryāyāṃ tatrāpi madhyasthitamānasasarovararājahaṃsātmakamithunāśrayeṇa ca vīryavattarasūkṣmopādhirūpatvamunneyam | ataeva ṣaḍadhvabodhakamātṛkārṇamālāyāmapi mātṛkācakravivekoktarītyā svarakhaṇḍasya jaḍasuṣiptidaśābodhakatvepi cinmayaṣoḍaśasomakalābodhakatayā saumyasvayaṃrājamānatvadharmeṇa svasvaratvakhaṇḍatvākṣaratvādikaṃ nirvācyam | tathā sparśānāṃ pañcendriyajaḍajāgraddaśābodhakatvepi cinmayamukhyaprāṇasūryaturīyajāgraddaśābodhakatvena cinmayasparśabodhakatvāt sparśakhaṇ‍ḍatvādikaṃ ca | tathā'ntasthoṣmāparaparyāyavyāpakavarṇakhaṇḍasyāpi bhūtāgneriva jāṭharāgnerapi bhuktānnapānādijāraṇenāntarmanaḥ kalpitavikalpātmakavyāvahārikasaṃsāradāhakatayāntarmukhavimarśānalaprādhānyenāntasthoṣmavyāpakakhaṇḍatvādikaṃ cetyādigurumukhaikavedyam | vākprāṇamanasāṃ tejobannabhavatvaṃ chāndogyopaniṣadi "annamayaṃ hi somyamanaḥ āpomayaḥ prāṇaḥ tejomayī vāk" ityādinā sopapattikaṃ varṇitaṃ tatraiva draṣṭavyam | etena mukhyaprāṇāgnereva maṇḍalatrayarūpatvaṃ sūryacandrayostadavayavajanyatvañca vakṣyamāṇaprakāśānandamūrticakramayavāgarthadevatāsvarūpavarṇanopodbalakaṃ sūcitam | tadapyukaṃta śrutyā "candramā manaso jātaḥ| cākṣassūryo ajāyata" ityādikayā | kiñca candrasya "yasmā nna jātaḥ paro anyo asti | ya āviveśa bhuvanāni viśvā | prajāpatiḥ prajayā saṃvidānaḥ | trīṇi jyotīgṣiṃ saca te saṣoḍaśī" ityādimantroktasakalajagatkāraṇaṣoḍaśīkalārūpatvaṃ ca | tathaiva jyotiśśāstroktā''pyagolasya candrasya bhūmyupagrahatvavarṇanamukhena kāryarūpatvaṃ | tathā sūryasyāpi "apāg‌ rasamudayag sann | sūrye śukrag‌ samābhṛtam | ime vai lokā apāg‌ rasaḥ | te'muṣṇinnādityeḥ samābhṛtāḥ" tathā "āpo vā idag‌ sarvam" ityādimantrabrāhmaṇavākyaparyālocanena "ekaikasminyathā'darśe prāsādo muhurāntaraiḥ | sahito dṛśyate deveṣvevaṃ lokassurāntaraiḥ || tasmātsyurdevatāssarvāḥ pratyekaṃ viśvayonayaṛ | anyonyayonayaścaiva yatāyāskamunīritam || ityuktasarvabhūtātmabhūtātmabhāvenaiva sarveṣāṃ sarvajagatkāraṇatvamityapi paryavasyatīti dik || 17 ||
<1-18>
evaṃ triguṇamayyā vāgarthaprakṛterbhedasṛṣṭihetutāṃ samunmīlya tasyā eva saṃsārātmanāpi paramaiśvaryarūpatāmāviṣkaroti -- 
īśvarasya tava bharturakṣayai
śvaryarūpamasi tatvamambike! |
jñānamasya śuci karma vā phalaṃ
te virāgamayapūrṇatā phale || 18 ||
īśvarasyetyādinā | he ambike ! pūrvaślokoktāsampūrṇasatvatamośamiśraṇavāgarthaśarīratayā prādeśikavāgarthaśarīrātmike ityalpavāgarthaśarīravivakṣayā ambike! iti sambuddhyarthaḥ | uktāca "ambe! ambālyambike" ityanuvāke aśvamedhakāṇḍe trividhavāgarthaśarīrā śaktiriti tatraiva draṣṭavyā | ataeva tava bharturīśvarasya | bibhartīti bharteti vigrahāttvatpoṣakasyātmana ityarthaḥ | tvaṃ pūrvoktaguṇatrayasthūlasūkṣmarūpiṇī tvamityarthaḥ | akṣayaiśvaryarūpaṃ vāṅmayaśarīre | ādikṣāntagaṇanayā punarapi vailomyena kṣādiyāntagaṇanayā ca vakṣyamāṇajñānamantrayogaśāktaraśmisaṃjñakabṛndacakraraśmīnāṃ ṣaṣṭhisaṅkhyākānāṃ "triṣaṣṭhi rvā catuṣṣaṣṭhirvarṇāśśambhumate matāḥ" iti vakṣyamāṇaśāmbhavavarṇaraśmiṣu caturvidhaśāmbhavaraśmirahitānāṃ lābhādakṣayaiśvaryarūpatvam | vakṣyati cāgrepi "kṣāntayādikalitākṣamālayā" ityādinā khaṇḍacakravarṇanāvasare imamevāśayam | arthamayaśarīretu | ahorātrātmakakālāvayavaṣaṣṭhisaṅkhyākaghaṭikāvighaṭikātithivatsarādinavavidhakālamānagaṇitasvāyambhuvakālāvayavaraśmyādijāgataiśvaryā bhedavivakṣayā cākṣayaiśvaryarūpatvaṃ bodhyamiti vivekaḥ | evamubhayavidhavāgarthāyaiśvaryarūpam | tat parokṣatvena vyavahāryam | tvaṃ aparokṣatvena yuṣmatpratyayavāgarthavyavahāryaṃ ca | tattvaṃ tvamevāsi | vāgarthasṛṣṭyādikṛtyapañcakasaṃsārātmaneti bhāvaḥ | taduktaṃ tantrāloke "sārametatsamastasya yaccitsāraṃ jaḍaṃ jagat | tadadhīnapratiṣṭhatvātsaṃsāraṃ hṛdayaṃ mahat" iti |
nanu yadi bhedasṛṣṭimayavāgarthasaṃsārasyāpi paramaiśvaryarūpatā, tarhi tasya "tyāgabhogaphalaṃ dhanam" iti nyāyena dhānyapaśuratnakanakarajatādimānuṣadhanasyeva phalamapi vaktavyamityāśaṅkya phalamapi darśayati -- jñānamasyeti | asya vāgarthasaṃsārātmakaiśvaryasya | śuci pariśuddhaṃ jñānaṃ, karma vā phalaṃ bhavati | jñānasya pāriśuddhyaṃ adhītibodhātmakatvam | karmaṇastu ācaraṇapracāraṇayoḥ phalānabhisandhirūpam |
tayorapi phalamāha -- te virāgeti | te pariśuddhajñānakarmaṇi | virāgamayapūrṇatāphale | virāgamayā virāgapracurā | yā pūrṇatā pūrṇabhāvaḥ | cijjaḍabhedabhāvarāhityena vāgarthaikarūpyasāmarasyamiti yāvat | saiva phalaṃ yayoste tathokte bhavitavye iti śeṣaḥ || 18 ||
<1-19>
evaṃ sāmānyena vāgarthayorakṣayaiśvaryarūpatāmunmīlya tatrāpi sūkṣmekṣikayā aupaniṣadayoreva tayośśāktaśaivayoriva pūrṇabhāvasāmarasyena puruṣārthatvadyotanāya satvatamoguṇamayadevatāsthūlarūpatāṃ pratyekatayāpi sūcayati -- 
jyotiramba! hṛdi vidyayekṣitaṃ
yallaghuprasṛtamarthamaṇḍo |
dhūmalakṣma guru yacca tat (cyu) ca taṃ
te tu satvatamasī vapustava || 19 ||
jyotirambetyādinā | he ambeti sambuddhiḥ pūrvavat | arthamaṇḍape pañcendriyapravṛttigocarapāñcabhautikabhūtapañcakakṛtyādyātmake | laghuprasṛtaṃ yat jyotiḥ "nīvāraśūkavattanvī pītā bhāsvatyaṇūpamā | tasyāśśikhāyā yadhyeparamātmā vyavasthitaḥ | sa brahma sa śivassaharissendrasso'kṣaraḥ paramaḥ svarāṭ" iti śrutyuktaśikhārūpatayā vibhāvyaṃ yadātmajyotiḥ | sarvadevatāmayaṃ pūrvoktadvairajorūpaṃ yadātmajñānajyotirityarthaḥ | vakṣyati cāgrepi caturthavimarśe "jyotiṣāmbaśikhayā sa hanyate puṃsi caitasikamuddhataṃ tamaḥ" ityādinā idameva jyotirabhisandhāya | tat jyotiḥ | hṛdi hṛdayākāśe | vidyayā "sattāyāṃ vidyate jñāne vetti vicāraṇe | vindate vindati prāptau" ityuktayāvadvidildhātupratyayaprātipadikārtharūpayā | aparokṣasākṣātkāravṛtyeti yāvat | īkṣitaṃ sākṣātkṛtaṃ "sa īkṣata lokānnu sṛjā iti" ityuktasañjātekṣaṇavyāpāravatkṛtaṃ ca yat jñānamayam | yacca karmamayaṃ jyotiḥ | dhūmalakṣma | dhūmasya lakṣmeva lakṣma yasya tat "dhūmo bhūtvā'bhraṃ bhavati" "dhūmadhvajaṃ lohitākṣaṃ" ityādiśrutismṛtibhyāṃ gūḍhābhisandhidyotitātīndriyavaidikakriyāmayaṃ, tatphalakṛtikartṛbhoktṛrūpe ca | ataeva cyutaṃ sat guru | nissāratvajñānottaraṃ svayameva galitam | lokasaṃgrahārthatvena lokagururūpaṃ, śreṣṭhaṃ ca vā | dhūma eva lakṣma yasya taditi kriyāvigrahavyutpattyā pūrvoktajyotirmayajñānarūpāpekṣayā sthūlarūpaṃ ceti vā "gurustu gīṣpatau śreṣṭhe gurau pitari durbhare" ityamaraḥ | tacca tamiti pāṭhe -- tamityuddeśaprādhānyātpuṃstvam | puṃ rūpaṃ tamarthamaṇḍapam | te pūrvokte jñānakarmaṇī satvatamasī tava vapuḥ śarīraṃ sat prasṛtamiti hṛdi vidyayekṣitamiti pūrveṇānvayaḥ |
etena vaidikajñānakarmamayaṃ vapustava tvadīyabhartuḥ puruṣasya cārthamaṇḍapātmakasya samameva prasṛtamiti vedanameva pūrṇāhantāmayaṃ pūrṇabhāvavidyāphalamiti tātparyārthaḥ | tadetaduktaṃ śrutyāpi "kaściddhavā asmāllokātpretya | ātmanaṃ veda | ayamahamasmīti | kaścitsvaṃ lokaṃ na pratiprajānāti | agnumugdhohaiva dhūmatāntaḥ | svaṃ lokaṃ na pratiprajānāti | atha yohaivaitamagnigaṃ sāvitraṃ veda | sa evāsmāllokātpretya | ātmānaṃ veda | ayamahamasmīti | sa svaṃ lokaṃ pratiprajānāti" ityādisāvitrāgnibrahmavidyāprakaraṇasthayā | etena vāgarthayoriva jñānakarmaṇorapi prātisvikapradhānāṅgabhāvena satvatamomayaśarīratāpi veditavyā vicitravāsanābhedabalādityapi samudāyatātparyārthaḥ || 19 ||
<1-20>
evaṃ vāgartharūpayoḥ vaidikajñānakarmaṇoreva vaidakasāṅkhyayogamayabhinnaprasthānavāgarthatripuṭībhedaprakhyāpanena ṣaḍadhvagurvaughaṣaṭkatārūpaṃ phalaikyamapi darśayati -- 
tvaṃ (hi) tu rudrajakaṭākṣavartinī
jāyamānamavalokya caṇḍike |
sāṃkhyayogapathadeśikadvayaṃ
jātasammati (sasādhikaṃ) samīpsitaṃ yathā || 20 ||
tvaṃ hītyādinā | hecaṇḍike ! `caḍi krodhe' iti dhāto svarūpetare krodhamayidevīti sābhiprāyasambuddhiḥ | tvaṃ | asmadvāgarthapratyayapratiyoginī tvanityarthaḥ | hi yasmāt | sāṃkhyayogapathadeśikadvayam | sāṃkhyayogapathāveva pathayorvā deśikau | tayordvayaṃ prātisvikavāgarthaprathānau pravṛttinivṛttivāṅmayavidyāmārgau | pūrvoktajīveśvarasrotobhedena "adhītibodhācaraṇapracāraṇairdaśāścatasraḥ praṇayannupādhibhiḥ | caturdaśatvaṃ kṛtavānkutassvayaṃ na vedmi vidyāsu caturdaśa svayam" iti śrīharṣoktau tayordvayaṃ naiṣadhīyavidyāsu saṃkhyādaśāmayadharmadvayavadābhāsamānavirodhamārgadvayarūpam | tadupadeṣṭṛgurudvayarūpaṃ vetyarthaḥ | karmaṇi tu śrautavāgarthamaye | pravṛttinivṛttibhedena jñānayogamārgadvayaṃ tadupadeṣṭṛgurudvayaṃ vetyarthaḥ | jāyamānaṃ janimāpadyamānamavalokya | rudrajakaṭākṣavartinī -- rudrajaḥ ṣaḍṛtuṣaṇmukhādirūpaḥ | taṃ vartayasi sāṃkhyayogapathadeśikadvayaṃ prati pravartayasi | deśikadvayasya pravṛttaye nivṛttaye tvaṃ vā pravartasa iti rudrajakaṭākṣavartinī | mārgadvayasaṃhārābhisandhinā rudrajakaṭākṣe prakṛtirūpiṇī tvaṃ vā vartasa iti vā rudrajakaṭākṣavartinī bhavasītyarthaḥ | tasmāttatsāṃkhyayogapathadeśikadvayam | jātasammatisamādhikaṃ sammatiḥ samyagjñānaṃ, samādhiḥ yathāvadanuṣṭhānam | te jāte yasya tat jātasammatisamādhikam | jāyamānamavalokyaiva | sṛṣṭhisaṃhārāparaparyāyapravṛttinivṛttisamādhikam | jāyamānamavalokyaiva | sṛṣṭhisaṃhārāparaparyāyapravṛttinivṛttimayaprakṛtisvābhāvyena rudrajakaṭākṣavartinī bhavasi | `tvaṃ tu rudrajakaṭākṣavartinī' `jātasammatisamīpsitaṃ yathā' iti pāṭhadvaye pūrvaślokoktākṣayāpāramaiśvaryarūpiṇī tvaṃ tu rudrajakaṭākṣavartinī | katham ? pūrvoktasāṃkhyayogapathadeśikadvayamanyonyasāmarasyena jātasammati | ataeva samīpsitaṃ yathā tathā icchānurūpyeṇa jāyamānamavalokyaiva tvaṃ rudrajakaṭākṣavartinī bhavasītyarthaḥ | ubhayadhāpi vaidikasāṃkhyayogayoḥ phalaikyaṃ tvadīyarudrajakaṭākṣavartanena tatsamādhānayutena tvatsamādhānayutatajjanmanā vā sūcitamiti bhāvaḥ | ataeva gītāsu bhagavānapi "sāṅkhyayogau pṛthagbālā pravadanti na paṇḍitāḥ | ekamapyāsthitassamyagubhayorvindate phalam" ityādinā samyagāsthānenāsthāpanena ca phalaikyamupadideśa || 20 ||
<1-21>
evaṃ sāṅkhyayogapathāparyāyavaidikavāgarthasthānīyayoḥ jñānakarmaṇo rābhāsamānavirodhaparihāreṇa phalaikyaṃ pradarśya tayoreva pūrṇimā'mārūpeṇa vaidikīṃ patilomānulomasṛṣṭisaṃhārahetutāṃ ca varṇayan prabandhaprameyāṃ cāpi sūtrayati -- 
sva [sa] prakāśakṛtamajjanaṃ jaga
tkurvatī bhavati pūrṇimā śive |
pūrṇameva tava rūpamanyadhā
kurvatī kila kūhūḥ pratīyase || 21 ||
sva (sa) prakāśamityādinā | hebhavati | pūrvoktasatvatamoṃśasammelanātmakarajaśśarīreṇa bhūśabdārthavyāvahārikajñānakriyāpradhānaśivasaṃsārātmike iti sambuddhyarthaḥ | śive! maṅgalarūpe! kālaśive jīvaśivo vā | saprakāśakṛtamajjanaṃ saprakāśaṃ prakāśasahitaṃ yathātathā | kṛtamajjanaṃ kṛtāvabudhaṃ, kṛtābhyāṅgasnānaṃ ca, svaprakāśakṛtamajjanamiti pāṭhe -- svarūpaprakāśe kṛtamajjanamityarthaḥ | jagatkutīsatī pūrṇimā | vāgarthamayavidyāprapaṃce sāṅkhyayogamārgadvayātmake kṛtyapañcakātmake vā | jagati "akuhavisarjanīyānāṃ kaṇṭhaḥ" ityādisvaravarṇotpattisthānaprayatnādyunmeṣapūrvakaṃ vānarthayoḥ, tadīyajñānakarmaṇorvā tattadanukūlasvaravarṇādyāvāpodvāpajñānakriyākṛtyapañcakādyunmeṣakrameṇa, vāgarthādikṛtyapaṃcakasvarūpaṃ, tadīyapūrṇarūpaṃ ca prakāśayantī pūrṇimetyarthaḥ | evaṃ kālakramepi evaṃ pūrṇameva tava rūpam | anyadhākurvatī āvidyakakāmadohanenāpūrṇatayāntarhitaṃ kurvatī | kuhūḥ kila pratīyase | atra pratīyasa ityanena māheśvaranārāyaṇīyasvāyambhuvādibhedabhinnāyāṃ vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇamātṛkārṇamālāyāṃ turīyavimarśe vakṣyamāṇaguptabhāṣitajagadvijṛmbhaṇena hādikādividyayorajhjalādyanulomapratilomakhaṇḍapratyāhārabhedena vidyā'vidyākṛtāhamātmake'kāmāhamātmake cānulomapratyāhārapakṣadvayasvarūpe hakārakakārayorgopanenāmānāmakalāsphuraṇavattatraiva kramapratinivartanena, sakārahakārādiśāktaśāmbhavavisargapūrṇānusvārāntaṃ vilomapratyāhāreṇa vijahatkrame sohamātmake haṃsohamātmake ca vā krameṇa hakāramātragopane someti halbarṇayo rhasakārayordvayorgopane omiti ca pūrṇasomanāmakalāsvarūpapratīteḥ | pūrṇarūpasyaiva pañcadaśakalāmayasvāvayavagopanenānyadhākaraṇe | kaḥ sraṣṭā prajāpatirhūyate saṃhārakriyāmukhena cinmayaprāṇasūryamūrtau, āhūyate vā sṛṣṭyarthamapānasomamaṇḍale anayeti vyutpattyā vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇā, sṛṣṭisaṃhārādikālasaṅkarṣiṇīparyantā, mahākālaśaktiḥ kuhūśabdena pratīyata iti rahasyam | ataeva vaitānikavaidikyāmupāsanāyāṃ dārśikavedīprabhṛtisaumikavedīparyantavedīvāstukalveṣu ca, digdeśakālatripuṭīṣaḍadhvamayeṣviva svaranādavarṇatripuṭīṣaḍadhvamaye, kalāmātṛkāvedīkalpepi "trig‌śatpadāni paścāttiraścī bhavati ṣaṭtrigaṃśatprācī caturvig‌śatiḥ purastāttiraścī daśa daśa sampadyante daśākṣarā virāḍannaṃ virāḍvirājaivānnādyamavarundhe" ityādinā taittirīyasaṃhitāyāṃ ṣaṣṭhakāṇḍe dvitīyaprapāṭhakatṛtīyānuvākoktapratipadavirāṭ‌sampattivadeva prativarṇavirāṭ‌sampattyāpi sakalānnāvarodhanaphalāvāptaye'nuttarākāravisargādyantasthavarṇaparāmarśaparyantaṃ pratīcīnatriṃśadvarṇapadasammitena tiryaksrotasā, soṣmakūṭārṇaparyantaṃ ṣaṭ‌triṃśatāvarṇaiḥ prācīnordhvasrotasā | punaranuttaravisargādisparśānteṣu ṣaḍviṃśativarṇeṣu cādyantavarṇapratyāhāreṇāhamā saṃdaṃśitacaturviṃśativarṇatattvagarbhitenaca, prācīnatiryaksrotasāca yathākramaṃ "vaḥ, vaha, akṣaṃ,;mā, ahamā, vahākṣamahamā" ityādyākāreṇa sāṅketikavaidikasāṅkhyavarṇapratyāhārairantarnimeṣasādāśivātmasampadbodhakaṃ ca mahāvākyamakhaṇḍamupadiśyata ityādiguptibhāṣitajagadvijṛmbhaṇātmasampaduddhāṭanakalāpāṇḍityena vaidikaistāṃtrikai rvā mahopāsakairvimarśakadhaureyaiśca naidavāgarthaṣaḍadhvādivairājātmasampaduddhāṭanamunneyam | vakṣyati ca kavirapi turīye vimarśe "satsudhābdhiśaśabhṛtkalāntarī garbhaṣoḍaśakalābdaviṣṭaram | pretamujhcitagamāgamaṃ śive! smerahṛcchirasijaṃ tvamāśritā" ityādinānuttarākāravisargagarbhitaṣoḍaśasvarakadambake ṣaṭ‌triṃśanmātṛkārṇamayahṛcchirojapaṅkajadvaye svarūpamātraviśrāntāṃ, antarnimeṣayoganidrāpannāṃ, sādāśivapañcamapretamañcaphalakātmikāṃ anuttarākārātmasampadamityādigūḍhābhisandhigarbhitaḥ kiletyavidyāpradhāne dhṛmamārge pārokṣyārthakaḥ | vidyāpradhānārtacirmārge'līkārthaśca nipātaḥ | vastutastu pratyakparāvāgarthajñānakriyāsaṃsārayoḥ dvayorapi vidyāvidyākṛtyapañcakapradhānayoranulomapratilomaprakramākramakālaśaktyoḥ kālasaṅkarṣiṇīrūpatvena bahirantaśca kālapuruṣaśaktisaṃjñāmātrabhedena dṛśyamānatvātkālālīkatābodhakaḥ kileti nipātaḥ | etadeva kālasaṅkarṣiṇyāḥ kālasaṅkarṣiṇītvaṃ yatsvarūpaeva bhedapradarśanaṃ, tathānulomapratilomakṛtyapañcakahetutvapradarśanaṃ ca | tadetatsarvaṃ taittirīyaśākāyāṃ tṛtīyakāṇḍe pañcamapraśne prathamānuvāke darśapūrmamāseṣṭyārambhaprastāve "ya enayoranulomaṃ ca pratilomaṃ ca vidyādityamāvāsyāyā ūrdhvaṃ tadanulomaṃ paurṇamāsyau pratīcīnaṃ tatpratilomaṃ yatpaurṇamāsīṃ pūrvāmālabheta pratilomamenāvālabhetāmubhapakṣīyamāṇamanvapakṣīyeta sāravasvatau homau purastājjuhuyādamāvāsyā vai sarasvatyanulomamevaināvālabhate'mumāpyāyamānamanvāpyāyate" ityādinā pradarśitam | jñānakriyākṛtyapañcakameva parasparaviruddhamiva sthitam | vaidikasāṅkhyayogamārgadvayānubaddha manulomapratilo kṛtyapañcakātmakaṃ ceti vastugatiriti saṃkṣepaḥ || 21 ||
<1-22>
etadeva kālapuruṣatripuṭīśaktidvayātmakamadhvaṣaṭkaṃ pūrvoktaguṇatrayaguṇitaṃ, "aṣṭādaśa nimeṣāstu kāṣṭā triṃśattu tāḥ kalāḥ" ityuktakāṣṭākalāprabhṛti "brāhmaṃ divyaṃ tathā pitryaṃ prājāpatyaṃ gurostathā | sauraṃ ca sāvanaṃ cāndramārkṣaṃ mānāni vai nava" ityuktanavavidhakālamānātmikakālapuruṣamahāmānaśrīcakrepi pūrvaślokasūcitapitṛmānaprabhṛti divyaprājāpatyabrāhmanāmakaśaivacatuṣkoṇātmanā caturvidhapāramārthikakālamānagaṇitasāmarasyenāpi karmopāsanopayogitayā vidyā'vidyākramātmakaṃ "sargasaṃhṛtiśarīrayordvayoḥ" ityādinā vakṣyamāmasṛṣṭisaṃhārādyarthakramabhedamupadarśayan sūtraprāyamādisaṃvimarśārthamupasaṃharati -- 
pūrṇatākṛośatayoryadantaraṃ
tatra kāli vijatkrame sthitā |
darśitakramavibhāgasambhramaṃ
kālamadyatanamattu mahī(se)te || 22 ||
iti śrīmahākavi kālidāsakṛtau cidgaganacandrikāyāṃ, prathamavimarśaḥ samāptaḥ ||
pūrṇatākṛśatayorityādinā -- pūrṇatākṛśatayoryadantaraṃ pūrṇāvasthākṛśāvasthayoḥ, pūrṇarūpatvakṛśarūpatvayośca yo madhyabhāgaḥ | vidyā'vidyāvasthayoḥ yauvanavārdhakyaprādurbhāvarūpaḥ puruṣadaśāsandhitulyaḥ kāladaśāsandhirityarthaḥ | kālakrame -- śuklakṛṣṇāṣṭamīpradoṣātmakakālasaṃdhibhāgaśca | vijahatkrame -- vijahat kramaḥ prakāśasṛṣṭikramaḥ vimarśasaṃhārakramaśca yasmin vidyāvasthāyauvanaprādurbhāvavināśānyatare kāle, avidyāvasthāvārdhakyaprādurbhāvavināśānyatare vā | tathā kālakramepi -- vijahat candrikāmayasṛṣṭiprakāśakramaḥ tamisrāmayasaṃhārakramaśca yasmin rākākuhūnāmakapūrṇimā'māvāsyātithiprādurbhāvasaṃdhikāle ca tasmin | vidyāyauvanaprādurbhāvā'vidyāyauvanavināśānyatarāvasthātulyaśuklāṣṭamīsaṃdhibhāge, avidyāyauvanaprādurbhāvavidyāyauvanavināśatulyakṛṣṇāṣṭamīsaṃdhibhāge ca tadubhayapūrṇarākāpūrṇimākuhūnāmakā'mātithyośca krameṇayathādhikāraṃyathāyogyaṃca viṣaye adhikaraṇe ca sthitā satī | tvaṃ | darśitakramavibhāgasambhramaṃ -- darśitaḥ prakāśitaḥ yaḥ kramavibhāgaḥ vidyāvidyāsṛṣṭisaṃhārakramavibhāgaḥ tasya sambhramaśca yasminvidyāsṛṣṭisaṃhārasaṃdhikāle, avidyāsaṃhārasṛṣṭikāleyā taṃ darśitakramavibhāgasambhramaṃ | tathāvidhamadyatanaṃ adyate bhakṣyate vidyāvidyādisaṃsāraḥ tanyate ceti vyutpattyā | asampūrṇavidyāvidyāsṛṣṭisaṃhārādyātmakaṃ vyāvahārikaṃ kālamityarthaḥ | attuṃ bhakṣituṃ īhase icchasi | kālapade darśitakramavibhāgasambhramaṃ -- darśitaḥ prakāśitaḥ, "darśā dṛṣṭā darśatā viśvarūpā sudarśanā" ityādikāṭhakaśrutyā sañjātadarśo vā kṛtaḥ | kramavibhāgaḥ pitryadivyādirātryaharmukhasandhirūpaḥ | śuklāṣṭamīmārabhya kṛṣṇāṣṭhamīparyantaṃ, tadādi punaḥ śuklāṣṭamīparyantaṃ ca prakāśitaḥ yaḥ pitryarātryahassandhikālarūpaḥ divyāhorātrasandhikālarūpo vā kramavibhāgasya sambhramo yasmintamadyatanaṃ kālaṃ śuklakṛṣṇapakṣātmakaṃ tattatpakṣasandhirūpamātyantikatayā bhakṣaṇayogyaṃ divyaprājāpatyabrāhmādimānasiddhaṃ ca kālamattuṃ bhakṣitumīhasa iti ca | īhata iti pāṭhepūrṇatākṛśatayorantarameva kartṛpadaṃ jñeyam | śuklāṣṭhamyāḥ "aṣṭamīcandravibhrājadalikasthalaśobhitā" ityuktasṛṣṭikālīrūpiṇyāśśuddhavidyātripurasundaryāḥ mukhāvayavacandrādhiṣṭhānakakālāntarālarūpatvāt, tathā kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ api pitṛvyavahārārambhasūryodayakālatvena sūryodayena śārvaratamovināśavatkṛṣṇāṣṭamyārambhe śuklapakṣīyapaitṛkatamovināśaśya kṛtatvena niṣpratyūhaṃ saṃhārakālīvibhūtimayaśrāddhādipaitṛkavyavahārasya sampravṛttatvācca pūrṇatākṛśatayorantarālarūpatvena kartṛtvaṃ pitṛrātryahassandhirūpayoḥ śuklakṛṣṇāṣṭamyoriti bhāvaḥ | taduktaṃ mātsye mānuṣapitryādikālamānagaṇanāprastāve -- "ahorātrairvibhajate sūryo mānuṣalaukikaḥ | ratrissvapnāya bhūtānāṃ ceṣṭāyai karmaṇāmahaḥ | pitrye rātryahanī māsaḥ pravibhāgastayoḥ punaḥ || kṛṣṇapakṣastvahasteṣāṃ śuklassvapnāya śarvarī | triṃśat dve mānuṣā māsāḥ pitṛmāsassa ucyate || śatāni trīṇi māsānāṃ ṣaṣṭyāvāmyadhikāni tu | pitryassaṃvatsarohyeṣa mānuṣeṇa vibhāvyate || mānuṣeṇaiva mānena varṣāṇāṃ yacchataṃ bhavet | pitṝṇāṃ tāni varṣāṇi saṅkhyātāni tu trīṇi vai || daśa caivādhikā māsaḥ pitṛsaṅkhyeha kīrtitā" ityādinā brāhmamānāntakālamānagaṇanāsaṃkṣepeṇa darśitā tatraiva draṣṭavyā | ataeva kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāḥ pañcaparvasu gaṇanaṃ darśe śrāddhavidhānaṃ cetyādi karmopāsanākaṇḍoktaṃ sarvaṃ sapapattikamasminnevārthe | evameva pūrṇāpūrṇadināntaraguṇitakālamānasaṅkhyāśrayeṇa "ekaṃ vā etaddevānāmahaḥ | yatsaṃvatsaraḥ" ityāśvamedhikaśrutyuktalakṣaṇe devamāne "mahāyugo vāstavasauraravarṣaḥ" ityuktavāstavasauramānaparaparyāyaprājāpatyamāne "caturyugasahasrāṇi brahmaṇo dinamucyate" ityuktabrāhmakalpamāne cāyameva ślokaḥ krameṇonmeṣanimiṣotpannavipannabhuvanāvalirūpaparaśivaśakti mithunātmakaparavāsudevamānaparyantatayā khagolakālaparibhramaṇatāratamyaparijñānena paramātmatatve samanvetavyaḥ | tattu granthavistarabhayādviramyata iti śivam ||
|| sūtraprāye'tra purato vimarśe prathame budhaiḥ | cidagnicandrikā bhāṣyā vyākhyā divyacakorikā || dhanadānandanādhena kṛtā sarvārthasiddhidā | viceyā svamahāmātṛpramātṛyugalātmatā || 2|| ye pūraṇātpuruṣatā mupasedivāṃsaḥ te gauravīṃ samupasadya sabha (rpya) rcya mūrtam | prauḍhāntarullasanadivyadaśāvalokamudghāṭayantu yadi māṃ sudidṛkṣavaḥ syuḥ || 3 || yepyāṇavādimalaśuddhimavāpuruccairye māntravīryavibhutāṃ kalidāsatāṃ vā | prauḍhāntarollasanadivyacakorikāṃ cedālokayanti ta imāṃ sudidṛkṣavaḥ syuḥ || 4 || dhanadānandanādhīyaṃ navadhā'nyavivarjitam | svātmīyaṃ dhanimityeva sarvaissamupa(yu) bhujyatām || 5 || pāścātyānāmivāṅkanāṃ gatissā vāmato matā | akṣarāṇāmivāsmākaṃ prācīnā dakṣiṇā gatiḥ || 6 || savyāpasavyamārgasthau prākpratyagdigvivāsinau | savo yāga iti proktaḥ prācīnā jīvikāhi sā || 7 || apasa (śa) vyo hyayajñaḥ syātsaiva pāścātyajīvikā | vaidiko dakṣiṇo mārgo vāmamārgastu yāvanaḥ || 8 || kaulassyāttatsāmarasyaṃ kuladharmo hi dakṣiṇaḥ | tasmātsvadharmaniṣṭhānāṃ bhuktimuktī karasthite || 9 || suguptametadrahasi śrutīnāṃ mūrdhasu sthitam | tadetatsarvasiddhāntasāmarasyaṃ kavermatam || 10 || etaddhikālikāstotrachadmanā hṛdayaṃ kaveḥ | vivrīyate mayāpīha taptaṃ paraśumālabhe || 11 || ityādime sūtrasame pratijñāpi tathāvidhā | pramāṇamatra tatsūtra srotassvacchandacāriṇaḥ || 22 ||
|| iti śrīdhanadānandanādhadīkṣānāmaśālikarrānvayāgnihotraśarmakṛtau cidgagana candrikāvyākhyāne divyacakorikākhyāne sūtraprāya ādimo vimarśaḥ | śrīkāmākṣyambānandānandanādhaparaśivaśakticaraṇayugalārpaṇamastu || śrī śrī śrī ||

"divyacakorikayā" samalaṃkṛtā |
dvitīyavimarśaḥ ||
<2-1-23>
evaṃ sūtraprāyaprathamavimarśagranthasandarbheṇa nādanādhanavakasamaṣṭyātmakavāgarthasvarūpasya sāṅkhyayogamārgadvayasāmarasyapūrvakaṃ kālaśaktyabhedenaikyaṃ sampādya, tatra "yāhamityuditavāk" ityādinā pūrvoktā''hatānāhatavāgarthasṛṣṭyādikṛtyapañcakakramopayuktavarṇapadamantrātmakaśābdādhvatrikasya kalātattvabhuvanātmakārthādhvatrikasya ca mūlabhūte, baindavavaisargikavakṣyamāṇaṣaḍvidhaparāmarśātmakaparāparasthūlasūkṣmanādabrahmatattvavivartabhūte, ṣoḍaśakālaṣoḍaśasvarakhaṇ‍ḍātmakaparā parāvidyāsāmarasyamayaviśvavidyāśarīre śāktānuttaravisargavivartanāmakalāṃ, śāmbhavānuttaravisargarūpakalāṃ ca maṇḍalatrayābhinnabindutrayakāmakalātmakatayā avaśiṣṭhacaturdaśarūpasamāṣṭikalāmayīṃ, caturdaśamānavasvaravibhūtiṃ māheśvaracaturdaśasūtrādicaturdaśasaṅkhyākāvidyābhuvanādivāgarthavivartatayā pariṇatāṃ cābhisandhāya tatrāpi "dve vidye veditavye parā cā'parā ca" ityuktā'parāvidyāprapañce ṣaḍvidhabauddhadarśaneṣvatisthūlacārvākakṣapaṇakamādhyamikādyavaidikamatāni prasaṅgenāgre nirākariṣyannādau vaidikarāmānujapūrṇaprajñādyarthādhvabhedadarśaneṣvapi mātṛbhānameyātmakatripuṭīvikalpabuddherarthātmani ātmārthe vā vāṅmātrakalpitatayā tayā svasvarūpanādamayīṃ vāgdevatāmakalaṅkitatvavimarśena svārthaprapaṃcaṃ sākṣātkurvatopi viduṣo nityamuktatāmādau pratijānīte -- mātṛmeyetyādiślokadvayena -- 
mātṛmeyamitisādhanātmikā
tvatkṛtonmiṣati yā vikalpadhīḥ |
tvatsvarūpamakalaṃkitaṃ tayā
kasya devi! viduṣo na muktatā || 23 ||
mātṛmeyeti | hedevi! "div‌krīḍā vijigīṣādyutivyavahārastutimodamadasvapnakāntigatiṣu" iti dhātupāṭhāt krīḍādiprayojakavāgartharūpiṇīti sābhiprāyasambhuddhiḥ | mātṛmeyamitisādhanātmikā -- mātṛsādhanamantākaraṇam | meyasādhanāni karmajñānendriyagolakāni sthūlāni | mitisāsvarūpaṃ yasyā vikalpadhiyaḥ sā tathoktā | teṣāmātmārūpiṇī vā | yā vikalpadhīḥ vilakṣaṇakalpadhīrvā vigatakalpadhīrvā | "rogai śca kṣayito dehaḥ kalpa ityabhidhīyate" ityuktadehātmabhedabuddhiriti yāvat | tvatkṛtā vāgartharūpiṇyāṃ tvayyeva tvayā nirmitā | unmiṣati vahirapi prakāśate | tayā vikalpadhiyā | tvatsvarūpaṃ vāgarthamahāsaratvatyāstava svaranādayugalasvarūpaṃ prākṛtaṃ svarūpamayamiti bhāvaḥ | akalaṅkitamiti viduṣaḥ vedituḥ | kasya siddhasya sādhakasyaveti bhāvaḥ | akalaṅkitamiti viduṣaḥ vedituḥ | kasya siddhasya sādhakasyaveti bhāvaḥ | na muktatāḥ? pramātrāditripuṭīsādhanātmikā bhedabuddhiḥ vāgarthakalākālaśaktikalpitaivonmiṣati | kalpitatvādevāviṣṭhānāsparśinīca | tasmādasaṅgacitsvabhāvaṃ mahāparānandanādanādhātmakamātmatattvaṃ na kalaṅkayatīti vidvānvimarśako vā sarvopi nityamuktaeveti bhāvaḥ | etena mātṛpramātṛtripuṭyorvyāvahārikapāramārthikabhedaḥ mātṛtripuṭyāḥ pramāṇāṃśagarbhitatvaṃ ca vivakṣitamiti bodhyam || 1-23 ||
<2-2-24>
evaṃ prākṛtatripuṭīvikalpabuddherapi prākṛtasvarūpākalaṅkakāritāṃ pratijñāya tatsvarūpavimarśakasyāpi tadubhayamadhyakendrasthityā vidyā'vidyākuladharmamukhyapīṭhabhūtāyāḥ tripuṭīvikalpabuddheḥ vimarśaprakāraṃ lakṣayati -- 
akramakramavimarśalakṣaṇaṃ
yā kramākramamayī kramākramam |
akramaṃ śivamavekṣya madhyagaṃ
tvāṃ ca satkulamidaṃ tavā(na)sanam || 24 ||
akrametyādinā | yā kramākramamayī -- kramaḥ vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇaḥ kādimatasiddho varṇamātṛkārṇalakṣaṇaḥ | akramaḥ hādividyāmatasiddhaḥ svaranādamātṛkākrama iti | tadubhayamayīti mātṛkāśābdavikalpabuddheḥ viśeṣaṇam | arthamātṛkāvikalpabuddhipakṣe tu -- "gurvāyattaṃ kramajñānamājñāsiddhikaraṃ param | kramajñānānmahādevi! trailokyaṃ kabalīkṛtam || kakāraḥ krodharūpastu makāro maṅgalapradaḥ | krodhe tu maṅgalaṃ kuryātkramaḥ kālakramo bhavet|| " iti tantrāntaroktalakṣaṇagurumukhaikavedyakramākramamayī vikalpabuddhirityarthaḥ | akramakramavimarśalakṣaṇaṃ -- akramavimarśaḥ vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇasvaranādamātṛkāyāḥ sṛṣṭilakṣaṇaḥ | kramavimarśaḥ varṇamātṛkāyā nādasaṃhāralakṣaṇaśca | tadubhayalakṣaṇaṃ | tathaiva kramākramaṃ pūrvoktavarmasvaranādamātṛkāvāgarthakramākramalakṣaṇam | ataevākramaṃ -- na kramate'pratihatagatyā na prāpnotītyakramam | ataeva madhyagaṃ śivajīvasaṃsāramadhyagatam | śivaṃ sādhakaśivam | tvāṃ tṛthāvidhāmeva tvāṃ ca | satkula -- satāṃ vyavahārasatyarūpāṇāṃ mātṛmānameyatripuṭīnākṣatrakiraṇānāṃ kulam | yadvā, "trivṛdvai jyaiṣṭhyaṃ | mātā pitī putraḥ | trivṛtprajananaṃ | upastho yonirmadhyamā | prayaśo jyaiṣṭhyamāpnoti" iti nāciketāgnicayanabrahmavidyāprakaraṇe śrutyuktatrivṛjjagatsṛṣṭyādikāraṇasvasvarūpajyeṣṭhāṃśānāṃ vā, brahmakṣatravaiśyaśabditatraivarṇikabhāvaśarīrāṇāṃ vā, dravyaguṇakriyātmamā jātikriyādravyarūpeṇa vā ghanībhūtasaccidānandāṃśānāṃ vā śāktāditrividhavisargāṇāṃ kulaṃ satkulam | sajātīyasamūhātmakamityarthaḥ | taduktaṃ tantre -- "sajātīyaṃ kulaṃ smṛtam " iti | idaṃ idamaṃśasphuraṇātmakam | tava vidyā'vidyā'bhinnacidacinmayaprakṛtyāstava | ānananaṃ paṃcendriyasaptaśīrṣaṇyaprāṇamayaṃ vakṣyamāṇapañcavāhasaptasrotasphuraṇātmanā tvadīyamukharūpaṃ, ca | yadvā, mānavaprajāpatyabrāhmaughāparaparyāyagurupaṃktisphuraṇarūpeṇa divyasiddhaughāditripuṭaurūpaṃ vā tava mukham | tavāsanamiti pāṭhe -- tvadīyapīṭharūpaṃ cetyarthaḥ | avekṣya tvatkṛtā yā vikalpabuddhiḥ tvatsvarūpaṃ na kalaṅkayati | tadviduṣo vimarśakasya vā | kasya na muktateti pūrvaślokenānvayaḥ || 2-24 ||
<2-3-25>
uktaprakāreṇa yadyapi nityamuktaevāhamapi tathāpi māyīyamalaprayuktā''ṇavamalasyoktānandānubhūtipratibandhakatvāttannirāsamātraṃ tvadadhīnamiti tvāṃ prādhayamītyāha -- 
vedyavedakavibhedadakṣayā
māyayā kramavadaṃgagāminī |
yā sthitāmba bahirakṣakāṃkṣiṇī
tāṃ jahi tvamaṇutāṃ mayi sthitām || 25 || vedyavedaketyādinā | he ambeti sambuddhyarthaḥ pūrvavat | vedyavedakavibhedadakṣayā -- vedyavedakayoḥ ciccaityayoḥ caityacitorvā yo vibhedaḥ arūpe sarūpe vā gandharvanagarasvaśiraśchedanadarśanādivat rūparūpāntarakalpanādivilakṣaṇabhedarūpaḥ | tasmin dakṣayā samarthayā | māyayā -- mīyate paricchidyate svenaiva svasvarūpamiti māyeti vyutpattisiddhabhedaprathā vāgartharūpayā | kramavadaṅgamāminī pūrvoktasṛṣṭisaṃhārānyatarakramavat yadaṅgaṃ vāgarthaśarīraṃ tadgāminī | ataeva bahirakṣakāṃkṣiṇī -- bahirmikhavāgakṣaṃ mātṛkārṇatatpratyāhārasiddhārthasammelanātmakaṃ ca bahirakṣaṃ | kāṃkṣata iti bahirakṣakāṃkṣiṇī | yā aṇutā vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇadvividhāṇavamalarūpā kiṃcidjñatāhetubhūtā jīvatā | mayi sthitā ahamarthapratyayarūpe ātmani sthitā | tāṃ tvaṃ jahi | madīyāṇavamalātmakaśiraśchedena māṃ sadāśivamañcaphalakātmakaṃ pañcamapretaṃ kurviti tātparyārthaḥ | vakṣyati cāgrepi "pretamujjhitagamāgamaṃ śive" ityādinā sadāśivātmakapañcamamañcapretasyācalarūpatāṃ | dvividhāṇavamalasvarūpaṃ tantrasāre trikaśāstrasya sarvottamatvapratiṣṭhāpanāvasare "svātantryahānirbodhasya svātantryasyāpyabodhatā | dvidhāṇavaṃ malaṃ proktaṃ svasvarūpāpahānitaḥ" iti || 2-3-25 ||
<2-4-26>
evaṃ jīvatvarūpāṇavamūlāpakarṣaṇottaramārabdhāditrividhakartavilāpanarūpakṛtārthatā tvadīyavṛttibhireva siddhatvādanupāyasthitissahajasiddheti sahajāvasthāmanusandhatte -- karmetyādinā ||
karma yadvividhavāsanātmakaṃ
māyayā sahakṛtāṇubhāvayā |
vṛttibhistava vilāpyate hi ta
ddevi bhānurucībhiryathā (tamaḥ) himam || 26 ||
he devi! sahakṛtānubhāvayā | uktajīvabhāvasahakṛtayā māyayā | vedyabhedaprathātmikayā | vividhavāsanātmakaṃ śubhāśubhavāsanātmakaṃ yatkarmakāryata iti śeṣaḥ | tatkarma tava vṛttibhiḥ bhānurucibhistamo yathā tama iva vilāpyate hi | nāśyate khalu | atra vedyabhedaprathātmikāyā māyāyā ssahakṛtāṇubhāvatvaviśeṣaṇena āṇavāvaraṇaśaktiṃ vinā vitrepātmikāyāstasyā akiñcitkaratvāttadapekṣayā''varaṇaśaktyātmakāṇutāyā eva saṃsṛtihetutvācca tadubhayāṇavamalanirāsakatvadīyavṛttisāntatyena bhānurucibhirandhatam iva kārmaṇamalavilāpanaṃ sahajasiddhameveti hīti prasiddhidyotakanipātārthaḥ | yadvā, hīti hetuvācako vā | hi yasmāt vividhavāsanātmakakarmasu tattatkarmavāsānāvilāpanamukhena tava vṛttibhiḥ sarvasambhuktayoṣiccāritrapālanavat bhānurucibhiḥ tattatkālavṛttyanurodhena tama iva svayameva naṣṭaṃ vāsanātmakaṃ kārmaṇaṃ malaṃ tvadīyavṛttivilāpitamiva bhavati | tasmānmayi sthitā maṇutāṃ jahīti pūrvaślokenavā sambandhaḥ | tathācoktaṃ mālinīvijayepi `tasmātkālo na bodhasya bhedakatvāya kalpate | nāpi vedyasya kālosau bhedakībhavituṃ kṣamaḥ' ityādinā kālasya vedyavedakabhedakatvaṃ nirasya "tasmādarkasya sadbhāve siddhe kaḥ khalu bāliśaḥ | brūyātkathamayaṃ svāṃśuśubhritāśeṣabhūriti || tasmātsiddhe prakāśesminyāḥ prakāśavikalpanāḥ | tasmāttāḥ sarvasambhuktayoṣiccāritrapālanāḥ | asiddhau ca prakāśasya kohaṃ kiṃ tvaṃ tamopi kiṃ | na kiṃcidapi vā kiṃ syāttūṣṇīṃ syādapi vā katham || tasmātprakāśatādātmyalabdhabhairavabhāginām | bhāvānāmapi kāloyaṃ na kiṃcitkartumarhati || ityādinā ātmavṛttiprakāśatādātmyalabdhabhairavabhāgināṃ sahajabhinnabhāvānāmapi bhedena sarvasvatantrasyāpi kālaśivabhaṭṭārakasyākiṃcitkaratvaṃ kaṇṭhata uddhoṣitam | kānāma kathā varākyāssahakṛtāṇubhāvāyā māyāyā iti | tamovināśe bhānurucīnāmiva tvadīyavṛttīnāṃ mahimā manovāgādyaviṣaye vartata iti bhāvaḥ | himamiti pāṭhe -- bhānurucibhiḥ himamiva vṛttibhiḥ karma vilāpyata ityanvayaḥ || 4-26 ||
<2-5-27>
evamārabdhādikarmatrayanāmakamalatrayavilāpe tvatsvarūpasākṣātkāralābhopītyāha -- 
cinmayīkṛtamatokṣadṛśyayā
guhyayāmba! viparītacaryayā |
labhyate samarasīkṛtākhila
dvaitavṛtti tava ghasmaraṃ vapuḥ || 27 ||
cinmayītyādinā | he amba! ataḥ kārmaṇamalavilāpanāt āṇavamalahāpanādvā | guhyayā hṛdguhāspandamātraśarīrayā | ataeva "guhūsaṃvaraṇe" iti dhātossaṃvṛtayathārtharūpatvena rahasyabhūtayā vā | tathāpyakṣadṛśyayā | ādikṣāntaśābdaśarīreṇa dagdhapaṭhākṛtikārthakṣaśarīreṇa ca dṛśyamānaśarīrayā | tādātvikadehendriyādipravṛttimātrarūpatvenāhantābhiniveśūnyayeti yāvat | ataeva viparītacaryayā lokavilakṣaṇatvena vikṛtakriyārūpayā | samarasīkṛtākhiladvaitavṛtti | samarasīkṛtāḥ sāmarasyaṃ prāpitāḥ akhiladvaitavṛttayaḥ sukhaduḥkhalābhālābhādisakaladvandvavṛttayaḥ yasmin tattādṛśam | tava vāgartharūpiṇyāḥ | ghasmaraṃ prakāśaikasvabhāvatayā sarvabhakṣakam | vapuḥ upyate sṛṣṭisaṃhārādivāgarthabījamasminniti vapuriti vyutpattyā nādavāgarthatripuṭīṣaḍadhvādyātmakaṃ dāharaṃ vyomamayaṃ śarīram | cinmayīkṛtam | pūrvamacinmayamidānīṃ cinmayaṃ sampadyamānaṃ kṛtaṃ cinmayīkṛtaṃ sat | ityabhūtatadbhāve ccipratyayaḥ | labhyate "tāṃ jahi tvamaṇutāṃ mayi sthitām" iti pūrvatamaślokoktāsmālliṅgāt | sādhakātmanā mayā madabhinnena vā sādhakeneti śeṣaḥ | tena saṃsargasvarūpādhyāsarūpayossādhakāhamorgauṇamukhyavāgarthātmaikarūpatvāttadaikyasiddhilābhastvarthākṣipta eveti bhāvaḥ | atra cinmayīkṛtaṃ samarasokṛtākhiladvaitavṛttīti ca cvipratyayāntaviśeṣaṇadvayena prākṛtadehendriyādisaṃghātopi kālakrameṇa sāmarasyopāstibalāccinmayasampadyata ityanusaramahārthaprameyakrame pramātrahamakṣatatparoyaṃ vimarśa iti vimarśaprameyamapi sūcitamiti veditavyam || 5-27 ||
<2-6-28>
tato'saṅgitāpi labhyata evetyāha -- 
yāścaranti tava khe cidātmake
śaktayaḥ karaṇalakṣaṇāśśive! |
muktabāhyapadajṛmbhaṇodyamā
tvaṃ hi tiṣṭhasi tadūrdhvage pathi || 28 ||
yāścarantītyādinā | śive sādhakaśivasvarūpe | hesarvamaṅgalecchārūpiṇīti sambuddhirvā | tava cidātmake | khe cinmaye daharākāśe | karaṇalakṣaṇāḥ | vyāpāravadasādhāraṇakāraṇaṃ karaṇamityuktabahirantaḥkaraṇalakṣaṇāḥ | prāṇavākśrotrādibahirantaḥkaraṇarūpā ityarthaḥ | yāśśaktayaścaranti saṃcaranti | carabhakṣaṇa iti dhātorannapānāditanmātrabhogānbhuñjate cetyarthaḥ | tvaṃ sarvaśaktisamaṣṭivyomeśvarīrūpiṇī tvamityarthaḥ | tadūrdhvage pathi | tāsāmantarbāhyakaraṇalakṣaṇaśaktīnāmūrdhvagate nādavāgarthaṣaḍadhvamārge ityarthaḥ | muktabāhyapadajṛmbhaṇodyamā | mukta bāhyapadānāṃ jāgradādisuṣuptyantānāṃ jṛmbhaṇasyodyamaḥ yayā sā tathoktāsatī tiṣṭhasi | hīti sammatidyotako nipātaḥ | prakṛterbāhyapadajṛmbhaṇaṃ svabhāvaḥ | saca prākṛtasaṃsārasaṅgitārūpaḥ | tasyāpi tvatsammatyā muktatvādasaṅgitālābha iti bhāvaḥ | yadvā muktānāṃ videhakevalināṃ bāhyapadasya turīyāvasthārūpasya jṛmbhaṇamātrodyamaḥ yasyāṃ nivṛttikalāmayyāṃ prakṛtyāṃ tādṛśīprakṛtissatī tiṣṭhasi hīti vā'rthaḥ | ubhayadhāpyasaṅgiteti bhāvaḥ | tathā ca saundaryalaharyāṃ śrīśaṅkarabhagavatpūjyapādāḥ "kṣitau ṣaṭpaṃcāśat‌dvisamadhikapañcāśadudake, hutāśe dvāṣaṣṭhiścaturadhikapañcāśadanile | divi dviṣṣaṭ‌triṃśanmanasi ca catuṣṣaṣṭhiriti ye, mayūkhāsteṣāmapyupari
tava pādāmbujayugam " iti || 6-28 ||
<2-7-29>
itaḥparaṃ pratīyamānadvaitavṛttīnāmasaṅgātmānandāvāptisahāyateti tṛtīyavimarśavakṣyamāṇadhāmādikramatrayepi saptasaptaprakāraṃ prākṛtavamanaṃ guptibhāṣitajagadvijṛmbhaṇena sūcayati -- 
saptadhā vamasi yā tvamīśvarī!
vyomaśāntamukhacidguṇāspadam |
āśritānnayasi nā'paraṃ śivaṃ
dvandvayādvayapadātilaṃghinī || 29 ||
saptatheti | heīśvarīti sābhiprāyasambuddhiḥ | yā tvaṃ vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇaṣaḍvidhaparāmarśātmikā tvamityarthaḥ | śāntamukhacid‌guṇāspadaṃ -- śāntamukhā yā cit, yeca tadguṇāḥ sarvajñatvādayaḥ "aiśvaryasya samagrasya vīryasya yaśasaḥ śriyaḥ | jñānavairāgyayoścaiva ṣaṇṇāṃ vargo bhagasstṛtaḥ || tathā -- utpatti ca vināśaṃ ca bhūtānāmagatiṃ gatim | vetti vidyāmavidyāṃ ca sa vācyo bhagavāniti" ityuktalakṣaṇāścetyanuttaraprāṇanādabindukalāṣaḍadhvavartmani | sarvajñatvādayaḥ, śamādayaḥ, kāmakrodhādayaśceti tadīyasaṃvitṣaḍadhvamārge vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇavarṇaparāmarśaṣaṭkātmakāssaṃhitādiṣu sandhivigrahayānāsanadvaidhībhāvasamāśrayāparanāmakaśābdavāgarthakāryātmakāśceti mātṛkārṇaṣaḍadhve ca santi, teṣāṃ sarveṣāmāspadaṃ | vyoma | saptadhā vamasi | tattatṣaḍadhvatripuṭīsamaṣṭiprakārabhedena | cidvyomadhāmani manobuddhiviśiṣṭapañcendriyavṛttimayasaptasrotaḥ prakāreṇa | tadīyasaṃvidi nāde ca krameṇa, ārambhataruṇayauvanaprauḍhatadantonmanānavasthākhyollāsasaptakaprakāreṇa, ṣaḍjādiniṣādāntagāndharvavedoktasvarasaptakaprakāreṇa ca, tadīyānuttarākārabindvartha candrarodhinyādivakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇonmanīnādanavakakramepi sū || "a i uṇ" ityādi "nanādaṃ ḍhakkāṃ pañcavāraṃ" ityuktanādalakṣaṇamāheśvaramātṛkārṇasṛṣṭyādikrameṇonuttarākārādripraṇavāntanādavarṇaprakāreṇa ca | tattadīyabhidheyārthaṣaḍadhvakramepi "saptaprāṇāḥ prabhavanti tasmātsaptārciṣassamidhassaptajihvāḥ | sapta ime lokā yeṣu caranti prāṇā guhāśayānnihitāssapta sapta | atassamudrā girayaśca sarve'smātsyandante sindhavassarvarūpāḥ | ataśca viśvā oṣadhayo rasācca yenaiṣa bhūtastiṣṭhatyantarātmā" "sapta te agne samidhassaptajihvāssaptarṣayassaptadhāmapriyāṇi" ityāditaittirīyayājñakīmahānārāyaṇīupaniṣanmantrādisakalaśrutiprakāśitasaptavyāhṛtiprakṛtivikṛtivarṇavargaprabhṛtilokālokāntayāvatsṛṣṭyādihetutvena sāṃkhyaśāstraprapañcitasapkasrotomayātmavibhūtiprakāreṇa ca | vyomaiva vividhākṛtikāṃ prāṇavānuttarākāraprakṛtiṃ ca vamasītyarthaḥ | tathāca sāṃkhyasūtram "mūlaprakṛtiravikṛtiḥ mahadādyāḥ prakṛtivakṛtayassapta| ṣoḍaśakaśca vikāraḥ na prakṛtirna vikṛtiḥ puruṣaḥ" iti | sāṃkhyapraṇavarūpiṇī mūlaprakṛtiḥ | vaidikavāgarthabhedavivakṣayānuttarākāravikṛtirūpeṇa saptamī aṣṭamī cāpi bhavatītyavikṛtiḥ | vaidikī tu vāgarthaikarūpatvādvikṛtirāhityenāvikṛtirnavamīti rahasyam | ataeva navamī daśamī ekādaśī dvādaśī trayodaśī ca tattanmātṛkārṇasamāmnāyabhedādbhavatītyapīti vaidikameva rahasyāntaram "itthaṃ yā tvaṃ vyoma vamasi | sā tvaṃ | dvandvayā vṛtyā dvaitavṛtyā | nā nṛrūpiṇī | guptibhāṣitajagadvijṛmbhamavyavahāre manuṣyavyavahāreṇa vyavahṛtāsītyarthaḥ | ataevādvayapadātilaṃghinī | advayapadaṃ turīyapadamapyatītya laṅghate vedyabhedaṃ prati bhogānnivartate turīyātītamārgeṇetyadvayapadātilaṅghinī | yadvā "laghi bhojananivṛttāviti dhātoḥ" `anaśnannanyo abhicākaśīti' iti śruteśca | dvaitapadaṃ praviśyāpīśvaraśaktyabhedena bhogānnivṛttitatpareti vā'nvayaḥ | anuttaranādamātṛkārṇakramepi sṛṣṭyādau advayam arthaikadvimātrātmakamakāradvayam | tadīyapadañca bindutrayātmakam | atītya plutaraṅgādisvaranādakrameṇātikramya laṅghata ityadvayapadātilaṅghinī ca | satī | āśritān saṃjātāśrayān | anuttarākāraśritāṃśca sādhakān | paraṃ śivaṃ | ātmabhedeneva parokṣatayā vidyamānaṃ jīveśvarānyatararūpaṃ ca | nayasi prāpayasīti nayatordvikarmakatvaṃ | yadvā paraṃ śivaṃ | āśritān sādhakān | pradhānāpradhānakarmavyatihāreṇa | paraṃ jīvaṃ | jīvaṃ ca paramīśvaraṃ vā yatheccham | nayasi prāpayasīti vā | tathaiva āśritān tvadāśritāṃ śca | siddhān | aparaṃ śivaṃ na nayasi | kintvātmarūpameva śivaṃ prāpayasi ca | ityāvṛtyā padabhedena vā samanvayabhedaśca | tadānīṃ dvandyāpi vṛtyā ātmāvāptikaraṇabhūtayā | dvayapadātilaṅghinī -- dvayapadāni pūrvoktajāgradādīni sṛṣṭyādīni vā | svaviruddhasuptisaṃhārādidvandvabhāvena dvayapadāni tāni atilaṅghayatyāśritāniti garbhitaṇijantavigraheṇa dvayapadātilaṅghinīti samāso vaktavyaḥ | "mahīmūṣu mātarag subratānāmṛtastha patnī mavase huvema | tu vikṣatrāmajarantīmurūcīgaṃ suśarmāṇamadi tig supraṇītim" iti mantroktadevataraṇi devamātṛrūpiṇī satī siddhānātmaśivameva prāpayasītyarthopi veditavyaḥ || 7-29 ||
<2-8-30>
evamuktalakṣaṇāṃ tvāṃ "catvāri vākparimitā padāni" iti śrutyuktaparādipadacatuṣṭhayasamaṣṭirūpayāpi vācā mukhyayā vṛtyā nirṇīya vaktuṃ na pāryate kimuta vaikharyetyāśayenāha -- 
yā pramātari niṣeduṣī parā
pratyagātmani vimarśavigrahā |
vāgiyaṃ trividhabhāvabhāsitā
tvāṃ (na) sa vakti kimutāmba! vaikharī || 30 ||
yā māpratarītyādinā | yā parānāmnī vāk | pramātari ātmani jyeṣṭhāyāmambāyāṃ ca | pramāṃ yathārthavṛttimatati vyāpnotīti vyutpattyā vyāpakātmani | triṣuṭīkalpanādhiṣṭhānātmanītyarthaḥ | vimarśavigrahā | vimarśamayaviśeṣajñānarūpiṇīsatī | niṣeduṣī | sā pratyak | pratyagātmetyarthaḥ | trividhabhāvaiḥ paśyantīmadhyamāvaikharīnāmakatriprakārakadharmaiḥ | pramātṛpramāṇaprameyarūpairvā | bhāsitā | iyaṃ vāk tvāṃ sa iti pratyagātmaiveti vakti | he amba! vaikharī kimuta | ekabhāvabhāsitā vaikharī tvāṃ pratyagātmeti vaktīti kimu vaktavyam | mahāpramātari pratyagṝpeṇa vartamānā parāvāgapi paśyantyāditrividhabhāvairbhāsitāpi mahākālīrūpiṇīmātmānaṃ svasvarūpeṇa vaktuṃ kartṛkarmavirodhenāpārayantī satī pratyak ātmā iti vakti | kimuta vaikharībhāvabhāsiteti bhāvaḥ | na vaktīti pāṭhe -- "trividhabhāvabhāsitā paraiva tvāṃ na vakti kimuta vaikharītyarthaḥ | ubhayadhāpi vāk svātmarūpiṇīṃ tvāṃ vaktumaśaktaiveti bhāvaḥ | ataeva "yato vāco nivartante | aprāpya manasā saha" ityādiśrutirapi parāprabhṛti vaikharyantaṃ tadādi parāṃtaṃ ca ānulomyena vailomyena vā gaṇanāyāṃ parāvaikharyorubhayorapi turīyarūpatvāttadatītamahāvimarśarūpiṇīṃ tvāṃ sṛṣṭisthitisaṃhārāmyatamarūpeṇa, tattritayasāmarasyaturīyarūpeṇa vā vaktumaśaktā kevalaṃ taccatuṣṭayātītamahāvimarśasadrūpeṇavāstitvaṃ dyotayantī vāca iti bahutvavivakṣayāpi svasvarūpasyāvācyatvena manasā saha svarūpānnivṛttiṃ kaṇṭharaveṇāha "yato vāco nivartante" iti | ataeva śrīsaṃvitstotrepi pañcapadamahāvimarśamayavāksvarūpemevamuktam "sambhāvyo gotare śabdaḥ pratyayo vā na cānyadhā | na sambhāvyau tadātmatvādahaṃkartustathaiva ca" iti | ahaṃ karturityekaṃ padam | ahaṃśabdaprayokturityarthaḥ || 8-30 ||
<2-9-31>
atha mahāvimarśamayavāgrūpiṇyāḥ kālikāyāḥ svasvarūpādadhaḥ sthitasṛṣṭisthitisaṃhārātmakakriyāśaktitripuṭyāḥ nādabindukalātmakajñānaśaktitripuṭyāśca madhyakendrasthityā pūrvoktaturīyatadatītādisaptasrotovamanena saptavyāhṛtirūpatāmapi vyākaroti -- 
nādabindulipivigrahāgira
stisra ūrdhvagavimarśa(śīrakāḥ) śaktikāḥ |
saṃhṛtisthitivisṛṣṭidhāmasu
vyāhṛtāstvadadha īśavallabhe || 31 ||
nādabindvityādinā | heīśavallabhe! pūrvoktamahāvirāṅgāyatrīvāgartharūpeśvaravāṇīrūpiṇīti sābhiprāyasambuddhiḥ | giraḥ mahāvimarśamayaparāvāgarthaśarīriṇyā vāgdevatāyā ityarthaḥ | ūrdhvagavimarśaśīkarāḥ ūrdhvabhūmiṃ gato yo vimarśaḥ tacchīkarāḥ | tadīyatrayasareṇavaḥ | vimarśaśaktikā iti pāṭhe -- vimarśasvarūpasyālpaśaktaya ityarthaḥ | tisraḥ tisaṅkhyākāḥ | nādabindulipivigrahāḥ -- nādaḥ anāhatākhyaḥ vivakṣāparaparyāyo'vyaktadhvaniviśeṣaḥ | janalokavyāhṛtisthānīyaḥ | binduḥ vaktavyākārākāritaniścayātmakabuddhivṛttiviśeṣaḥ | bindati spandatīti vyutpattyā tapolokavyāhṛtisthānīyaḥ | `tapa ālocane' iti dhātoḥ prāṇaspandākhyabuddhivṛttirūpa iti yāvat | lipiḥ ahantāmamatādisvarūpādhyāsādhyāsitakalāparaparyāyaḥ satyalokavyāhṛtisthānīyaḥ | vyāvahārikapāramārthikasattāsāmarasyasphuṭākṛtirdhvaniviśeṣaḥ | evaṃ trirūpavigrahāḥ | tvadadhaḥ | saccitsukhātmakatvasvarūpasyādhastāt saṃhatisthitavisṛṣṭidhāmasu | vyāhṛtāḥ | vailomyena subarbhuvaḥ bhūḥ ityuktavilomavyāhṛtisthāneṣveva janastapassatyavyāhṛtayopi paṭhitā ityarthaḥ | nādibindulipināmabhirvyavahniyamāṇapadānyeva arthaprakriyāyāṃ saṃhārasthitisṛṣṭirūpeṇa vyavahriyanta iti bhāvaḥ | tādṛśaśabdārthaśarīriṇītvaṃ parāvāgrūpeṇa tadubhayamadhyakendravartinīti prauḍhaprakriyayā varṇanam | etena parāyāḥ bahiḥpade paśyantīmadhyamāvaikharīrūpeṇa traividhyam antaḥpade nādabindukalātmakavimarśāṃśarūpeṇa ca | ṣāḍvidhye svasvarūpeṇa saha saptasrotomayatvam | nādabindukalātmakatripuṭīmayatve maha iti caturthavyāhṛtimayaturīyarūpatattvasya sṛṣṭisthitisaṃhārassaha svasya nādabindukalātmakasvarūpatriṭhībhede vivakṣite svarūpatripuṭyapekṣayā turīyātītatvaṃ cetyādi yathāyathamūhyam | ataeva śrutirapi turīyavyāhṛtisvarūpavarṇanāvasare `bhūrbhuvassuvaritivā etāstisro vyāhṛtayaḥ | tāsāmuhasmaitāṃ caturthīm | mahācamasyaḥ pravedayate | maha iti | tadbrahma | sa ātmā | aṅgānyanyā devatāḥ' ityādinā turīyanādamayamahāvyāhṛtereva vāgarthabrahmarūpatvam | ante ca "tā vā etāścatasraścaturthā| catasraḥ catasro vyāhṛtayaḥ | tā yo vedaḥ | sa veda brahma | sarvasmai devā balimāvahanti" ityādinā mahācaturarthakramarūpeṇa tatsvarūpaveditussarvadevatāpūjyatvadharmapuraskāreṇa brahmatvañca kaṇṭhata udaghoṣayaditi dik || 9-31 ||
<2-10-32>
evaṃ vāgarthatripuṭīdvayena saha saptavyāhṛtisaptasrotomayatvamupavarṇya somasūkyavahnimayamaṇḍalatrayātītaturīyāgnidhāmānamākāśastha kālanāmamūrticakramayasaṅkarṣaṇāgniśivadarśanaṃ prārthayan stauti -- 
dṛkkriyātmaśaśibhānumadhyagaṃ
khe(caratyanilavatsudhāmayaḥ)caratvanalayaṣṭidhāma yat |
yattadūrdhvaśikharaṃ paraṃ nabha
statra darśaya śivaṃ tvamambike || 32 ||
dṛkkriyetyādinā | he ambike ! dṛkkriyātmaśaśibhānumadhyagam | dṛkkriyātmānau jñānakriyāmayau | yau śaśibhūnū tayormadhyagatam | anilavat prāṇavāyumat | yat khaṃ sudhāma tatra khe | sudhāmaya iti pāṭhe -- amṛtajīvaśaktimayaḥ | carati prāṇātmanā sañcarati | "carabhakṣaṇe" iti dhātoścaturvidhānnaṃ bhakṣayatīti vā'rthaḥ | atra śrutirapi "sograbhugvibhajantiṣṭhannāhāramajaraḥ kaviḥ" iti tasya caturvidhānnasyāgrabhuktvaṃ tathā "nīvāraśūkavattanvī pītā bhāsvatyaṇūpamā | tasyāśśikhāyā madhye paramātmā vyavasthitaḥ | sa brahma sa śivassa harissendrassokṣaraḥ paramaḥ svarāṭ " iti tasyaiva sarvadevatāmayatvaṃ ca varṇayati | śārṅgadharasaṃhitāyāṃ ca "nābhisthaḥ prāṇapavanaḥ spṛṣṭvā hṛtkamalāntaram | kaṇṭhādbahirviniryāti pātuṃ viṣṇupadāmṛtam || pītvācāmbarapīyūṣaṃ punarāyāti vegataḥ | prīṇayandehamakhilaṃ jīvayanjāṭharānalam" iti | smṛtirapīmamevārthaṃ sphuṭayati | khecaratvanalayaṣṭidhāmayat iti pāṭhe -- analayaṣṭeḥ yaṣṭhyāśrayāgneḥ dhāmeva dhāma yasya khasya tādṛśaṃ yat cidāgnidaharākāśamayaṃ | caratu sañcaratu | bhakṣayatu vā | iti sambhāvanāyāṃ loṭ | caturvidhānnajāraṇakāryaliṃgakānumānasiddhaṃ tatsañcaraṇaṃ bhakṣaṇaṃ veti bhāvaḥ | tadūrdhvaśikharaṃ tādṛśacinmayadaharākāśasya yadūrdhvaśikhararūpaṃ paraṃ nabhaḥ | astīti śeṣaḥ | "ākāśaśarīraṃ brahma" ākāśastalliṅgāt" ityādiśrutisūtrapramāṇakaṃ brahmabiladvādaśāntādirūpaṃ yat cinmayaṃ vyomāsti tatra khe śivaṃ darśaya | sarvamaṅgalā daharākāśarūpiṇī tvam | sarvamaṅgalaṃ dvādaśāntabrahmavilātmakaṃ svātmarūpaṃ śivaṃ pratyakṣīkurviti phalitorthaḥ | "sa ya eṣontarhṛdaya ākāśaḥ | tasminnayaṃ puruṣo manomayaḥ | amṛto hiraṇmayaḥ" ityupakramya taittirīyaśīkṣāvalyāṃ "āpnoti svārājyam" ityādinā mokṣaphalopanyāsāditi bhāvaḥ || 10-32 ||
<2-11-33>
evaṃ maṇḍalatrayasāmarasyātmanā tricaturarthakramopayogisaptavyāhṛtisaptasrotomayatvamupavarṇya pūrvoktavāgarthasāmarasyatripuṭīkṛtyatrayātmanā vakṣyamāṇapañcārthamahākramopayoginavabrahmayatvaṃ sphuṭamupavarṇayati -- 
vaikharīprabhṛtivāktrayaṃ pare
meyamānami(ti kartṛ) taratra) lakṣaṇam |
udbhavasthitilayātmakaṃ vapu
rbhūri bhedamaparaṃ smaranti (yat) te || 33 ||
vaikharītyādinā | pare parāvāgrūpiṇi | abhinnavāgarthātmakamahāpramātarītyarthaḥ | hepare parāvāksvarūpiṇi kālīti sambuddhirvā | jñānakriyātatsāmarasyāparaparyāyaśābdarthakṛtyatripuṭītrayātmanā navabrahmasamaṣṭhyātmakatvātparāyā iti bhāvaḥ | vaikharīprabhṛtīnāṃ vaikharīmadhyamāpaśyantīnāmnīnāṃ vācāṃ trayam | meyamānamiti kartṛlakṣaṇam | yathākramaṃ meyamānamiti kartṛlakṣaṇam | yathākramaṃ meyamānamiti kartṝṇāṃ prameyapramāṇapramātṝṇāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ svarūpaṃ bhavati | arthapade iti bhāvaḥ | meyamānamitaratralakṣaṇamiti pāṭhe itaratra arthapade | bhedapramātrātmanītyarthaḥ | meyamānaṃ prameyabhūtasya bhedasya mānaṃ pramāṇam | lakṣaṇaṃ svarūpaṃ bhavati | evamabhedabedayośśarīraṃ nirūpya tatsāmarasyaśarīramapi nirūpayati -- udbhavetyādinā | udbhavasthitilayātmakaṃ sṛṣṭisthitisaṃhārarūpam | bhūrimedaṃ pūrvoktabhedābhedasvābhāvyādadhikatarabhedayuktam | ataeva te tava aparaṃ vapuḥ jaḍājaḍasāmarasyātmakaṃ śarīraṃ smaranti | smartāra iti śeṣaḥ | smaranti yaditi pāṭhe -- yadbhūribhedaṃ vapurasti tadaparaṃ "a i uṇ"ityādinādasvaranavakasāmarasyātmakaṃ smarantītyarthaḥ || 11-33 ||
<2-12-34>
evaṃ bhedābhedasāmarasyamayatripuṭītrayaṃ vivicya tatra navayonirūpe śivaśaktyorāśrayāśrayibhāvena pañcavāhatāṃ vivecayati -- 
viśvamūrtiyadadṛṣṭavigrahaṃ
tva (ta) tpraśāntipadamaiśvaraṃ vapuḥ |
tatra te ya udayaḥ kuleśi (suveśi)taṃ
sṛṣṭirāśrayati paṃcavāhṛtā || 34 ||
viśvamūrtityādinā | hekuleśi! sajātīyasamūhaḥ kulam | tasyeśiniyantrīti sambuddhiḥ | viśvaṃ vyomavyāpyādyekāśītipadātmakameva mūrtiryasya tat, yadvā "viśvaḥ sthūlābhimānī syādviśvaṃ cetsarvanāmakam" ityuktaliṅgānuśāsananayena sarvanāmakajagadākāramūrtikaṃ, sthūlābhimānimūrtikaṃ vā | tathāpyadṛṣṭavigrahaṃ adṛṣṭaḥ carmacakṣuragocaraḥ vigrahaḥ vividhagrahaḥ | bhedajñānavāsanārūpa iti yāvat | yasya tat adṛṣṭavigrahamityarthakrame | akāreṇa anuttarākāramātṛkārṇamūlabhūtena dṛṣṭavigrahaṃ adṛṣṭavigrahamiti śābdakrame ca tādṛśam | tvatpraśāntipadaṃ tvadīyapraśāntisthānam | tatpraśāntipadamiti pāṭhetasya vāgarthamayeśvarasya praśāntisthānam | tat parokṣatvena vyavahāryaṃ praśāntipadamiti vā | aiśvaryaṃ vapuḥ pūrvavimarśoktakaisāsādrimayaṃ īśvarasambandhīśvarābhinnaṃ vāgarthamayaṃ vā vapuḥ yadasti tatra tasminvapuṣi te tava udayaḥ | pañcākṣamālāmayajñānendriyavṛttipañcakavikāsarūpaḥ | vakṣyamāṇakhecaryādivṛttipañcakarūpo vā asti | taṃ tvadīyodayam | pañcavāhatā sṛṣṭiḥ | jñānagarbhitakriyāśaktivikāsarūpiṇī | karmandriyavṛttipravāhatā ca | kriyāgarbhitajñānaśaktivivikāsarūpiṇī jñānendriyavṛttipravāhatā ca | ubhayasāmarasyavikāsarūpiṇī khecaryādiśaktipañcakapravāhatā ca | sṛṣṭirūpiṇī āśrayati | suveśatamiti " iti śrutyuktaṃ suṣṭu veśitaṃ saṃjātasṛṣṭyanupraveśarūpaṃ tvadīyodayaṃ pañcavāhatāsṛṣṭirāśrayatītyarthaḥ | tvadanupraveśa eva vāgarthasṛṣṭitvena vyavahriyata iti bhāvaḥ | atra kuleśīti kālīviśeṣaṇamahimnā viśvamūrtyadṛṣṭavigrahamiti vāgarthamayeśvaravapurviśeṣaṇamahimnā ca navayoninādanādhasāmarasyātmakaviśvamayaśrīcakre tripuṭītatsāmarasyātmanā baindavatrikoṇacakrayorantaḥ tripuṭītatsāmarastharūpayoḥ antarbahirbuddhīndriyatripuṭīsāmarasyasthānīyāṣṭārāṣṭadalāmbujayoḥ, antarbahirvimarśakriyāsādhanaprāṇendriyamaṇḍalasūryamanaścandrātmanordaśāradvayaṣoḍaśadalapadmayoḥ, antarbahirahaṅkārātmanorbhuvanāsrabhūpurayośca āśleṣātmakaṃ śaivaśāktacatuḥpañcakoṇacakradvayamithunātmakaṃ sāmarasyamapi sūkṣmekṣikayā sūtritamiti bodhyam | taduktaṃ brahmāṇḍapurāṇe lalitātriśatīnāmottarapīṭhikāyām "trikoṇe baindavaṃ śliṣṭamaṣṭāreṣṭadalāmbujam | daśārayoṣṣoḍaśāraṃ bhūpuraṃ bhuvanāsrake || śaivānāmapi śāktānāṃ cakrāṇāṃ ca parasparam | avinābhāvasambandhaṃ yo jānāti sa cakravit " || 12-34 ||
<2-13-35>
evaṃ śiśaktyorāśrayāśrayibhāvena navayonicakramayamabhedamupapādya tatra pūrvokta "catvāri vākparimitā padāni" iti śrutisiddhavedamayacatussamudrarūpaparāvāṅmayaśabdārṇave taraṅgamayatvenārthapañcakamayīṃ vakṣyamāṇapañcavāhakramopayoginīṃ vāgarthasṛṣṭiṃ vyutpādayati -- 
yā parā sphurati śaktirambike !
tatra hi tvayi payodhivīcivat |
śiṃjikāvayavakārthapaṃcakaṃ
vartate vividhasūkṣmavigraham || 35 ||
yā paretyādinā | he ambike! yā parāvāgrūpiṇī śaktiḥ sphurati | tatra tasyāṃ tvayi | tadrūpiṇyāṃ tvayītyarthaḥ | payodhivīcivat samudravīcaya iva | śiṃjikāḥ jñānakriyāmayavyāpārapādābharaṇadhvaniviśeṣā eva avayavāni yeṣāṃ śabdasparśādyarthānāṃ jātivyaktidravyaguṇakriyārūpāṇāṃ, puruṣārthacatuṣṭhayatatsāmarasyarūpāṇāṃ vā, arthānāmarthāśarūpāṇāṃ ni || `puṃsyartho'rtha sameśake' `artho'bhidheye raivastu' ityubhayatrāpyamarakośādabidheyānāṃ pañcakaṃ ca śiṃjikāvayavakārthapañcakaṃ | vividhasūkṣmavigrahaṃ nānāprakārakasūkṣmākāravilakṣaṇajñānayuktaṃ tadrūpañca vartate | antassamudraṃ dhvaniyuktavīciparamparāvat atarhṛdayaṃ saśabdaṃ viṣayapañcakaṃ, puruṣārthapañcakaṃ vā, abhidheyārthapañcakamapi vā sāṅketikasūkṣmaśabdaśarīreṇaiva vartata iti bhāvaḥ | athrārthapañcakasya payodhivīcivadityupamānasāmarthyena śiṃjikāvayavakatvaviśeṣaṇasāmarthyena ca samudravadadhiṣṭhānabhūtāyāṃ caturvidhābhidhāyakaśaktirūpeṇa parāśabdaśaktirūpiṇyāṃ sṛṣṭikālyāmabhidheyārthapañcakasyāpi pañcavidhatvaṃ śabdaśarīreṇaivetyapi dyotitam | tadetatsarvaṃ caturthavimarśe -- "arthapañcakasudhāprapūrite yadbhavāmṛtanidhau sukhaplave" ityetat ślokavyākhyānāvasare vivecayiṣyata ityatra viramyate || 13-35 ||
<2-14-36>
evamantarbahissādhāraṇaṃ vāgdevatā tricatuḥpaṃcārthamahākramasvarūpamupavarṇya"antarbahiḥ puruṣakālarūpaiḥ" iti pūrvamuktaprakāreṇāntarātmavibhavena saha bahiḥ kālavibhutimapi sāmarasyena vitatya varṇayituṃ dvādaśamāsātmakasaṃvatsarakalpepi "asti trayodaśo māsa ityāhuḥ" ityuktadvādaśāntabindusāmarasyena sṛṣṭikālīmukhacatuṣṭayātmakaṃ caturarthakramavarṇanamārabhate -- 
tvanmukhaṃ tripathanetri! bhātmakaṃ
śaktibṛndamihaturyadhāgamam |
dvādaśakramapadaṃ padādibhiḥ
(jṛmbhitaṃ)bṛṃhitaṃ) jagati taccatusrikaiḥ || 36 ||
tvanmukhamityādinā | hetripathenetri! trīṇi vakṣyamāṇabījayonivarṇotpattiprakriyāyāṃ akāra ikāra ukāraparāmarśatrikarūpāṇi, śāmbhavaśāktāṇavavisargatrayavilāsātmakapramātṛpramāṇaprameyāṃśajñānopāsanākarmarūpāṇi, tadupayogisthūlasūkṣmakāraṇaśarīrarūpāṇi, tadīyajāgratsvapnasuṣuptyavasthārūpāṇi, tatsarvakāraṇabhūtabhaviṣyadvartamānakālāvayavāni ca panthānaḥ tattvāvabodhakamārgaviśeṣāḥ | teṣāṃ samāhārastripatham | tasya netri nāyike! itivā, trayaḥ panthāna eva netrāṇi yasyāḥ sā tripathanetrīti
