siddhaśaktītyādinā | he ambike ! tvadantike iti pāṭhe tvatsamīpeyoyamarthanikaraḥ śabdasparśādivāgrūpaḥ ghaṭapaṭādyartharūpaśca | dharmādicaturvargapuruṣārthanikaraśca | siddhaśaktimayabhātṛbhāsanavyāptibhāvaḥ | siddhatyātmanaḥ śaktimayaḥ jñānakriyāśaktisāmarasyarūpaḥ yo bhātṛbhāsanavyāptibhāvaḥ | deśakālādiviprakarṣepi apratihatajñānaśaktimayaḥ bhātṛvyāptibhāvaḥ | tādṛśakriyāśaktisiddhirūpaḥ bhāsanavyāptibhāvaśca yasya sa tathoktassan prakāśatāmupayuka svayaṃprakāśatvopayogī bhavati | yadvā siddhaśaktimayabhātṛbhāsanavyāptyā bhātaḥ prakāśitassan yat yasmātprakāśatā mupayugbhavati tasmāt | asau cittaprabodhapradhānaṃ śāktavisargavilāsaśahdanikarātmakapratyakṣaparāmantravāgrūpiṇī, cittapralayapradhānaṃ śāmbhavavisargavilāsaparokṣā, parāmantrārthaghaṭapaṭādyabhidheyārtharūpiṇī, parokṣā'parokṣacaturvidhapuruṣārtharūpiṇī vā tvam | tvatpramātrabhimukhaṃ prāk tvadbhinnapramātrabhimukhatveneva vidyamānaṃ vāgarthanikaraṃ idānīṃ tvadabhinnapramātrabhimukhaṃ yathā bhavati tathā tanoṣītyarthaḥ | atrāvasthāyāṃ tripuṭībedabhānasya svayaṃprakāśātmatattvaikarasyānnādvaitasvarūpavyāhatiśaṅkāvakāśa iti bhāvaḥ || 38-60 ||
<2-39-61>
etāvatā granthasandarbheṇa cidacinmayavāgarthaprakṛtisāmarasyaṃ śābdanādajyotirmayatvenopavarṇya tanmayasyātmanaḥ svarūpaeva sākṣitvasaṃsāritvadharmadvayaprakaṭanena cinmayānandatripuṭyaṃśapramātṛprameyabhāvayorvinimayenāṃ tasthānandasvarūpatatvabodhanāya dhāmasaṃvidvarṇakramaiḥ prakāśānandamūrtimayacakratrayopāstiṃ krameṇa vistārayiṣyannādau saṃkṣepeṇa dhāmamayaprakāśacakraṃ sūcayati -- 
draṣṭṛtā (dṛṣṭatā) jagati yatprakāśane
yacca niścayavidhau pramātṛ (kā) tā |
grāhaka (prāhakaṃ) tvamapi niścitagrahe
veda tatsa tava vṛttipoṣitaḥ || 61 ||
draṣṭṛtetyādinā | jagati gacchatīti jagat iti | "siṃho varṇaviparyayāt" iti siṃhādigaṇapaṭhitavarṇaviparyayavyutpattyā varṇāgamavyutpattyā vā sādhakamukhyaprāṇarūpe gatimati jīve ityarthaḥ | prakāśane viṣaye antarbahirindriyapravṛttinivṛttyātmakavaiṣayikavyāpāre viṣaye ityarthaḥ | yat draṣṭṛtā darśanakartṛtā | iti sarvajñānendriyadharmasāmānyoddeśavivakṣayā yaditi napuṃsakam | evamuttaratrāpi jñeyam | dṛṣṭateti pāṭhe -- yacca dṛgviṣayatvaṃ | antarbahirindriyavṛttiviṣayatvamityarthaḥ | niścayavidhau viṣayatatvaniścayavidhānātmakabuddhivyāpāre tathā niścitaviṣayavyāpārātmikāyāṃ karmendriyapravṛttau ca | yacca pramātṛtā ahaṃpramātetyākārakaniścitapramāśrayatvarūpābhyantarakartṛvyāpāraḥ | pramātṛketi pāṭhe -- prakṛṣṭavarṇamālā, tadīyabuddhivṛttirvetyarthaḥ | tathā niścitagrahe niścitaviṣayasvarūpasambandhini jñānendriyavyāpāre | viṣayādānādikarmendriyavyāpāreca viṣaye | yacca grāhakatvamapi jñānakarmobhayendriyarūpatvamapi | prāhakaṃ tvamiti pāṭhe tvameva saḥ | saeva tvamiti ca | prakṛṣṭavarṇamātṛkā tvaṃ kaṃ prāhetyarthaḥ | śāktavisargavarṇamālārūpiṇī tvaṃ āṇavaśāmbhavavisargayormadhye kamuddiśya prāheti jīvamīśvaraṃ vā uddiśyetyarthaḥ | tatsarvaṃ tava vṛttipoṣitaḥ parāvāgdevatāyāstava vaktṛtvaśrotṛtvādibahirantarvyāpāreṇa sañjātapoṣaṇassan | saḥ pūrvaślokoktatvadabhinnapramātā mukhyaprāṇāśivaḥ veda vidyayā labhate | `vidl jñānalābhayoḥ | iti dhātvarthe viparītapramātṛbhāvena labhan jānīte | kuta iti cet gajavṛṣabhacitranyāyena prathamaṃ tvadīyabuddhivṛttyupetakarmendriyavyāpāreṇa poṣitaḥ jāgataviṣayavargaṃ karmamayo labhan paścānmanovṛttyupetajñānendriyavyāpārapoṣaṇena jñānamayopi saṃsarannantatassāmarasyavyāpāreṇa kevalaṃ sākṣitayā paśyaṃ ścāste iti bhāvaḥ | etadeva nādanavakamayasyātmanaḥ svarājyāparaparyāyatripuṭīṣaḍvargaṣaḍaṅgātmakāṣṭādaśasvarasaṃkhyayā paṃcatrikasamaṣṭyātmakaṣoḍaśasaṃkhyayā ekadvitricaturvargādipūrvoktayathāsambhavaparāmarśatrikādirūpeṇa ca svato rājanarañjanādirūpaprakāśaraśmicakramayatvena pravṛttinivṛttimayasvarājyapraṇetṛtvaṃ veditavyam | vakṣyati cāgrepi kavireva -- "rājanātprakṛtiऱñjanācca māṃ rājasaṃjña"mityādinā jñānakriyārājyabhāgayorvivicya ṣaṇṇāṃ jñānakarmendriyacakrabhāvabhedena rājaputrabhāvaṃ pārthakyena samaṣṭyācātmano rājabhāvaṃ ceti prakāśacakrasūcanam || 39-61 ||
<2-40-62>
atha pūrvaślokasūcitatripuṭītrayanādanavakasamaṣṭidhāmamayaprakāśacakrasya śāktaśāmbhavānuttaravisargatrayasamaṣṭivilāsātmakasadasadvāgarthamaṇ‍ḍale dhīmanokṣavapurasmitāyāśca svātantryābhyāṃ svasvarūpaśeṣaśeṣibhāvaprayuktaṃ caturthavimarśe vakṣyamāṇamūrtiprakāśacakraprādhānyopakārakamaṃśāṃśiprādhānyabhāvaṃ pāṭhabhedena vivicya darśayati -- 
āsthitasdasadarthamaṇḍalaṃ
dhīmanokṣavapurasmitāmayam |
yatra yo daśavidhaḥ (yatra yā daśavidhiḥ) svatantratā
tadvayopyahamasau tavāṃśakaḥ (tavādhikaḥ) || 62 ||
āsthita ityādinā | ayaṃ pūrvaślokoktaśāmbhavāṇavānyataravisargamayo mukhyaprāṇaśivaḥ | dhīmanokṣavapurasmitāṃ sadasadarthamaṇḍalamūlakāraṇabhūtāmiti bhāvaḥ | sadasadarthamaṇḍalaṃ ca āsthitaḥ | āṇavavisargavilāsātmanā adhiṣṭhitaḥ | yatra dhīmanokṣavapurasmitāyāṃ tatkāryasadasadaryamaṇḍaleca | yaḥ āṇavavisargavilāsāpannamukhyaprāṇaśivaḥ | daśavidhiḥ dhīpradhānakarmākṣavapurasmitāprakāreṇapañcamanaḥ pradānajñānākṣavapurasmitāprakāreṇa pañcacetyāhatya daśajñānakarmendriyāṇividhīyante aneneti daśavidhiḥ | kiñcātra yattrayodaśavidha iti pāṭheyābhyāṃ dhīmanokṣavapurasmitāsadasadarthamaṇḍalābhyāṃ trayodaśavidhaḥ | sadasadarthamaṇḍaloktadaśaprakārabhedena dhīvapurasmitāmanovapurasmitākṣavapurasmitāmayatriprakārakāsmitābhedena ca vakṣyamāṇadvādaśāntamahābindvabhinnatrayodaśaprakāraścāyamāṇavavisargātmakaḥ prāṇaśiva ityapi dhvanyate "prāṇasūrya iha cidviṣaṭkalaḥ" ityādi vakṣyamāṇatvāt | yadvā pūrvoditayatra yā daśavidhiriti pāṭhabhedapakṣe yatra sadasadarthamaṇḍale yā dhīmanokṣavapurasmitāpi daśavidhiḥ statantratā'taddvayopi svatantratayā dhīmanokṣavapurasmitāmayātmasvātantryeṇa svatantratā'taddvayopi tvatantratayā dhīmanokṣavapurasmitāmayātmasvātantryeṇa ataddvayopi cittapralayātmakaśāmbhavisargavilāsena vapurasmitāprasaktasadasadarthamaṇḍaladvayarahitopi | yadvā cittaprabodhātmakaśāktānuttaravisargavilāsena pūrvokta "a i uṇ" ityuktanādanavakayonisvarabhūtānuttarākārātmakatadubhayavilāsopi akāragatataddvayavilāsaḥ ataddvayavilāsa iti vyutpattyā svatantratāsiddhā'taddvayavilāsopīti vā | tavāṃśakaḥ ahaṃ iti śāmbhavavisargavilāsatmanā nādapradhānena | asau iti śāktānuttaravisargavisātmanā vā śābdabindupradhānena | tava nādanavakātmikāyāṃ aṃśakaḥ | nādātmanā vāgarthaśabdātman vā apracyutāṇavavilāsātmadharmakatvāttvadīyāṃśarūpaevetyarthaḥ | tavādhika iti pāṭhe -- śāmbhavavisargavilāsanādākāreṇa śāktānuttaravisargavilāsavāgarthasāmarasyātmanā vā | ahamevāsau iti vā asāvahamiti vā pracyutāṇubhāvassan prāptonmanīmahāprāṇanāda bhāvo vā san vāgartharūpiṇyāstavāpi pūjya evetyarthaḥ || 40-62 ||
<2-41-63>
evamuktavāgarthasāmarasyavartmanā pracyutāṇavavisargavilāsabhedabhāvasya prāptaśāktaśāmbhavānuttaravisargavilāsā'bhedabhāvasya ca dvādaśāntonmanīprakāśamahābindudhāmātmakaprakāśacakrasvābhāvyenaivābhinnānandamūrticakatramayapuruṣottamabhāvamapi dyotayati -- 
tattadarthamayabhogya (ga) lakṣaṇā
nandamūrtirayamuttamaḥ pumān |
arpaṇena jagatāścidambare
bhāti saṃhṛtimayaśiśave ta [taḥ] va || 63 ||
tattadarthetyādinā | ayaṃ pūrvaślokoktaḥ aṇavavisargassādhakaḥ | tattadarthamayabhogyalakṣaṇānandamūrtiḥ taṃ tadarthaṃ vācyalakṣyatātparyādirūpamarthaṃ ayaṃ te pratipādyatāsambandhena gacchantīti tattadarthamayāśśabdāḥ | taeva bhogyavastūni lakṣaṇaṃ ca yasyānandasya sa tattadarthamayabhogyalakṣaṇānandaḥ | sa eva mūrtiryasya nādānandanimagnaprāṇamayasya sa tattadarthamayabhogyalakṣaṇānandamūrtiḥ | vidyānandasāmrājye tattadarthabodhakaśabdānāmeva viduṣāṃ prākṛtānāṃ kṣunnivartakānnādibhogyavastūnāmiva yathādhikāraṃ bhaktijñānavairāgyahetutvenābhyantaratṛptihetutvādānandamūrtilakṣaṇatvamiti bhāvaḥ | taduktaṃ śrīmadbhāgavate aikādaśaskaṃdhe "bhaktiḥ pareśānubhavo viraktiranyatra caiṣatrikakāla ekaḥ | prapadyamānasya yathāśranaḥ syāttuṣṭiḥ puṣṭiḥ kṣudapāyonughāsam" iti | śrutirapi vājapeyaprakaraṇe -- "vāgvai devatānāṃ purānnamāsīt" iti | tattadarthamayabhogalakṣaṇeti pāṭhe -- tattadabhidheyārthamayaṃ tricaturvargapuruṣārthamayaṃ ca bhogaṃ lakṣayatīti tattadarthamayabhogalakṣaṇaḥ | sacāsāvānandaḥ tasya mūrtiḥ sa eva vā mūrtiryasya saḥ | tattadarthamayabhogalakṣaṇānandamūrtirityarthaḥ | ataeva uttamaḥ vāgarthātītākṣaraprakāśanādaparatvena udgatatamaskaḥ | viśvottīrṇanādamayatvāditi bhāvaḥ | ataeva pumān viśvottīrṇatvenaiva puruṣottamaścetyarthaḥ | taduktaṃ gītāsu bhagavatāpi "kṣararssavāṇi bhūtāni kūṭasthokṣara ucyate | uttamaḥ puruṣastvanyaḥ paramātmetyudāhṛtaḥ | yo lokatrayamāviśya bibhartyavyaya īśvaraḥ | yasmātkṣaramatītohamakṣarādapi cottamaḥ | atosmi loke vede ca prathitaḥ puruṣottamaḥ | iti | etenātmano viṣayānandabodhakavāgarthahetūnmanīmahānādalakṣaṇasyaivānandamūrtitve tasya bhoktrahaṃkārasākṣitvasyāpyuktaprāyatvādāvidyakagraṃdhibhedo vyākhyātaḥ | tatprakārastu vedāṃtapaṃcapaśyāṃ pratyaktatvaviveke | "ahaṃkāracidātmanāvekīkṛtyā vivekataḥ | idaṃ me syadidaṃ me syādidaṃ me syāditīcchā kāmaśabditāḥ | apraveśya cidātmānaṃ pṛthakpaśyannahaṃkṛtiṃ | icchaṃstu koṭivastūni na bādhograṃdhibhedataḥ iti | heśive! viparītecchārūpe ! heamba! tataḥ uktalakṣaṇaviṣayabhogāddhetoḥ | jagataḥ pūrvoktagatimatprāṇalakṣaṇasya jīvasya | cidambare cinmayākāśe arpaṇena saṃhṛtimayaḥ saṃhṛtiṃ saṃhāraṃ ayate prāpnoti prāpayati ceti saṃhatimayaḥ | svayameva saṃhārakriyāmayaḥ cittapralayaśāmbhavavisargavilāsātmanā svātmopasaṃhartā sṛṣṭikṛtyakārī vā cittaprabodhaśāktavisargavilāsātmanā bhavatītyarthaḥ |jāgatajaḍājaḍavātūnāṃ pralapakālikabhūtākāśavilaya iva vidvadbhogyavastūnāṃ bhogakālikadaharākāśavilaya iti nādopastividyārahasyamitibhāvaḥ | tavatipāṭhe tava cidambare tvadabhinnacinmayākāśe sahṛtamayānandamūrtyanyatararūpo bhātītyanvayaḥ || 41-63 ||
<2-42-64>
nanvevaṃvidhanādopāstibalenāvāptanādānandamūrtilakṣaṇaśarīrakasya puruṣapaureyasya śārīrakavāsanāsvapravṛttervāgarthamayaśśavasaṃsāropi lupyatetyāśaṅkya na kulīśapāśupatādiśaivatantramaryādayā mātṛkāvargāṣṭakābhedakalpitavāgarthaparyaṣṭakalaiṅgikavāsanāmayaṃ vāgarthavyavahāraṃ darśayanviśvamayaviśvottīrṇajīvadevapaśubhāvabhedamapi darśayati -- 
aṣṭadhā sphurati yā purī śive!
sūkṣmabhāvamupagamya laiṅgikam |
tajjapauṣṭa] tajjuṣoṣṭa] kṛtacittavṛttayo
nāsanā(stva)sta] durasi) stvadudara]sthite paśau || 64 ||
aṣṭhadheti | heśive iti sambuddhiḥ | saptamī vā kāryaśivādhikaraṇavivakṣayā | laiṅgikaṃ sūkṣmabhāvaṃ | ekatra pṛthivyādipañcabhūtasthūlaliṅgabhāvena agnisūryasomamaṇḍalatrayasthūlabhāvena ca kalpitaṃ tadīyagandhādipañcatanmātrarūpaṃ citprāṇadeharūpaṃ ca | anyatra "citiścittaṃ ca caityaṃ ca cetanādvayameva ca | jīvaḥ prāṇaḥ śarīraṃ ca puryaṣṭakamudāhṛtam ||" tathā "vāgādipañca śravaṇādi pañca prāṇādi pañcabhramukhāni pañca | buddhyādyavidyāpi ca kāmakarmaṇī puryaṣṭakaṃ sūkṣmaśarīramāhuḥ" ityuktalakṣaṇaṃ ca sūkṣmabhāvaṃ | ataeva -- "aṣṭācakrā navadvārā | devānāṃ pūrayodhyā | tasyāgaṃ hiraṇmayaḥ kośaḥ | svargo loko jyotiṣā vṛtaḥ | yovai tāṃ brahmaṇo veda | amṛtenāvṛtāṃ purīṃ | tasmai brahma ca brahmā ca | āyuḥ kīrti prajāṃ dadhuḥ" iti śrutyuktaṃ jagatkāraṇaṃ ca laiṅgikam | līyate viśvamayī bāhyaprakṛtiḥ gamyate cāṣyātmikī vāgarthamayī atreti liṅgam | tadīyalaiṅgikaṃ sūkṣmabhāvaṃ upagamya yā purī arthamayī śabdamayī nādabrahmamayī ca krameṇa sthūlasūkṣmakāraṇopādhitritayasamaṣṭirūpā aṣṭadhā sphurati | ata evoktamanenaiva kavinā śākuntalanāṭakīyanāndīprastāve sūkṣmabhāvenāṣṭamūrtīnāmapi pratyakṣatvam "yā sṛṣṭiḥ saṣṭurādyā vahati vidhihutaṃ yā havi ryāca hotrī ye dve kālaṃ vidhattaḥ śrutiviṣayaguṇā yā sthitā vyāpya viśvam | yāmāhussarvabhūtaprakṛtiriti yayā praṇinaḥ prāṇavantaḥ pratyakṣābhiḥ prasannastanubhiravatu vastābhiraṣṭābhirīśaḥ ||" iti | tajjapauṣṭakṛtacittavṛttayaḥ | tajjape śābdavarṇakrame | teṣāṃ aṣṭamūrtibodhakamātṛkāvargāṣṭakavarṇānāṃ jape oṣṭhakṛtāḥ oṣṭacālanaśikṣitā cittavṛttayaḥ yāsu vāsanāsu tāstathoktāḥ | ārthikavāgarthakrame tu | tajjaṃ aṣṭadhā sphuritaprāṇamanovijñānamūrtimayatrivṛttraivarṇikasūkṣmapurījanyam | yatpauṣṭaṃ puṣṭibhāvaḥ tāvanmātrapoṣakaṃ karma vā | tajjapauṣṭaṃ | tatkṛtāḥ tāvanmātraśikṣitāḥ cittavṛttayo yāsu tāstathoktāśca | tajjuṣoṣṭakṛtacittavṛttaya iti pāṭhe tāmaṣṭavargamātṛkāṃ sūkṣmapurīṃ ca juṣanti prīṇayantīti tajjuṣaḥ | aṣṭa aṣṭasaṅkhyākāḥ "ghṛṇā śaṅkā bhayaṃ lajjā jugupsā ceti pañcamī | kulaṃ jātiśca śīlaṃ cetyaṣṭau pāśāḥ prakīrtitāḥ" ityuktalakṣamapaśupāśarūpāḥ | kṛtacittavṛttayaḥ | bahubhyo janmabhyaḥ jannanaḥ prabhṛti vā śikṣitacittavṛttaya ityarthaḥ | vāsanāḥ vasatyāsu pratyagātmā vāsyate ceti vyutpattibhedena ādhikārikamanovāsanāḥ | tadurasi pūrvoktapuryaṣṭakorasi | sthite paśau bhavantīti śeṣaḥ | viśvamaya eva jīve pāśavyo vāsanā bhavanti | natu viśvottīrṇe iti bhāvaḥ | tvadurasi sthite" tvadudarasthite paśau | iti pāṭhadvayepi uktaparyuṣṭakarūpiṇyāḥ prakṛtestava vāṅmaye urasi arthamaye udare vā yajñavedīyāstumaṇḍapātmike sthite paśāveva bhavanti | natu viśvottīrṇasvabhāve paraśive ityarthaḥ | dehendriyapoṣaṇamātraparyavasannacittavṛttaya eva paśuvāsanārūpeṇa rūḍhamūlā bhavantīti phalitorthaḥ | ataeva paśulakṣaṇamuktaṃ tantrāntare śabdarāśisamutthasya śaktivargasya bhogyatām | kalāviluptavibhavo gatassan sa paśuḥ smṛtaḥ" iti | tattvaprakāśapi "paśavastrividhāḥ proktā vijñānapralayakevalau sakalaḥ | malayuktastatrādyo malakarmayukto dvitīyaḥ syāt || 1 || malamāyākarmayutassakalasteṣu dvidhā bhavedāyaḥ | ādyassamāptakaluṣo'samāptakaluṣo dvitīyaḥ syāt || 2 || ādyananugṛhya śivo vidyeśatve niyojayatyaṣṭau | mantrāṃśca karosyaparāṃ stecoktāḥ koṭayassapta || 3 || ityādinā saptakoṭimahāmantraśarīratve siddhepyādhikārikaṃ pañcatvamapyanivāryamiti darśitaṃ tatraiva | anenaivābhisandhinā śrutāvapi caturdaśasvarābhinnacaturdaśasaṃkhyākavidyābhuvanādīnāmapi chandaḥ pādākṣaravṛttādivāgarthaniyamabaddhaśabdasparśādyapañcīkṛtavāgarthaśarīriṇāmapi vidyāmantrādivāgarthaśabdābhimānināmājānadevānāṃ paṃcīkṛtapāñcabhautikaśarīrābhimānināmiva grāmyāraṇyapaśujātibhedena vibhinnasaptasaptasaṃkhyākapaśujātimattvaṃ "sapta grāmyāḥ paśavassaptāraṇyāssapta chandāgaṃsi" tathā -- "ye grāmyāḥ paśavo viśvarūpāḥ | ya āraṇyāḥ paśavo viśvarūpāḥ" tathā oṣadhijātimattvaṃ ca "sapta grāmyā oṣadhayassaptāraṇyāssapta chandāṃsi" ityādivākyaiḥ taittirīyasaṃhitāyāṃ tṛtīyākāṇḍaprathamapāṭhakacaturthānuvākādiṣu navamānuvāke ca mānavanābhānediṣṭacaritravyājena taittirīyāraṇyakāṇḍe ca tattadvākyaiśśrūyate ataeva bhaviṣyottarapurāṇaturyakhaṇḍaikonaviṃśādhyāyatripañcāśat ślokamārabhya mahādhikārikamapi paśutvāmālabhyatvena kaṇṭhata evoddhoṣitam | yathā "yasya buddhisya vai brahmā'haṅkāro yasya vai śivaḥ" ityādinā yajñapuruṣasyāpi paśutvaṃ vyavasthāpya "hiṃsāyajñaiśca bhagavāṃtsa vai tṛptimavāpnuyāt | sa cedyajñapaśurvahnau brahmabhūyāya kalpate" ityādinā tasyāpyālabhyatvena brahmabhūyatvavarṇānāt | īdṛśamahādhikārikasyāpi yajñapaśutvenālabhyatvaṃ nāma | ācāryamūrtisthaśaktipātāparaparyāyadīkṣāsaṃskāreṇa | tathā "jananaṃ jīvanaṃ caiva tāḍanaṃ bodhanaṃ tathā | abhiṣeko'thavimalīkaraṇāpyāyane punaḥ || tarpaṇaṃ dīpanaṃ guptirdaśaitā mantrasaṃskriyāḥ |" ityādinā śāradātilakādyuktamantrasaṃskāraiśca saṃskāryatvaṃ tāntrikam | uktaṃ ca śaivagame "paripakvamalānetānutsādanahetuśaktitapātena | yo jayati pare tattve sa dīkṣayācāryamūrtisthaḥ" iti | tathā "pūrvaṃ vyatyasitasyāṇoḥ pāśajālaṃ vyapohati | mantrasya mantriṇo vāpi dīkṣā siddhipradā bhavet || pṛthaṅmantraḥ pṛthaṅmantrī na siddhyati kadācana | uccāryamāṇā ye mantrā na matrāṃścāpi tānviduḥ || mohitā devagandharvā mithyā'jñānena garvitāḥ | puṃḍrekṣoriva mantrasya mādhurye hṛdayaspṛśi || ṛjīṣamānane tiṣṭhatyakṣaroccāralakṣaṇam" ityādinā tantrālokādiṣu tadīyavistaropi sphuṭamupalabhyate | atha vaidikaṃ tu vedāntapañcadaśyāṃ citradīpe buddhivṛttipratibimbitacidābhāsarūpeṇa vartamānakṛterjīvasya vāsanāpratibimbitacinmayaśarīreṇeśvaratvāpādanaparyantaṃ svasvarūpabhinnadharmadharmipaśubhāvabhedadarśanasyaiva vivicya darśitatvena tasyavakṣyamāṇaprakāreṇa bimbapratibimbātmake svasvarūpe dharmadharmibhedasyāpyābhāsīkaraṇameva svasvarūpatattvasākṣātkārakṛtamiti saṃkṣepaḥ || 42-64 ||
<2-43-65>
evamuktaprakāreṇa nādanavakavāgarthaṣaḍadhvamamārgabhedena śaivajevapāśukasaṃsāreṣvanyonyabhakṣyabhakṣakayossṛṣṭisaṃhārakriyāmayaśarīrayoḥ pūrvoktadīkṣāmantrasaṃskārādibhissiddhamantramantritādātmyābhedānyatarabhāvayoḥ sraṣṭṛtirodhātṛkāryamayaśarīyorvāntarāle antassākṣibhāsyatvena bahirindriyavedyatvena vā anubhūtaśāktaśāmbhavānyatara unmanīnādanavakānandamūrterjīvasaṃsārabhājopi viśrāntilakṣaṇāṇavavisargapuruṣadhaureyasya svasvarūpa eva kṛtyapañcakakṣamaṃ, pūrvoktalaiṅgikaṃ dhīmanokṣamayaṃ sūkṣmaśarīramunmanīnādanavakarūpiṇyā tvayaiva prasādīkriyata ityāha -- 
sarsaṃ(dṛ)vṛ)ti śarīrayordvayo
rbhakṣyabhakṣakapadasthayostayoḥ |
antare (da)va) mahāmāsthitaśśive !
śrīmanokṣavapurarpitaṃ tvayā || 65 ||
sargasaṃhṛtītyādinā | he śive iti sambuddhiḥ | avidyāniketapadasthitajīvaśivavivakṣayā adhikaraṇasaptamī vā | sargasaṃhṛtiśarīrayoḥ sargaśarīraṃ cittaprabodhalakṣaṇaśāktavisargātmakaṃ jīvaśarīraṃ | saṃhṛtiśarīraṃ cittapralayalakṣaṇaśāmbhavavisargātmakam | sargasaṃvṛtiśarīrayoriti pāṭhe -- cittaprabodhapralayānyatarasvaṣṭṛvāgarthasargaśarīram | saṃvṛtiśarīraṃ tu cittaviśrāntilakṣaṇāṇavavisargamayam | sṛṣṭisaṃhārānyataratādātmyenobhayatādātmyena vā svarūpatirodhānenāvidyakāniketānyatarasthitilakṣaṇena svasvarūpāvarakaṃ ca | tayoḥ sargasaṃ(hṛ(vṛ)tiśarīrayoḥ | ataeva bhakṣyabhakṣakapadasthayoḥ | pūrvaślokoktapaśupāśāṣṭakabhakṣyabhakṣakapade tadbhāve vā tiṣṭhatoḥ | āṇavavisargāpannaśāktaśāmbhavavisargayoryāvatpūrṇabhāvamarthānusvārātmakayoranyonyabhakṣyabhakṣakapadasthitervāgarthamaryādāyamapi samatvāditi bhāvaḥ | tadetatsarvaṃ "kuṇḍahṛtsvaracatuṣṭaye sthito vyaktitaḥ prabhṛti yo jagatkramaḥ" ityatra tṛtīye vimarśe sphuṭamupapādayiṣyata itīha viranyate | dvayoḥ vāgartharūpayoḥ | antarā antarāle | idaṃ pūrvaślokoktaṃ tvadūrorūpaṃ tvadudararūpaṃ vā anuttarākāranādamayaṃ vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇapramāṇāmakṣarūpaṃ magāsthitipadaṃ varmānaṃ | ahaṃ āsthitaḥ | pūrvoktajīveśānyataraṇaśukabhedabhāveneti bhāvaḥ | yadvā pūrvaśloktaṃ tvadurastvadudaraṃ vā ahamevetyabhedātmanā sākṣitvenāsthita iti vā bhāvaḥ | tvayā unmanīnādavāgarthaṣaḍadhvaprakṛtyā dhīmanokṣavapuḥ arpitaṃ | bhedavāsanayā tvadurasyudare vā sthitayoḥ paśupaśupatyoḥ samamevārpitamiti na tavāsti pakṣapāta iti bhāvaḥ || 43-65 ||
<2-44-66>
evaṃ pāśupatādiśaivaśāktatantrāgamamaryādayāpi vidyā'vidyābhuvanamayaviśvottīrṇasya tadīyakamajñānendriyacakrayośca kriyājñānamayasarvayajñakratvarthatvena kṣarākṣaratattvātmakayoḥ "apaśavo vā anyago aśvebhyaḥ" iti taittirīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pañcamakāṇḍadvitīyaprapāṭhake navamānuvāke āgnikakāṇḍoktavaidikapraśastapaśutvanirvāhapūrvakaṃ puruṣottamasya paśupaterātmanaścakrarājasaṃjñāmindriyādiparivārasya dvādaśarājamaṇḍalasyeva rājaputrasaṃjñāṃ ca nirvahan tadubhayasāmarasyena vakṣyamāṇaprakāśānandamūrtimayasamaṣṭibṛndacakrarājavarṇanāya bījamāvapati -- 
rājanātprakṛtiraṃjanācca māṃ
rājasaṃjñamanubodhakarmaṇoḥ |
ṣaṭtvayā pṛthagamī pravartakāḥ
putrabhāvama(dhi(pi) ropitāśśive || 66 ||
rājanādityādinā | he śive! iti cittasambodhalakṣamaśāktānuttaravisargacinmayajīvaprakṛtivivakṣayā sambuddhiḥ | cittaviśrāntilakṣaṇāṇavānuttaravisargajaḍajīvamayajagadadhikaraṇavivakṣayā, cittapralayalakṣaṇaśāmbhavānuttaravisargaparaśivasvarūpaviṣayavivakṣayā vā saptamī vā | rājanāt "rājṛ rañjanadīptyoḥ, ithidhātoḥ pūrvoktonmanīnādāparaparyāyamukhyaprāṇasvarūpe svayaṃprakāśanāt | tathā prakṛtirañjanācca prakṛteḥ vāgartharūpiṇyā nādaprakṛteḥ rañjanācca | trayīprabhṛticaturdaśaparyantavidyābhuvanādivāgarthasṛṣṭau māheśvaramātṛkāsūtradvāreṇeti bhāvaḥ | rājasaṃjñaṃ -- rājeti saṃjñā yasya taṃ tathoktam | tathācoktaṃ parimalapi "jagato rañjanādrājā sākṣāddevo maheśvaraḥ" iti | māṃ vāgarthatripucītrivṛtprāṇarūpamātmānaṃ | anu anusṛtya | bodhakarmaṇoḥ pūrvoktasāṃkhyayogamārgarūpayoḥ jñānakarmaṇorviṣaye | pṛthakpravartakāḥ "deśakālakalanāviśeṣato yadbhavāni vihitaṃ nigadyate (niṣidhyate)" iti pūrvoktādhyāropāpavādāparaparyāyapravṛttinivṛttimārgabhedena viruddhamārgabhedeneva pravartakatvātpṛthakpravartakatvamiti bhāvaḥ | ṣaṭ amī krameṇa manassahitajñānendriyaṣaṭkarūpāḥ nādasrarūpajñāne jñānātmanaḥ pravartakā nivartakāśca kriyātmanaḥ | tathā buddhisahitakarmendriyaṣaṭkarūpāḥ | vāgarthajananādikṛtyapañcakātmake karmaṇi vidhayeñca kriyātmanaḥ pravartakāḥ nivartakāśca jñānātmanaḥ | evaṃpṛthakpravartakāḥ putrabhāvaṃ buddhisahitakarmendriyaṣaṭakam | karmamārgaputrāvaṃ cādhiṣṭhāpitā ityarthaḥ | api ropitā iti pāṭhena kevalaṃ "brahmaṇaḥ prabhṛtisiddhapañcakam" iti ślokoktasiddhabrahmaśaktipañcakadvayābhinnonmanīnādākṛtibhāvaṃ nāpi pūrvaślokoktapaśupaśupatibhāvaṃ kintu rājaputrabhāvamapi ropitāḥ prāpitā ityarthaḥ | yathā loke prākṛtāsāmrājye yauvarājyapadamadhiṣṭhāpitāḥ pālayanti, tathā ātmasāmrājyepi puṃnāmanarakatrātṛtvenāpyātmapadaṃ pālayituṃ pūrvaślokoktapaśubhāvāduddhṛtya putrabhāvaṃ prāpitā iti bhāvaḥ | śāktānuttaravisargavāgarthanādaprakṛtirūpiṇyā tvayaiveti śeṣaḥ | uktaṃ ca mahārthamañjarīparimalepīmameva ślokamudāhṛtya -- "jagato rañjanādrājā sākṣāddevo maheśvaraḥ | tatputrā rājaputrā ssyurindriyāṇi ṣaḍeva hi" tatra sādhikārāḥ buddhisahitakarmendriyāṇi niradhikārāḥ manassahitajñānendriyāṇi ca iti | sādhikāratvaṃ karmakartṛtvarūpaṃ niradhikāratvamakartṛtvamiti tadarthaḥ | kāmandakīyādiṣu vaiṣayikārthaśāstreṣvapi tattadīyagūḍhābhisandhiḥ | yathā "viṣayānantaro rājā śatrurmitramataḥparam | arirmitramarormitraṃ mitramitramataḥ param | athārimitramitraṃ ca vijigīṣoḥ purassarāḥ | ityādi tatraiva draṣṭavyam | śārīrakabrahmasūtrabhāṣyepi | "jagadvyāpāravarjaṃ prakaraṇāt"| ityetatsūtrabhāṣyāvasare jagacchabdasya rūḍhyarthasvīkāreṇa jaḍajagatsṛṣṭyādikṛtyapañcakabāhyavyāpārasambandha eva muktānāṃ nirākṛtaḥ | natvābhyantaravāgarthakṛtyapañcakavidyāmayaḥ | anyathā tatraiva "saṅkalpādevāsya pitarassamupatiṣṭhante" ityādyuttaragranthavirodhāt | etenātra prācīnāvapi smārtasāṃkhyayogamārgatvena prasiddhau kāpilasāṃkhyahaṭhayogādibāhyavidyāmārgaupayikau pāramarṣāvapi vaidikakriyāmātropakārakau yaugikārthatatparāvavā arvācīnau tu dayānandādipuruṣakalpitau vaidikachāyānukāriṇau cāryasamājādisamajasaṃjñāntaravyavahṛtau pāṃcabhautikabāhyaprakṛtimātrepyaviśrāntau ādhyātmikarājavicārājaguhyayogāparanāmakavaidikavidyārājamārgaviruddhatvenāvaidikāviti pratyuktau veditavyau | vaidikavidyārājamārge "guruśiṣyabhāvātmakabrahmabhāvasya trayīmāsaprameyasya, rājaprakṛtyātmakakṣatrabhāvasya ṣaḍaṅgaṣaḍadhvamātraprameyasya, paśupaśupatyātmakavaiśyabhāvasya tantrāgamādividyāntaraprameyasya, sevyasevakamūrtimayaśūdrabhāvasya bhāṣāmayaprabandhaprameyasya ca | evaṃ caturvidhavāgarthaprameyasyāpyātmamātraviśrāntatvayuktyā vaidikaśabdānāṃ "yogādrūḍhirbhalīyasī" iti laukikanyāyena rūḍhyarthasvīkārasya "janmanā jāyate śūdraḥ karmaṇa jāyate dvijaḥ" ityuktaśūdrabāvaprabhṛtikakarmamārgopakāraktvapraṇālyā vaidikabrahmabhāvaparyantamārādupakārakatvavivakṣayā śiṣyaughānugrahārthakalpitatvena vidyāsantānābhivardhakatayā dvādaśāntanādakalpe mahonmanībinduśarīre rūḍhipradarśanārthatvāt | etadevābhipretyoktamabhiyuktairlaukikaguruśiṣyalakṣamaṃ lakṣīkṛtya "adhyāpayanti yadi darśayituṃ kṣamante (vedokta (nādānta) karmaguravo guravasta eva | śiṣyāsta eva ra(ca(sa) yanti puro gurūṇā manye punastadubhayābhinayaṃ bhajante" iti | etadeva bhinnasampradāyadvayapravartakagurvoghatrikadvayamapi jñānārṇavoktapuṣpasaṅkocakramasūcanapūrvakamanupadamevopariṣṭāttanaślokadvayepi varṇayati ca || 44-66 ||
<2-45-67>
evaṃ manobuddhisahitajñānakarmendriyaṣaṭkayoḥ prākṛtāprākṛtasvaravarṇātmakavaidikasāṃkhyayogaparāmarśavartmanoḥ prasaraṇapratisaṃcaraṇasampradāyapravartakaparaughamahaughadivyasiddhamānavaśiṣyaughākhyaguruṣaṭrakamithunarūpatvenātmanaḥ kālīkālaparaśivamithunātmakatrayodaśarasmirūpatayā ca vakṣyamāṇapañcavāhamahārthakramarūpavṛndacakrasya bījamupanyasya tameva bījaṃ "brahmaṇaḥ prabhṛtisiddhapañcakam" iti pūrvamuktanādanavakasya jñānārṇavoktagurupuṣpasaṅkocakramopayogidvādaśāntamahābinduviśrāntyā dvādaśādityamayātmasampadvismṛtyākhyanaṣṭasantatitvadoṣarāhitvena vāgarthamayaśivasaṃsāravṛkṣamūlatayā nirūpayiṣyan tatra prāṇāpānasūryacandrayorurdhvādhovahanakramaviparyayeṇa vahnibindumayanādamūrticakrepi agnīṣomakalāviparyayaṃ ca sūcayannagnīṣomanādamūrtimayaprakāśacakrasya siddhaughagurumaṇḍalātmatāṃ vivṛṇoti -- 
aurdhvavāhamayabindulakṣaṇaṃ
prāṇasūrya ihi cidviṣaṭkalaḥ |
tatkalādharatanorgurukrame
tvaṃ prakāśatanu (siddha(siddhi) maṇḍalat || 66 ||
aurdhvavāhetyādinā ślokadvayena | aurdhvavāhamayabindulakṣaṇaṃ svaravarṇanādakrame | pūrvaṃ "saptadhā vamasi yā tvamīśvarī" iti ślokasūcitasaptasrotassabandhisaptasvaravarṇabodhaka "a i uṇ" ityādimāheśvarasūtratrayamūlāgnisūryasomabindavaḥ, yadvā turīyavimarśānte vakṣyamāṇakāleyahṛdayodyogasambodhanaparyantatayā kādipāvasānakasparśavargavarṇākṣarasamāmnāyasaṅketita `kapay' iti prayuktadvādaśāntākṣara samāmnāyamūlabhūtā vā ūrdhvavāhamayābindava ucyante | tān lakṣayatīti ūrdhvavāhamayabindulakṣaṇaḥ | praṇavassvaraḥ | tadīyanādamūrtimayaṃ cakraṃ aurdhvavāhamayabindulakṣaṇam | yadvā, "gataṃ tiraśvīnamanūrusāradheḥ prasiddhamūrdhvajvalanaṃ havirbhujaḥ" ityuktāgnisūryasomadhāmagatibhedānāṃ prāṇavyānāpānavṛttitrayābhinnaūrdhvatiryagadhassrotastrayātmakatvena tadīyordhvasrotaḥprāṇavṛttimayo vahnibinduḥ | tallakṣaṇanādamayaṃ mūrticakramiti vā | iha rājane prakṛti rañjane vā | cidviṣaṭkalaḥ pūrvoktacinmayajñānakarmendriyaṣaṭkadvayaṃ cidviṣaṭkaṃ | tallāti gṛhṇātīti cidviṣaṭkalaḥ prāṇasūryaḥ | aurdhvavāhaprāṇabindukamagnimūrticakraṃ prakāśacakramayaḥ prāṇasūrya evetyarthaḥ | tatkalādharatanoḥ tatkalāḥ tayoragnisūryayoḥ kalāḥ dharati krameṇa śuklapakṣe tanoti vistārayati ca pūrṇimāparyantaṃ paścātkrameṇa tanoji kṣīṇayati ca kṛṣṇapakṣa iti tatkalādharatanuḥ | pūrvaṃ "ṣaṇḍavarjabhahimadyuteḥ kalāḥ | ityadhastāttanaśloke ṣoḍaśasvarāṇāṃ candrakalātvenaiva varṇanādvyavahārikaḥ pūrṇacandraḥ tasya tatkalādharatanoḥ | vyāvahārikacandrasya | yadvā, tatkalādharaścandraḥ sa eva tanuśśarīraṃ yasyāstava iti vā | gurukrame -- tatkalādharatanorityatra akārapraśleṣeṇa adharatanoḥ adharayasi vyavahāracandraṃ sūryātmanā avidyārātritulye darśe tanoṣi kṣīṇayasi kṛṣṇapratipattithimārabhya yāvat śuklapratipadaṃ "prathamāṃ pibate vahniḥ dvitīyāṃ pibate raviḥ" ityādismaraṇāditi bhāvaḥ | tadādi pūrṇimāparyantaṃ vistārayasi cetyadharatanostava | gurukrame ca pūrvoktatvadīyasāṃkhyayogamārgadvayapravartakagurukrame viṣaye ityarthaḥ | tatkalā tayoragnisūryayormūrtikalā tatkalaiva | "agniṃ vā vādityassāyaṃ praviśati | adyantaṃ vā vādityamagniranusamārohati" iti śruteḥ | raghuvaṃśakāvye anenaiva kavinā raghurājyavarṇanāvasaraṃ "sa rājyaṃ guruṇā dattaṃ pratipadyādhikaṃ babhau | dinānte nihitaṃ tejassavitreva hutāśanaḥ" ityukteśca agnisūryamūrtikalāyā ekatvasyāpi śāstrānubhavasiddhatvāttayorekaiva mūrtikalā | tadīyamūrtikalārūpiṇītvam | prakāśatanusiddhamaṇḍalaṃ -- svayaṃprakāśate ca prathamaṃ maṇḍalatrayābhinnopādhitrayeṇa, tanyate vistāryate svasvarūpameva ṣaḍadhvābhinnaṣaḍbhāvavikārātmanā, kṣīyate vā paścāt siddhyati ca kālakrameṇa devīmānasiddhamaṇḍalātmanā iti prakāśatanusiddhamaṇḍalaṃ bhavasīti śeṣaḥ | siddhimaṇḍalamiti pāṭhe -- prakāśatanu prakāśacakrarūpaṃ siddhimaṇḍalam | aṇimāsiddhyādisarvatrikhaṇḍāmudrāśaktiparyantatrailokyamohanākhyabhānumaṇḍalasya prakāśacakrarūpatayā prathamaprapūjyaṃ tattvameva bhavasītyarthaḥ | etenātra maṇḍalatrayātmake srotastrye, vakṣyamāṇādhovahananādalakṣaṇāpānanādamaye vā srotasi | yathecchaṃ trivṛddhāṅmayāgnerbindurūpatvaṃ, trivṛtprāṇamayasūryasya dvādaśāntaparyantanādātmakatvaṃ, dehamayaprakāśaśivacandrasya tadubhayakalādharatvaṃ cetyapīti siddham | tena sādākhyaṣoḍaśīkalātmakapuruṣacandrasya saptasaṃkhyākapūrvoktasaptasrotomayasūryakuṭumbādisakalakhagolādhārakatvena śivaśaktisadāśivādikalāntam "saptadhā vamasi" iti pūrvoktatattvasaptakavāmeśīkālīśabdārthakālaprabhṛtirūpatvaṃ, kaśyapanāmakāṣṭamasūryastha kālasūryātmakatayā paraśivakālarūpatvaṃ ca yadaruṇaprapāṭhakepi taittirīyāraṇyakakāṇḍe samāmnātam | yathā -- "yatte śilpaṃ kaśyapa rocanāvat | indriyāvatpuṣkalaṃ citrabhānu | yasmitsūryā arpitāssaptasākam | tasminrājānamadhiviśrayemamiti | te asmaisarve kaśyapāt vyotirlabhante tāṃtsomaḥ kaśyapādadhinirdhamati| bhrastākarmakṛdivaivaṃ" iti tatraiva tadīyāṇavavisargalakṣaṇaprakāśacandrajīvaśivātmanā saptasaṃkhyakasūryagoleṣu | bhrastākarmakṛdiveti vākyaśeṣasthasomanāmnā saptasrotomayatripuṭīdvayamadhyakendrasthityā viśrāntipradhānāṇavavisargadharmitvam | tathā "yasmānna jātaḥ paro anyo asti | ya āviveśa bhuvanāni viśvā | prajāpatiḥ prajayā saṃvidānaḥ | trīṇi jyotīṃṣi sa ca te saṣoḍasī" iti ṣoḍaśīnāmakasomayāge tannāmakagrahābhedena ṣoḍaśīkalāyāḥ śrutiśataiḥ prādhānyaṃ ca | tathā gītāsu vibhūtiyogānyāye "nakṣatrāṇāmahaṃ śaśī" iti prājāpatyabhijinnakṣatrātmakatvaṃ cābhihitam | tathā śāmbhavabindoḥ āgameṣu kāmakalāsvarūpabhāvanāyāṃ agnīṣomāpmakavisargabindudvayasya devatāsthūlarūpe kṛcadvayatvena anusvārātmakamiśrasūryabindormukhāvayavatvena paraśurāmakalpasūtre "bindunā mukhaṃ bindudvayena kucau sa parārthena ye niriti kāmakalāṃ dhyātvā" ityuktakāmakalāsthūlarūpe paradevatācinmayasvarūpārambhavācimukhacandraprayogena prakāśānandamūrtimayavāgarthacakratrayopāsanāyāṃ vāgarthapūrṇānusvārabindvātmanā upāsanāmukharūpatvapratīteḥ mūrtyānandacakrarūpayoragnīṣomavāgarthavisargabindvormadhyepi somabindorānandacakrābhedena madhyasthitiḥ, mūrticakramayasyāgneyabindoḥ sakalapāśavadvaitasaṃsāramayamṛtyudāhakatvena vilayādhiṣṭhānatvācchāmbhavavisargavilāsatvenāntatoniveśaśca siddhyata ityupāstiprakārabhedarahasyaprakāśanaṃ sarvatantrasiddhāntaprameyamapi sūcitam | ataeva saundaryalaharyāṃ śrīśaṅkaragurupādasārvabhaumapradarśitaṃ kāmakalāsvarūpavarṇanamapi -- "mukhaṃ binduṃ kṛtvā kucayugamadhastasya tadadho harārthaṃ dhyāyedho haramahiṣi te manmathakalām" ityadhovahananādalakṣaṇāpānasomamayaharārthakāmakalāsvarūpopāsanāyāmapi manobuddhiyutadaśendriyāṇāṃ dvādaśasaṃkhyāmātraviśrāntyā ātmā'tmīyānyatarakṛtyavāsanālope kāryavāsanāsantatināśena naṣṭhasantatitvamiva kālīkālayugalātmakabāhyabhautikakālasvarūpepi cāndrasaurādivyāvahārikakālamānasiddhamāsasaṃkhyāyāṃ "asti trayīdaśo māsa ityāhuḥ" ityādiśrutivākyaiḥ luptādhikamāsārthamāsatithyādikālāvayavavāsanālope "tvanmukhaṃ tripathanetri bhātmakam" iti pūrvoktabahirjṛmbhitacatustrikasvarūpavijñānavāsanānaṣṭasantatitvasyāpi dyotakamiti | dhāmasaṃvidvarṇakramātmakaviśvamayagurupuṣpasaṅkocakramepi dvādaśaraśmisaṃkhyāmātraviśrāntau jñānarṇave paraśivena niṣedhaḥ kaṇṭharaveṇoktaḥ | yathā jñānārṇave ṣoḍaśe paṭale -- "raśmibṛndaṃ dalayutaṃ guruvastu dalādhikāḥ | tasmātsaṅkocayetpuṣpamamitāssidbhihānidāḥ || 47 || naṣṭasantativijñeyā bhitāssarvasamṛddhidāḥ | puṣpasaṅkocanaṃ noceddvādaśe naṣṭasantatiḥ || 48 || santatyā naṣṭarūpassannabhavedevatāpriyaḥ | ataeva mayā puṣpaṃ samyaksaṅkocitaṃ priye || 49 || ityādinā kāmarājākhyavidyāsampradāyapravartakagurvoghatrayapaṃktiyajanaṃ vidhāya tāmeva gurupuṃkti parāparabhedena vibhajya mānavaughānte "aparāḥ parameśāni niyatā akṣarā ime | etattrayaṃ tu niyataṃ deśikānāṃ hitāya ca | mayoghghṛtya maheśāni puṣpaṃ saṃkucitaṃ priye | mānavaighāntike paścātsvagurutritayaṃ yajet" ityādinā yajanaprakārabhedopi vivicya varṇitaḥ | tasmādvāmasaṃvidvarṇākhyakramabhedena yathādhikāramadhibhūtādhidaivādhyātmapadeṣu prakāśānandamūrtimayacakrabhāvanā bhedena ca vāgarthamayākṣaratattvānāmeva unmanīprāṇanādasvarūpābhedasiddhiparyantaṃ gurupaṃktirūpeṇopāsyatetyupāsanārahasye gurupuṣpasaṅkocakramaḥ yathāsampradāyaṃ gurumukhaikvedyaśca || 45-67 ||
<2-46-68>
evamagnīṣomamūrtikalābhyāṃ sākaṃ dvādaśakalātmakacinmaryaurdhvavāhabindulakṣaṇaṃ nādanavakasamaṣṭimayamukhyaprāṇasūryaṃ prakāśacakratvenopavarṇya adhovahananādalakṣaṇaṃ manomayāpānasomamaṇḍalaṃ tadbhogyavigrahatvena nirdiśan‌ pūrvoktaṣoḍaśasvarāṇāṃ tadīyakalārūpatvavarṇanena tasyānandacakrarūpatāṃ darśayati -- 
yastvadhovahananādalakṣaṇā
pānasoma iha bhogyavigrahaḥ |
tasya ṣoḍaśakalāḥ kṛtā (stva[tya) yā
nandacakratanavo gurukrame || 68 ||
yastvityādinā | iha rājanarañjanasāmarasyātmake pūrvoktasāṃkhyamārge yogamārge vā smārte | gurukrame viṣaye | yadvā prakāśacakrasya pūrvaprakṛtatvena tadīyaparauoghamahaughadivyaughātmakagurukramasya pūrvaślokavivakṣitatve iha siddhamānavaśiṣyaughatrayātmakāvaśiṣṭavimarśaughatrayātmake gurukrame iti vā ihaśabdārthaḥ | yastu tuśabdaḥ pūrvavailakṣaṇyabodhakaḥ | pūrvaṃ prakāśacakre aurdhvavāhamayabindulakṣaṇaḥ prāṇasūryaḥ | atra tu adhovahananādalakṣaṇa apānasomaḥ | anacalane' iti dhātoḥ pūrvaṃ prakṛṣṭacalanātmako mukhyātmanā | calanasya prakarṣonāmāpracyutasvarūpatayā svasvarūpamātraviśrāntilakṣaṇaḥ | idānīṃ spandamātrasya svarūpāt pracyutatvenāpagatacalanātmakaḥ apānasoma iti viśeṣaḥ | ataevādhovahananādalakṣaṇaḥ sacāsāvapānasomaḥ | ataeva bhogyavigrahaḥ | prathamaṃ manomayāntarātmani "candramā manaso jātaḥ" iti śrutyā manomayaśarīreṇa vilakṣaṇaḥ lakṣaṇaśūnyaḥ viśiṣṭagrahalakṣaṇo vā mānasikavigrahaḥ | paścātkriyāśaktyātmanā pariṇatajñānaśaktikaḥ jñānaśaktyātmanā pariṇatakriyāśaktiko vā yathādhikāraṃ kriyāmayabhogyaśarīraḥ | tatopyanu tadubhayānyatarātmanā sāmarasyātmanā vā kāryamayabhogyavigrahaścetyarthaḥ | ataeva "adyatetti ca bhūtāni tasmādannaṃ taducyate" iti śrutyā kriyākāryānyatarasāmarasyātmakamanobuddhipradhānaṣaḍadhvamārgavigrahatvena pariṇatasaptasrotomayahṛdayodyogarūpeṇa "sa vā ayaṃ manuṣyeṣu yajñassaptahotā | amutra sadūbhyo devebhyo havyaṃ vahati" iti taittirīyabrāhmaṇe dvitīyakāṇḍadvitīyapraśne saptamānuvākoktasaptahotṛyajñamayakāryaśarīreṇa parokṣadevebhyopyamṛtapradaśca bhūtvā vakṣyamāṇaśāktaśāmbhavānuttaravisargapradhānyena paṃcadaśatidvyādikālaprāṇānyataratrivṛtpañcavāhaśarīreṇa ca vṛddhikṣayāvanubhūyāntatastadubhayasāmarasyātmanā viśvottīrṇopi bhavatītyathovahananādalakṣaṇāpānasomasya bhogyavigrahatvamiti bhāvaḥ | evaṃ jñānakriyāyutakartṛkāryakriyāpaṃcavāhamayabhogyavigrahaḥ yastvadadhovahananādalakṣaṇāpānasomosti | tasya vistārayanti saptasrotomayavāgarthānyataranādarūpeṇa tanūkurvanti vetyānandacakratanavaḥ ṣoḍaśakalāḥ `jñānamamba tava bheditaiḥ svaraistaitu yoga iha ṣaṇḍavarjitai'ritivakṣyamāṇatvāt | jñānakriyāsamapradhānā eva tvayā vāgarthaṣaḍadhvatripuṭīnādaprakṛtirūpiṇyā tvayetyarthaḥ | kṛtāḥ katham "tadātmānaṃ svayamakuruta | tasmāttatsukṛtamucyata iti | yadvai tatsukṛtaṃ | raso vai saḥ | rasaṃhyevāyaṃlabdhvānandī bhavati | kohyevānyātkaḥ prāṇyāt | yadeṣa ākāśa ānando na syāt | eṣa hyevānandayāti" iti śṛteḥ | adhovahananādalakṣaṇāpānasomadaharākāśavṛttirūpiṇyāstava kṛṅdhātuvāgarthajñānakriyobhayaśaktyātmakatvena śāktavisargavilāsamayyā tvayā vāgarthakramayodvayorapi pūrvoktaprāṇādināmakalāntaṣoḍaśakalāvācakāḥ kāmeśvaryādimahānityāntaṃ tithyādikālaśaktibodhakāścānuttarākārādivisargāntāṣṣoḍaśasvarāḥ prathamaṃ jñānaśaktyabhedena jijñāsitāḥ, paścādvivakṣitāḥ, tadanu kāmeśvaryādimahānityāntatithiraśmidevatāprakāśātmanā bhāvitāḥ, vimarśātmanā vimṛśya nirmitāśca, tatopyanu yathādhikāraṃ yathākramaṃ ca pūrvoktaprāṇādināmāntaṣoḍaśasaṃkhyākamanaścandrakalādyātmanā pariṇamayyārohāvarohābhyāsakrameṇāvayāvacchikṣitāśca bhavantītyaho tvadīyādhovahananādalakṣaṇāpānasomamayavidyāviṣayānandacakrakāmakalāgurukramodyāpyavidhāturīyapāde vimarśakadhaureyasya sphuṭa iti pāṭhāntaramiṣeṇa spaṣṭayati -- kṛtātyayānandacakratanava iti | `ay pay gatā' viti dhātoḥ gatyarthakadhātūnāṃ prāptyarthakatvaniyamācca kṛtaḥ śikṣitaḥ pratyayaḥ pūrvoktanaṣṭārthacatuṣkāvabhāsanādisvasvarūpalābhaparyantapañcavāhamahārthakramaḥ yasya tattathoktamānandacakrameva tanuryāsāṃ tāstathābhūtāṣṣoḍaśakalāḥ bhavantīti spaṣṭorthaḥ | etena sakalā vidyā'vidyātmikāpi sṛṣṭiścittaprabodhalakṣaṇaśāktānuttaravisargavilāsātmanau kāmakalāmayānandacakrābhinneveti phalitārthaḥ | etadevoktaṃ mālinīvijayavārtike catuśtvāriṃśat ślokādiparaśśataślokairvyāvahārikabhedasyāpi nirākaraṇena tatraiva draṣṭavyam | mahārthamañjarīparimalepi -- "duḥkhānyapi sukhāyante viṣamapyamutāyate | mokṣāyate ca saṃsāro yatra mārgassa śāṅkaraḥ" iti || 46-68 ||
<2-47-69>
evamānandacakrasvarūpavibhūtiṃ vyākhyāya tadīyanādanavakapratīkopāsanopayogitayā "brāhmaṃ divyaṃ tathā pitryaṃ prājāpatyaṃ gurostathā | sauraṃ ca sāvanaṃ cādramārkṣaṃ mānāni vai nava" ityuktanavavidhakālamānasvasvarūpasṛṣṭyādikṛtyapaṃcakapaṃḍitāyā mahākālyāḥ vakṣyamāṇakālasaṃkarṣiṇīvahnimūrtimayamahācakre pūrvoktaprakāśānandakālacakrapravartakasūryacandrayoḥ vidyā'vidyāmayavāgarthavyāvahārikanetradvayātmakatāṃ svasyaiva vahnimayatṛtīyanetratāṃ ca pāṭhabhedasiddhāmudghāṭayati -- 
vāsareśvarajamīśayoḥ kalā
(ścaṃ(caṃ)ḍi yāvadudayaṃ vita (nva(nya)te |
tāvadeva (taṃcedava) divasakṣayoda(ya(yā)
statvataḥ (tvatkṛta)prakaṭagupta(vigrahaḥ) viṣṭapāḥ || 47-69 ||
vāsaretyādinā atra prathamaṃ kalāśabdabahuvacanāpāṭhamanusṛtya vyākhyāyate | hecaṃḍi "caḍikrodhe" iti dhātoḥ traivarṇikabhāvāpayatvadīyanetrabiṃduṣu svarūpe vā maṃḍalatrayātmakeṣu bhedakalpake puruṣe krodhinīti sābhiprāyasambuddhiḥ | vāsareśa rajanīśayoḥ | kālajñānakriyāniyāmakatvena tradīyanetrabhūtayoritibhāvaḥ | kalāḥ yathākramaṃ māsārthamāsayośśuklakṛṣṇaniyāmikāḥ dvādaśasaṃkhyākāḥ vāsare śakalāḥ, tithiṣu divāniśākhyaśuklakṛṣṇaniyāmikāḥ ṣoḍaśasaṃkhyākā rajanīśakalāśca | yāvadudayaṃ vitanvate | yāvatākālena svasvodayaṃ vistārayanti | yadvā | "yāvatpurā nipātayorlada" iti bhaviṣyadarthakalaḍaṃtavivakṣayā udayaṃ vistārayiṣyatīti jñātvā vetyathaḥ | tāvadeva tāvatā kālanaiva | tatvata pūrvataraślokasūcitadvādaśatatvābhāvataḥ, pūrvaślokasūcitaṣoḍaśatatvabhāvataśca | prakaṭaguptavigrahaḥ | tvadīyavahnimūrtimaye varṣakalpe prakāśacakrasambandhidvādaśakalātatvabhāvataḥ māsādau prakaṭaḥ māsāṃte guptaśca vigrahaḥ tvadīyakālajñānapradhānasomanetravigrahaḥ tathaiva dinakalpe tithyādau prakaṭaḥ tithyante guptaśca "yāṃ tithiṃ samanuprāpya udayaṃ yāti bhāskaraḥ | sā tithissakalā jñeyā śrautasmārtādi karmasu" iti śāstrāt tvadīyakālakriyāpradhānavyavahāradakṣiṇaḥ prāṇasūryamayavigrahaśca yasmin sa tathoktaḥ | divasakṣayodayaḥ divasāśca kṣayāśca divasakṣayāḥ amāṃtavyāvahārikacāṃdramānasiddhāḥ pūrṇimāṃtavyāhārikabārhaspatyamānasiddhā vā amāpūrṇimānyatarāmāsātmikāḥ dvādaśasaṅkhyākāḥ divārāccaya ityarthaḥ saṃvatsarapratimā vai dvādaśa rātrayaḥ" iti śruteriti bhāvaḥ | tathaiva "śuklaḥ svapnāya bhūtāmāṃ ceṣṭāyai karmaṇāmahaḥ" iti pūrvoktamāstyapurāṇavacanānusāreṇa ca dvādaśasaṃkhyakapitṛmānasiddhaśuklapakṣapitṛrātrirūpāḥ kṛṣṇapakṣapitrahorūpā vā "caturviṃśati rarthamāsā ssaṃvatsaraḥ" iti pitṛdevāmānaśruteśca varṣakalpedvādaśasaṅkhyākacāṃdrahorātrarūpāḥ | tathā ṣaṣṭyuttaraśatatrayasaṅkhyākā ssāvanāhorātrarūpā divasakṣayāśca | teṣāmudayaḥ | bhavatīti śeṣaḥ | taṃ ca devadivasakṣayodaya iti pāṭhe | tamudayamanūsṛtya | devayoḥ prakāśānaṃdacakrarūpayorvyāvahārikacandrasūryayoḥ divasakṣayodayo bhavatīti | divasakṣayodayā iti bahuvacanapāṭhe | tvatkṛtaprakaṭaguptaviṣṭapā iti bahuvacanapāṭhāṃtarāśrayaṇenabhavantīticāpyāhāreṇānvayaḥ | iyaṃ vāsareśarajanīśayoḥ kalā iti bahuvacanakalāśabdānusāriṇī vyākhyā | atha vahnimūrtimayatṛtīyanetravyāpakṣe -- vāsareśarajanīśayoḥ kalā "bhīṣāsmādvātaḥ pavate | bhīṣodeti sūryaḥ" tathā "sūryācandramasau dhātā yathā pūrvamakalpayat" ityādi taittirīyopaniṣadi nārāyaṇī pariśiṣṭeca śṛteḥ | dakṣavāmanetrabhṛtayostayormadhyakendrasthityā tadubhayasṛṣṭyādi kṛtyapaṃcakaniyāmikā tṛtīyāgninetramūrtimayī brāhmī mūrtikaleti yāvat "brahmavigrahakalādhirohiṇī" iti vakṣyamāṇatvāt | yāvat yāvatā kālana | udayaṃ ātmonmeṣaṃ prati | vitanyate vistāryate | agnimūrticakrarūpiṇyā tvayaiveti śeṣaḥ | tāvadeva tvakṛtaprakaṭaguptaviṣṭapāḥ tvatkṛte tvadīyacinmayakṛtyapaṃcake kṛtayuge vā, tvatkṛte tvannimitte vā tvatsvarūpāvabodhārthamityarthaḥ | prakaṭāni guptāni ca viṣṭapāni yeṣu te tathoktāḥ | tattvataḥ prakacaguptavigraha iti pāṭhe -- ṣaṭ‌triṃśattatvabhāvato prakaṭā guptāśca vigrahā yasmin devadivasakṣayodaye `caturyugasahasrāṇi brahmaṇo dinamucyate' ityuktabrāhmāhorātrakalpānāṃ `unmeṣanimiṣotpannavipannabhuvanāvaliḥ' ityuktabrahmadivasakṣayodayasya vā bhuvanālirūpasya tvadīyonmeṣanimeṣamātrakālikatvāditi bhāvaḥ | tādṛśadevadivasakṣayodayā iti bahuvacanapāṭhāntareṇaivānvayaḥ | devānāṃ tvadīyapīṭhāvayavapañcabrahmaṇāṃ divasakṣayodayāśca bhavantīti cānvayaḥ | uktaṃca laghubṛṃhaṇyāmapibrahmāyuṣo daśaguṇaṃ viṣṇorāyuḥ paraṃ smṛtam | sahasraguṇitaṃ tasmādrudrasyāyuḥ paraṃ kila || tasmāllakṣaguṇaṃ proktamīśvarasyāyuruttamam | tasmātsadāśivasyayuḥ proktaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ budhaiḥ || samudraguṇitaṃ tasmācchivayordehadhāraṇam ||" iti | ataeva pūrvaṃ "manameyamiti netṛlakṣaṇā" iti ślokavyākhyānāvasare prāṇādināmakalāparyantaṣoḍaśakalātmako brahmaṇassamaṣṭivāsanāmanomayaścandra avināśisādākhyanāmakalāmātrāvaśeṣeṇa suṣuptau vyaṣṭimana ivāvaśiṣṭapañcadaśakalābhissaha samaṣṭyātmakacitprāṇasūryātmani yadā līyate tadā brahmaṇopi kevalatamomayī avidyārātriḥ vyavahāracandrasya darśaivodbhavati | samaṣṭicitprāṇasūryavivekena yathā yathā kalānāmātmanaḥ pṛthagbhāvastathā tathā bodhamayaścandropi vardhate | yā tu sattvapuruṣānyathākhyātissaiva sūryacandrayoratyantaviśeṣarūpā vidyā paurṇamāsī ca kalāṣoḍaśakena poḍaśyā vā kalayā manaścandropetā sambhavati | ata ṛtasya karmaṇaḥ phalabhṛtaṃ śivasaṃsāracakraṃ vidvaddṛṣṭyā "pañca daśa vā arthamāsasya rātrayorthamāsaśassaṃvatsara āpyate tāsāṃ trīṇi ca śatāni ṣaṣṭiścākṣarāṇi tāvatīssaṃvatsarasya rātrayokṣaraśa evaṃ saṃvatsaramāpnoti" iti sāmidhenībrāhmaṇoktaprakāreṇa svaravarṇamātrākṣararūpatvātkālacakrāvayavāhorātrayāṇāṃ tatrāpi sūryacandrapradhānavidyā'vidyācakṣurunmeṣanimeṣātmakatayā'māpūrṇimātmakamāsakalpayorapi "katamāsādevākṣarā bṛhatī | yasyāṃ tatpratyatiṣṭhat | dvādaśa paurṇamāsyaḥ | dvādaśāṣṭakāḥ | dvādaśāmāvāśyāḥ | eṣā vāvasādevākṣarā bṛhatī | yasyāṃ tatpratyatiṣṭhaditi" iti taittirīyabrāhmaṇe prathamakāṇḍe pañcamaprapāṭhakacaramānuvāke chandorathaprastāve prājāpatyachandorathaśrutyuktaprakāreṇa kāryabrahmakalāsu bṛhatīchandomayadaivatākṣarātmakatvena śrutitātparyaviṣayatvācca ātmavilāsākṣaratattvātmakaṃ kālacakramavidvatsādhakānāṃ vidyonmeṣārthaṃ karuṇayā śrutismṛtyādikalpitajananamaraṇapravāhe kṛtyapañcakasaṃsārātmanā kālacakrātmakaṃ śrīcakrameva | āsurajīvalokasya tattajjīvavāsanānurodhena sunābhanāmakaṃ viṣṇusudarśamākhyaṃ mārakacakramapi bhavatyeva | etena kālapravartakacandrasūryanakṣatrādibrahmalokāntakhagolasthadevasadmasu tattallokalokeśakālaparimāṇabhedena svasvalokalokapālasṛṣṭisthitilayādikṛtyapañcakādhikāranirvāhāya tattacchatavārṣikāyuḥ parimāpakakālaśaktyāstava kālasaṅkarṣiṇyāssarvalokaikajananītvamagnisūryasomamaṇḍalatrayacakṣuṣkatayaivānvarthakamiti bhāvaḥ | ataeva nādamātṛkāyāṃ nādanāvakasyeva varṇamātṛkāyāṃ caturdaśasvaravarṇānte bindutrayasya mālinīvijayoktapunaḥpunaḥ prakṣobhagaṇanena saptāṣṭādaśāntena svāyambhuvatvaravarṇaparyantatayā kṛtyatrayādiṣaḍadhvāntaṃ varṇaparāmarśalābhopīti mātṛkāntarasāmarasyaṃ veditavyam | tadayaṃ mantrarāmāyaṇakāreṇoktassarvadevatānāṃ kṛtyapañcakādhikārasaṃgrahaḥ "ekaikasminyathādarśe prāsādo muhurāntaraiḥ | sahito dṛśyate deveṣvevaṃ lokassurāntaraiḥ || tasmātsyurdevatāssarvāḥ pratyekaṃ viśvayonayaḥ | anyonyayonayaścaiva yatāyāskamunīritam ||" ityādinā yāskaniruktodāharaṇena tatra kiṃcitprapaṃcitastatraiva draṣṭavyaityalaṃ vistareṇa || 47-69 ||
<2-48-70>
athedānīṃ kālasaṅkarṣiṇīmahākālyāḥ pramāṇapadātmakatārtīyanetrātmanā pūrvoktavāsareśarajanīśamūrtikalayorviparyāsitapramātṛprameyabhāvena vyāvahārikakālanetrītvamupāsanāmārge ityamuṃ rahasyārthamapi sūcayannagnermahākālīmūrticakrarūpatāṃ darśayati -- 
devi! taddvayakalāvimiśra (ṇā) yā
sāmarasyāpadameti yā sthiti |
bindunādasamavedyavedakā
ntargatā hi tava mūrtiratra sā || 70 ||
devītyādinā | hedevi! vāṅmanaḥprāṇamayamaṇḍalatrayātmanā prakāśātmike iti sambuddhyarthaḥ | taddvayakalāvimiśrayā tayorvāsareśarajanīśayordvayasya kalānāṃ tadīyamūrtikalayorvā pūrvoktadviṣaṭkapañcadaśakalāsaṅ‌khyāmelanena saptaviṃśatinakṣatramīrtirūpāṇāṃ cā maṇḍalatrayābhinnapradhānamūrtikalayāsākamabhedavivakṣayā "brāhmaṇo vā aṣṭaviṃśo nakṣatrāṇām" iti śrutyuktābhijinnakṣatrākhyasādācandrakalārūpāṇāṃ bhedavivakṣāyāṃ triṃśattithisamaṣṭidvādaśamāsarūpāṇāṃ kalānāṃ vā | tatsampādakamūrtikalayordvayorvā vimiśraḥ vilakṣaṇamelanaṃ yasyāṃ tvadīyāgnimūrtikalāyāṃ tayā tathoktayā taddvayakalāvimiśrayā | tvadīyāgnimūrtikalayā saha | bindunādasamavedyavedakāntargatā jñānakriyāparaparyāyābindunādadvayabhāvātmanā samau yau vedyavedakau | prameyapramātṛvyavahāralocanabhūtau sūryacandrau tayorantargatā sthitiḥ | pramāvedanāvedādyaparaparyāyāgnimaṇḍalacidicchārūpiṇyāstava tādrūpyeṇa madhyasthitiḥ "sthitiḥ svarūpārohitvaṃ pratibimbavadevaṃ yat | sthitirnāma svarūpasya tattadrūpatayā dhṛtiḥ" ityuktajāṭharavaiśvānarātmanā bimbapratibimbānyataramūrtidhāraṇanaipuṇyamiti vā arthaḥ | ityuktalakṣaṇā yā sthitiḥ sāmarasyapadaṃ maṇḍaladvayamādhyasthyaṃ pramātṛsūryamūrtyātmanā prameyacandramūrtyātmanā samarasabhāvaṃ vā eti prāpnoti | yadvā prameyapramāṇapramātṛvāṅmanaḥ prāṇamaṇḍalatrayepi vāṅmayatripuṭyāṃ vāgarthanādamūrtiṣu ṛgyajussāmātmikāsu trayīmūrtimayīṣu "prajāpatiṃ vai devāśśukraṃ payo duhan | tadebhyo navyabhavat | tadagnarvyakarot | tāni śukriyāṇi sāmānyabhavan | teṣāṃ yo rasotyakṣarat | tāni śukrayajūgraṃṣyabhavan" iti śukrīyakāṇḍaśrutyuktaprāṇavāyunādarasābhinnayajurvedapuruṣavāṅmūrtimayī "tasya yajureva śiraḥ | ṛgdakṣiṇaḥ pakṣaḥ | sāmottaraḥ pakṣaḥ" iti taittirīyabrahmavallīśrutyuktadakṣiṇottarapakṣaṛksāmnorvāgarthanādayormadhyapadasthityā śiraśśabdārthapradhānarasabhāvāpattirūpaṃ sāmarasyam | tatraiva "śukriyāṇāṃ vā etāni śukriyāṇi | sāmapayasaṃ vā etayoranyat | devānāmanyatpayaḥ | yadgeḥ payaḥ | tatsāmnaḥ payaḥ | yadajāyai payaḥ | taddevānāṃ payaḥ" iti vākyaśeṣoktagośabdārthasārasvatanādapayorasapradhānabhāvarūpaṃ eti prāpnotīti vā arthaḥ | evameva manaḥprāṇatripuṭyorapi nādamūrtisthitisāmarasya yojyam | sarvatra vāgarthanabakāparaparyāyanādanādhamithunasāmarasyasyaiva mūrtitattvarūpatvāt | taddvayakalāvimiśraṇeti pāṭhe -- sthiterviśeṣaṇamiti jñeyam | sā sthitireva | atra pramātrāditripuṭīmadhyakendre tava mūrtirhi hīti sakalavedaśāstraprasiddhidyotako nipātaḥ | iyamevedṛśī tvadīyā mūrtiriti sakalavedaśāstrādivāṅmanaḥ prabhṛtikapramāṇajātaṃ saṃvādyata iti bhāvaḥ | atra "annamayaṃ hi somyamana āpomayāḥ prāṇāstejomayī vāk" ityādichāndogyaśrutyuktavākprāṇamanasāṃ yathecchaṃ pratyekamapi vedyavedakāparaparyāyaprameyapramātṛbhāvamadhyasthityā tripuṭīmūrtitvaṃ mūrtitrapuṭītvaṃ vā sampādanīyamityapi mūrticakrarahasyamiti saṃkṣepaḥ || 48-70 ||
<2-49-71>
evaṃ vedyavedakaprakāśānandacakrātmanossūryacandrayorantaraṅgatāyāmunmanīnādanavakamahākālīvahnimūrtau saṃhārepi mahāsaṅkarṣaṇāgnimūrtivilāse nādanavakasvaravarṇāsāṃkhyayogasāmarasyakhaṇ‍ḍacakrapi gurukramātmanā mūrticakratripuṭīsthitiṃ darśayati -- 
līḍhabhānuśaśimaṇḍa(lā(la)sthiti
rlaṃ(bhi(bi)tā pralayavahninā tvayā |
prāṇadhāmni daśa saptadhā satī
mūrtirāśritagurukramā śive || 70 ||
līḍhabhānvityādinā | heśive adhikaraṇabhūtasādhakaśive iti saptamī vā pralayavahninā tvayā saṅkarṣaṇākhyapralayaprāṇāgnirūpiṇyā tvayā | līḍhabhānuśaśimaṇḍalā pūrṇavidyodayaparyantaṃ bhānumaṇḍalātmanā vedyavedakapadāgnīṣomaśāktavisargabindudvayāntarāle sthitvā vidyodayottaramāṇavonmanīvisargamaryādayā uditavidyādārḍhyāya vedyavedakapadāpannayossūryāgnyorantarāle abhijitsādākalācandrākhyasomamaṇḍalātmanā śāktavisargamaryādayā ca sthitvā vidyādārḍhyalābhottaraṃ vyāvahārikamaṇḍalatrayasaṃhārecchayā śāmbhavavisargabhāvena apāṃvatsanakṣatragolāparaparyāyamukhyaprāṇasaṅkarṣaṇavahnimaṇḍalamūrtyātmanā vyāvahārikatripuṭīmaṇḍalatrayamātmasātkṛtya turīyacidagnimūrtirūpeṇa līḍhabhānuśaśimaṇḍalāsthitiralambhitesyarthaḥ | līḍhamanuśaśimaṇḍalasthirlambiteti pāṭhāntarapakṣe -- pūrvoktatṛtīyanetramūrtikalāviśeṣaṇam | līḍhā ātmasātkutyāmilitā | bhānuśaśimaṇḍalayoḥ sthitiḥ | "sthitiḥ svarūpārohitvaṃ pratibimbavadevayat" ityuktalakṣaṇāparivartitaturīyāniketasthitirvā yasyāṃ mūrtikalāyāṃ sā lambitā | avalambitetyarthaḥ | saiva sthitimūrtikalā vā | prāṇadhāmni mukhyaprāṇatejasi | daśa saptadhā satī | prāṇāpānādimukhyaprāṇavṛttipañcakena nāgakūrmādyupaprāṇavṛttipañcakena ca saha daśadhā | manobuddhiyutajñānakarmānyatarendriyapañcakena gaṇanāyāṃ saptadhā | yadvā, tantroktadaśavahvikalānā kālīkarālītyādīnāṃ hiraṇyākanakādīnāṃ vā vahnijihvānāṃ melanena vā sapdadaśadhā | mahārthamañjarīparimalādau vahnimīrticakravarṇanasya darśanāt | kṛṣṇayajurvedepi taittirīyasaṃhitāyāṃ vahneḥ prājāpatyamūrtivarṇanaprastāve prathamakāṇḍaṣaṣṭhaprapāṭhakaikādaśānuvāke "yo vai saptadaśaṃ prajāpatiṃ yajñamanvāyattaṃ veda" ityādau "āśrāvayeti caturakṣaramastuśrauvaḍiti caturakṣaraṃ yajeti dvyakṣaraṃ yeyajāmahe iti pañcākṣaraṃ "dvyakṣaro vaṣaṭkāra eṣa vai saptadaśaḥ prajāpatiḥ" ityādyuktaprājāpatyavahnimūrticakrasvarūpe āśrāvayeti caturakṣaramiti sāṅketikavākyena | "a i uṇ" pratyāhārātmakasya sārthavisargasyāntarmukhasyāṇavavisargapratyagātmanaḥ astu śrauṣaḍiti caturakṣaramityanena tadīyadīrghasvaratrikayutavisargaṣaḍadhvaparāmarśasya, yajeti dvyakṣaramityanena sandhisvarānyatarayugalasya, ye yajāmaha iti pañcākṣaramityanena ṛ ṝ lṛ lṝ varṇacatuṣṭayasya ṣaṇḍasvarātmakasya āṇavārthabinduyutasyaiva, dvyakṣaro vaṣaṭkāra ityanena prasaraṇapratisañcaraṇātmakaśāktaśāmbhavānuttaravisargaparāmarśa ṣaṭkadvayasya ca `eṣa vai saptadaśaḥ prajāpatiḥ' ityādivākyaśeṣeṇa bījayonividhibhedabheditakriyāśaktimayāmātṛkārṇakadambasya tanmūlabhūtasvarakhaṇḍasyavā mūlasvaraṣoḍaśake svarakadambe "ṣoḍaśakalo vai puruṣaḥ" ityuktavāṅmayapuruṣasvarūpe āṇavavisargārthānusvārayogābhisandhinā anuttaraśāktavisargāvayavāgniṣomabindvovā bhedavivakṣayā vākpade chandovīryāparaparyāye śabdabrahmaṇa eva samaṣṭivāṅmayaprājāpatyapuruṣamūrtisaṅketena śrutivivakṣitatvācca | tanādamayavāgarthaprāṇadhāmni svaranayanādhanavake vā vāgarthaprāṇanādasāmarasyātmikā tvadīyamukhyavahnimūrtirāśritagurukramānuttarākāradhāmasaṃvidvarṇakrameṣu triṣvapi ākārādiśritagurukramā bhavatītyarthaḥ | idameva sakalavāgarthasāmarasyātmakamaṇḍalatrayaparāmarśaprathamasūtrātmanā ekadhā | nādanavakābhinnasvaranavakabodhakasūtracatuṣṭayātmanā turyadhā | caturdaśabhuvanātmakamāheśvaracaturdaśasūtrakadambātmanāca "nava turyadhā gamaḥ" iti ślokavākyaśeṣeṇāpi sūcitaṃ turīyaśuddhavidyāmayaṃ cidagnimūrticakramiti | hal ityuktacaturdaśatamasūtrahalvarṇahakārasya pañcamasūtreṇa gatārthatvepi dvādaśasūtrakadambamātraviśrāntau ūṣmavarṇatrikasya "śa ṣa sa r" iti trayodaśatamasūtropadiṣṭasya vidyākrame sāntatyābhāvenāpi vidyāsantatināśasambhavādatopi pūrvoktanaṣṭasantatitvaṃ nādadvādaśāntaviśrāntimukhena praṇava iva māheśvarasūtrepi trayodaśe "yaḥkilāse"ti prathamavimarśaślokoktaparāmantramayaśāktavarṇapratyāhārasiddhyā dyotitamiti pūrvoktagurupuṣpasaṅkocakramaḥ svaravyañjanakhaṇḍadvayepi samavalambanīya iti rahasyam | ataeva vedāṅgaśīkṣāyāmekaviṃśatiparyantaḥ svarakhaṇḍavarṇakramaḥ paṭhitaḥ satu purastādvivecayiṣyate ||
evaṃ kālasaṅkarṣiṇīcidagnimūrticakragurukramaṃ vivicya tadīyakāmakalāvibhūtivarṇanena tasyā eva kāmakalākālyabhedamapi dyotayan, tadīyanetratrayātmaka maṇḍalatrayasya tejobannātmakabhūttrayābhedena saṃhārasthitisṛṣṭimayakṛtyatraye vakṣyamāṇavarṇakrame vidyā'vidyāmayasuṣuptisvapnajāgradavasthatrayeca saṃvitkrame svasvarūpamayaturīyasthānādeva vāmnaitīcchājñānakriyāsaṃvitkramaprasāraṃ varṇayati |
<2-50-72>
iṣṭhabodhakṛtaśaktayastvayā
saṃhṛtisthitivisṛṣṭisadmasu |
turyacitpadabhayaprasāritā
ssuptitaḥ prabhṛti dhāmasu triṣu || 72 ||
iṣṭetyādinā -- saṃhṛtisthitivisṛṣṭisadmāsu sīdantyeteṣu svecchayā siddhā iti vyutpattyā sadmāni siddhānāṃ viśrāntisthānāni | saṃhārādikṛtyatrayādhikārapadānītyarthaḥ | teṣu | iṣṭabodhakṛtaśaktayaḥ | suptitaḥ prabhṛti dhāmasu triṣviti liṅgāt prathamaṃ vidyā'vidyānyatarasaṃhārakrame | iṣṭaḥ icchaviṣayīkṛtaḥ vidyāvidyānyatarasaṃhārasaṅkalpaḥ | bodhaḥ tattadviṣayakaṃ jñānam | kṛtaṃ tattadanukūlaṃ karma ca bāhyakriyāsṛṣṭirūpamiti tisraḥ teṣāṃ śaktisāmarthyamavandhyecchādi siddhirūpaṃ ceti catasro vā | tvayā pūrvoktaprakāśādicakratraye saṃhārapradhānadaśakalā'bhinnamūrticakragurukrame paraughamahauvadivyaughagurukramatripuṭīnavakasamaṣṭidevatārūpayā daśamyā | tathā sthitisṛṣṭipradhānadvādaśaṣoḍaśakalā'bhinnaprakāśānandackayorgurukramepi śeṣamānavasiddhaughadivyaughacatuṣṭayagurvaughasamaṣṭimahābindurūpayā trayodaśyā saptadyā tvayaivetyarthaḥ | dhāmasu triṣu dhamantyuchvāsaniśvāsavāyuvṛttibhireṣviti, dhamyante pūryate karmavāsanābhirantarbāhyendriyāṇi vā eṣviti dhāmāni sthūlasūkṣmakāraṇaśarīrāṇi | teṣu triṣu suptitaḥ prabhṛtijaḍacanmayānyatarasuṣuptimārabhya turyacitpadamayaprasāritāḥ saṃhārakrame turyacitpadamayaṃ nādāntanādanavakaśaktirūpaṃ tadarthaṃ prasāritāḥ vistāritā bhavanti | sṛṣṭikrametu dvādaśāntamahābindumārabhya kāmakalāntagaṇanayā iṣṭa icchāviṣayīkṛtā kāmakalā cakratrayābhinnalipitrayanirmāṇaviṣayiṇī | bodhāḥ trividhalipivāsanārūpāḥ | kṛtāni tadīyabāhyavyāpārasāmarthyāvalambanena āntaravāsanāsaṃhṛtirūpāḥ kṛtayaḥ, śaktayaḥ tadīyavyaktinirmāṇaśaktayaḥ iti militvā dvādaśa | trayodaśaṃ vyaktinirmāṇaṃ dvādaśantamahābindurūpaṃ | evaṃ vakṣyamāṇaniketasthitikramepi ṣoḍaśāntamūhyam | evamiṣṭabodhakṛtaśaktayaḥ | turyacitpadamayaprasāritāḥ "yadvaiśivaṃ | tanmayaḥ" iti śṛtyā turyacitpadameva mayaḥ svātmānandasukhaṃ | tadrūpeṇaiva prasāritā bhavanti | evaṃ jaḍaprapaṃcepi paṃcavāhamahārtharūpe pradhamaṃ siddhaugharūpiṇyā tvayā pūrvānubhūtasauṣuptānandecchāvadeva pūrvānubhūtajaḍavaiṣayikānandecchā janyate | tadanu, mānavaugharūpiṇyā mananātmanā svapnāvasthāyāmiva tadavāptyupāyo budhyate | tatopyanu, śiṣyaugharūpiṇyā tadanukūlakarmānuṣṭhāpanena sādhakopi taccheṣīkriyata iti siddhamānavaśiṣyaughākhyagurupaṃktikramo bahirmukhasādhakavargastadubhayakramamadhyakendravartinī śrīkākalā kālī bhuktimuktipradā āśritagurukramā mūrtirbhavati | saiva divyaughādiśiṣyaughāntacatuṣṭayaugharūpiṇī mahaughāṃtaprakāśacakradvādaśāntaraśmiviśrāntā cetprakāśānandayoścakragurukramarūpāpītyatopi dvādaśegurukramavicchedāt naṣṭantatitvamiti sarvamanavadyam || 50-72 ||
<2-51-73>
ekamuktakāmakalā kālī turīyapadamevānākhyakālīkālaturīyapadamapi bhavatīti vivinakti -- 
meya(mātṛ(māna)miti lakṣaṇaṃ kulaṃ
prāntato (prāptito) vrajati yatra viśramam |
tatpadaṃ tava turīyamambike
yanmahābdhi (yaṃ mahābdhi)ranupākhyamiṣyate || 73 ||
meyamityādinā | he ambike meyamātṛmitilakṣamaṃ meyamātṛmitisvarūpaṃ | kulaṃ sajātīyasamūhaḥ | meyamānamitilakṣaṇamiti pāṭhe -- prāptita iti pāṭhāntaradṛṣṭyā mānaśabdo'bhimānaviśrāntibodhanaparaḥ | meyamānamitīrlakṣayatīti meyamānamitilakṣaṇam | kula | saśaktikabrahmaviṣṇurudragranthitrayanāmakamūlādhārādyājñācakrāntacakraṣaṭkamityarthaḥ | tallakṣamaṃ tu pūrvamasminnevavimarśe `pañcavṛttitanurambe' tyetachlokavyākhyānāvasare vivecitaṃ | prāntataḥ prakārasamīpataḥ meyamātṛmānamitiśabdānāṃ pravarṇasāmīpyāt prameyapramātṛpramāṇapramitiśabdākāreṇa niṣpadyamānatvāt | prāntataḥ prakārāntataḥ yatra sthāne | pāṭhabhedakrameṇa pramāṇasthāne pramātṛsthāne vetyarthaḥ | viśramaṃ vrajati | divyamahāparaughākhyakāraṇagurvaughaviśrāntisthāne viśrāmyatītyarthaḥ | prāptito vrajatīti pāṭhe -- meyamātṛ (māna)mitilakṣaṇakulasya prakārāptitaḥ paramapramātṛkulasthāne iti yāvat | viśramaṃ vrajatīti samānam | yat paramapramātṛrūpaṃ kulaviśramasthānam | mahadbhiḥ mahaughākhyagurubhiḥ ādisiddhanādhādibhirvāhṛdayacandrikādyuktagurukramavaṃśīyaiḥ | anupākhyaṃ `ana calane' iti dhātoḥ prāṇaspandamātropanāmakaṃ iṣyate icchāvāgartharūpatayā viṣayīkriyate | tat tava turīyaṃ padam | tvadīyaturīyasthānaṃ tadeva | yaṃ mahābdhiriti pāṭhe -- yaṃ paramapramātṛviśramaṃ pramātrahanabhimānaṃ vā ityarthaḥ | mahābdhiḥ pūrvoktanistaraṅgaśivacitpayonidhiḥ anupākhyaṃ prāṇaspandamātropanāmakaṃ avyabhicaritavāgarthaspandamātranāmarūpātmakaṃ vā iṣyate icchati tattava turīyaṃ padamevetyanvayaḥ || 51-73 ||
<2-52-74>
evamuktā'vyabhicaritavāgarthaspandamātranāmarūpātmakaṃ tvadīyaprāṇaspandanādanāmakaṃ turīyapadameva sāṃkhyayogatripuṭīsargasampradāyabhedena mudrātripuṭīmātraprabhedena ca tripādekapādvibhūtidṛgbhyāṃ gurusampradāyaparamparā bhajata ityāha -- 
cārarāvacarubhirvibheditai
rmudrayā ca yadupāsanaṃ tava |
tadvaśena bhajate paramparāṃ
tāvakakrama (gatā sthirākṛtiḥ) yā sphuṭhākṛti || 74 ||
cārarāvetyādinā | vibheditaiḥ guruśāstraśiṣyanāmakaparādyūrdhvasrotomayagurusampradāyabhedena siddhimananaśeṣatāparaparyayatiryaktrotomayagurvaughakramasampradāyabhedena vā viśiṣyasañjātabhedaiḥ | cārarāvarubhiḥ atra cārādiśabdatrayeṇa gūḍhacāranyāyena pramātrādimukhyātmatripuṭī vivakṣyate | yadvā carabhakṣaṇe iti dhātoḥ cāraḥ bhoktṛbhāvaḥ | ruśabdane iti dhātoḥ rāvaḥ vaktṛvākyādikartṛkriyāvyāpāraḥ | caruḥ cīryate kriyākalāpaḥ atreti vyutpattyākāryakartubhāvāparaparyāyaḥ bāhyaḍambarakriyākalāpaḥ taiḥ | yadvā, cārassamayācāraḥ, rāvo vāgvimarśaḥ, caruḥ prathamadvitīyāditantrāditantrāgamanasampradāyasiddhapadārthapañcakadravyajātaṃ vā | yadvā, cāraḥ rāmānujapūrṇaprajñanakulīśapāsupatādisaṃskāradarśanatrikaprameyasabhayācāraḥ, rāvo nāma śaivapratyabhijñāraseśvarādirāsāyanikadarśanatrikaprameyaṃ rāsāyanikamābhyantaraṃ karma, caruḥ kāṇādākṣapādajaiminyādidarśanaprameyabhūtaḥ kartṛcaitanyātmeti vā | śārīrakasvarūpānubhavavacanakarmānuṣṭhānātmakavyāvahārikasaccidānandatripuṭīmayanāma rūpābhinnabāhyāditraivarṇikakuladharmāvā cārarāvacaruśabdārthāḥ | taistaistādṛśaiścārarāvacarubhiḥ | mudrayā mudaṃ mānasikavāsanābhūmikābhedabhinnamānandaviśeṣaṃ rāti gṛhṇātīti mudrā tattadānandānubhavakālikā''kṛtiviśeṣaḥ | pāṇinisāṃkhyapātañjalādidarśanatrikaprameyabhoktṛbhāvasphorakasmphoṭāparaparyāyaḥ | bāhyākṛtimātraliṅgaṃ vā | tasyāpi vibheditayeti liṅgavipariṇāmanānvaya iti cakāreṇāvagamyate | brāhmaṇyāditraivarṇikajñānakriyāmātrasphorakeṇa caturthena dehendriyasaṅghātamātraviśrāntabāhyacihneneti phalitārthaḥ | etādṛśamudrayā ca tava yadupāsanaṃ tadvaśena tāvanmātrāyattatayā `vaśa āyattatāyāṃ ca vaṃśamicchāprabhutvayo'riti keśānta tadicchayā tatprabhutvena vā | tāvakakramagatā -- tāvakaḥ tvadīyaḥ yaḥ kramaḥ sṛṣṭyādibhāsāntakṛtyapañcakavistṛtirūpaḥ paṃcārthamahākramaḥ, sṛṣṭyāditirodhānāparaparyāyānākhyāparyantaścaturarthamahākramaḥ, cijjaḍāparaparyāyavidyā'vidyānyatarasṛṣṭyādisaṃhārāntastryarthamahākramaśca taṃ gacchatīti tāvakakramagatā | sthirīkṛtiḥ asthirā sthirā sampadyamānākṛtā kṛtiḥ sthirīkṛtiḥ | acalatayā sampādyamānā, prākṛtikaprāṇaspandākhyahṛdayodyogarūpiṇī, niṣādapañcajanamānavahṛdayodyogarūpiṇī, cāturvarṇyatraivarṇikādihṛdayodyogarūpiṇī ca kṛtirityarthaḥ | paramparāṃ paraughaprabhṛtidivyaughaparyantāmūrdhvasrotogurusampradāyarūpāṃ bhajata ityata mṛgvedādisakalasmārtatantrāgamasampradāyāntamaparāvidyāmātṛkāyāḥ cārarāvādyupāsanakramapāramparyanirṇayaḥ | athātaḥ parāvidyāmātṛkāyāstu prathamamanuttaratantrāgamasampradāye nādabindulipibhedena mātṛkā tāvatsaṃkṣepatastrividhā | tatrāpi hādikādividyābhedena pratyekadvaividhye ṣaḍvidhā ca | prāṇasvaravarṇaśabditayā tṛtīyavimarśe vakṣyamāṇalakṣamayā vedāṅgaśīkṣoktayā svāyambhuvakālarasmimayabṛndacakrabhinnayā brāhmīmātṛkayāsākaṃ saptavidhāpi etāeva "ambādule"tyādikṛttikeṣṭakāmantroktāḥ ṣaṭsapta vā kṛttikā iti vaidikīpratipattirapi | tatrāpyanuttarāmnāyaprasiddhalopāmudrādividyākāmakalāpāraṃparyakrameṇa hādikādisādyanādyakṣāntakāmakalāsvarūpe ca matabhedenānantaprakāratvepi tattadīyānuttaropāsanakramapāramparya sṛṣṭisaṃhārānākhyābhāsakramaśabditaṃ naṣṭārthacatuṣkāvabhāsādinirvikalpakameyāvabhāsāntaviparītakṛtyapañcakaśabditaṃ vā ekavidhameveti na tatra pāramparyakramabhedaḥ | tathā ca śrutirapi `dve vidye veditavye parācāparā ca aparā ṛgvedādilakṣaṇā | atha parā | yayā tadakṣaramadhigamyate" ityādiḥ | tatrāpi saṃhārakrameṇa śiṣyaughādigurvaughaṣaṭkalakṣaṇaṃ saṃkṣepatayo yathā svātmabhedeneva bahiriva nissarantī vaikharī vāgarthamayī gauravī śiṣyaughamūrtiḥ | aparā vidyāsṛṣṭisaṃhārānyatarakramapradhānā prathamā | tasyā eva svarūpārohe tattadūpatayā dhāraṇāyoga eva sthitimayī mūrtiḥ | saiva `sthitirnāma svarūpasya tattadrupatayā dhṛtiḥ | sthitiḥ svarūpārohitvaṃ pratibimbavadeva yat" ityāditantrāntaravacanaiḥ sthititattvavimarśamayī cenmānavaugharūpiṇītyucyate | sace dvamarśaḥ pūrṇarūpatayā svātmanyupasaṃhārakameṇa cetsiddhastadā tatsaṃhārakramapradhānā nādamātṛkā tadarthamātṛkā vā paśyantī vāgarthaṣaḍadhvamayī śāmbhavavisargapradhānā gaurī | siddhaughamūrtirapi bhavitumarhati | tadanantaradaśāyāmiyameva sādāśivāntarnimeṣasampatprādhānyena śāmbhavavisargamayyapi naṣṭārthacatuṣkāyabhāsāditirvikalpakameyāvabhāsāntavakṣyamāṇalakṣamaviparītakṛtyapañcakakrameṇa vidyā'vidyānyatarakṛtyapañcakakramayoryathādhikāraṃ tirodhānānugrahāparaparyāyānākhyābhāsākramapraṇālīvyājena divyaughamithunamūrtimanyapi ca kramaparyāvartanena sarvabhūtātmabhūtāsmatāparanāmakamahāsthityā ca mahāparāśiṣyaughatripuṭī mahāmūrtireva binduvimarśasaṃśitasphoṭanādavāgarthatripuṭīṣaḍadhvātmakatvātmamūrtyā parāvaikharīvāgarthavimarśaikarūpyaṃ svātmaprakāśena sākaṃ sphoṭayantī vāvabhāsata iti gurvaughaṣaḍadhvakramasya kālīkālamithunapañcavāhanahārthakramasya vā svarūpatattvanirūpaṇamiti saṃkṣepaḥ | evaṃ tripuṭīṣaḍadhvakramasya tricatuḥpañcavāhamahārthakramasya vā tryarthakramāntarbhāvopi anuttaramahāsaṃvitsrotaḥ svacchandacāribhissamabhyūhanīya iti dik | tāvakakramatayā sphuṭākṛtīti pāṭhe -- upāsanameva kartṛ | bheditacārarāvacarvādibhiḥ yat tava upāsanaṃ pūrvaślokoktatvadīyaturīyapadasambandhi vartate tadevopāsanam | vaśatayā tāvakaturīyapadāyattatvena tadicchayā tatprabhutvena vā | tāvakakramatayā tvadīyapañcārthamahākramāditryarthamahākramaparyantatvena siddhimananaśeṣatāparanāmakamadhyauvagurukramatvena vā sphuṭākṛti | tāvakakramaśabdārtharūpatayā ā''vipālagopālaṃ carmacakṣurvedyadehendriyādikramagatajātivyaktiguṇakriyādikramarūpeṇa vā sphuṭākāraṃ yathā tathā | paraṃ kālaparaśivarūpam | parāṃ parāvāgartharūpiṇīṃ tvāṃ ca bhajate svasvakuladharmānupālanadvāreṇa sevataeva | uktaṃ ca gītāsu bhagavatāpi `ahaṃ sarvayajñānāṃ bhoktā pramureva ca | natu māmabhijānanti tattvenātaścyavanti te' iti || 52-74 ||
<2-53-75>
evamuktacatuḥpañcārthamahānādamayagurusampradāyamahākrameṣu caturarthagurukrame pūrvaṃ puṣpasaṅkocakramoktadvādaśe naṣṭasantatitvadoṣanivṛttidvāreṇa vakṣyamāṇasparśasphoṭaparyantavāgarthamūrtito nītasantatitvalābhāya ca madhyamamānanyāyena vāgarthanādatripuṭīmayīmarthaprakṛtiṃ vaidikatrivargātmanā paramaprameyabhūtāṃ `vimarśo bindunādau ca sphoṭaśśabdaśca vākkramaḥ" ityuktapañcavāhamayīṃ paramapramātṛbhūtāṃ śābdīṃ prakṛtiṃ ca -- "catvāri vākparimitā padāni | tāni vidurbrāhmaṇā ye manīṣiṇaḥ | guhā trīṇi nihitā neṅgayanti | turīyaṃ vāco manuṣyā vadanti" iti śrutyuktavaidikakratvarthavāgarthamūrtau padminyādihantinīparyantakāmaśāstroktatāṃtrikatraivargikapuruṣārthamūrtau ca pramāṇapadamūrtyaṣṭakarūpāyāṃ mūrtaprakṛtau ca vaidikatāntrikajñānakriyādūtīyāgayogasāmarasyamukhena dhāmasaṃvidvarṇakramairvakṣyamāṇaprakāśānandamūrtimayabṛndacakrātmanā nādabindulipimūrtikalā cāturvidhyena cejyāyogyatāṃ varṇayan vimarśamupasaṃharati -- 
maṅgale tvamasi vākcatuṣṭaye
yā parā sphurati mūrtimaṇḍa (pe le) |
sā prakāśa (su(mu)khabṛndacakra (bhā(gā)
nādabindulipimūrtiri (jya(ṣya) se || 75 ||
iti śrīmahākavikālidāsakṛtau cidgaganacandrikāyāṃ dvitīyo vimarśassamāptaḥ ||
maṅgale ityādinā | maṅgale iti dehalīdīpanyāyena sambuddhisaptamyubhayavibhaktivivakṣayā vāgdevatāmūrtimaṇḍapayorviśeṣaṇatayā yojyam | hemaṅgale vimarśaśarīreṇa sarvamaṅgalātmike mahākāli | maṅgalātmake vākcatuṣṭaye parāpaśyaṃtyādyātmake | tathaiva mūrtimaṇḍape ca | tvaṃ vimarśamayī asi | ekatra śabdarūpeṇa, anyatrārtharūpeṇa, ubhayatra sāmarasyavimarśātmanā ca tvaṃ maṅgalaivāsītyarthaḥ | mūrtimaṇḍale iti pāṭhe -- maṇḍalātmanā sthitamūrtisamūhe ityarthaḥ | vākcatuṣṭaye parāpaśyantyādirūpe mūrtimaṇḍale, padminīcitriṇyādirūpe ca | yā parā parānāmnī vāk padminījātīyā mūrtiśca | yadvā vaikharīnāmnī vāk hastinījātīyā mūrtiśceti vā | kāmaśāstroktasvīyāparakīyāsāmānyādināyikābhedeṣu parakīyā ca dhvanyate | sphurati tredhā bhedasphuraṇenāpi vimarśavelāyāṃ svābhedena prakāśate | tathāhi vaikharyapi svīyā, vargāṣṭakajananītvena paraśrotrendriyagrāhyatayā svaviṣayatayā ca parā sarvotkṛṣṭāsphurati | mūrtimaṃḍalapakṣepi -- parāpadminī śreṣṭhā sphurati | hastinī ca mūrti rasvajātipuruṣopayogitayā parā śreṣṭhā sphurati | parakīyāpi "chandasi vyavāyepi"ti pāṇinīyavaidikaśābdaniyamasya vaidikaniyogādau ubhayasammatau manvādibhirvaśantatvādimahārthahetutayā arthātideśenāpyaṅgīkṛtya parastriyo niyogaviṣayatāpradarśanātparakīyāpi mūrtiḥ vaṃśatantupratiṣṭhāhetutayā sarvotkṛṣṭā sphurati | sā parā padminīva | "aṣṭācakrā navadvārā" ityādiśrutyuktapadmāni asyāssantīti vyutpattyā padminyeva | evaṃ vaikharyapi | sā parā vaikharī vā vāk | prakāśamukhabṛnda cakrabhā prakāśacakraṃ mukhe ārambhe yasya prakāśānandamūrtimayabṛndacakrasya tat prakāśamukhabṛndacakraṃ prakāśamukhabṛndacakrasya bheva bhā yasyāḥ parāvācaḥ vaikharīvāco vā sā prakāśamukhabṛndacakrabhā | prakāśasukhabṛndacakrageti pāṭheprakāśarūpaṃ yat sukhabṛndacakraṃ cakrākārasambhāvitaviṣayānandasamūha ityarthaḥ | mahārthamañjarīparimalavivṛtapañcavidharasmiskandhātmakaṃ bṛndacakraṃ vā tadgacchatīti prakāśasukhabṛndacakragā | pañcaskandhātmakabṛndacakrasvarūpavistarastu jñānamantrayogaśāktaśāmbhavākhyavakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇapañcaskandharasmimayaḥ catuṣṣaṣṭhisaṅkhyākamātṛkārṇabṛndamayaḥ agre vitanyate mūrtipakṣepi sā padminyādijātīyā strīmūrtiḥ | prakāśamukhaṃ (sukhaṃ) vā bṛndayati ekatra kendrīkaroti prakāśamukha(sukha)bṛndaḥ | svayaṃprakāśātmānandasukhasya viṣayānandasukhasya vā upadeṣṭā dātā vā guruḥ bhartrādirūpaḥ | sopi prakāśamukha (sukha)bṛnda eva | taccakraṃ jīvacakrāparanāmakaṃ jñānakriyādūtīyāgapīṭharūpaṃ tṛtīyavimarśe vakṣyamāṇam | tasya bhā kāṃtirūpiṇī vā taṃ gacchati prāpnoti vā | prakāśamukha(sukha) bṛndacakra (bhā(gā)vā | nādabindulipimūrtiḥ nādaḥ nadatīti nāda iti vyutpattyā "vimarśo bindunādau ca sphoṭaśśabdaśca vākkramaḥ" ityuktabahirmukhapramātṛnādasthānīyā'vyaktabindurūpaḥ vimarśāparanāmakaḥ binduḥ pramāṇapadaṃ gatavivakṣāparanāmakaḥ paśyantīmadhyamānyataravyaktāvyaktadhvanirūpaḥ | lipiḥ prameyaviṣayākārakāritakalātmikāsphoṭarūpā | tattritayasamaṣṭiḥ caturthamūrtirūpā | ijyase | sādhakairiti śeṣaḥ | iṣyase iti pāṭhe -- yajidhātusamprasāraṇena `iṣa icchāyā'miti vā dhātoḥ krameṇa yāgārthakatvādiṣṭiśabdārthaviṣayīkriyase ityarthaḥ | icchāviṣayīkriyane ijyāviṣayīkriyate ceti labhyate | etadeva vedānāṃ devānāṃ ca sṛṣṭyādikṛtyapañcakakrameṇa guptibhāṣitajagadvijṛmbaṇamaryādayā ca parokṣapriyatā pratyakṣadveṣitā ceti bhāvaḥ | tathā ca taittirīyabrāhmaṇe tṛtīyakāṇḍe dvādaśaprapāṭhakadvitīyānuvākādigranthasandarbheṇa svargadvoraṣṭiprakaraṇe "devebhyo vai svargo lokastiro bhavat | te prajāpatimabruvat | prajāpate svargo vai no lokasmirobhūt | tamanvicceti | taṃ yajñakutūbhiranvaicchat | taṃ yajñakratubhirnānvavindat | tamiṣṭibhirasvaicchat | tamiṣṭibhiranvavindat | tadiṣṭenāmiṣṭitvam | eṣṭayo havai nāma | tā iṣṭaya ityākṣate parokṣeṇa| parokṣapriyā iva hi devāḥ | tamāśābravīt" ityādinā sphuṭīkṛtam | etena `eṣasatyānveṣaṇe' ityādidhātuprātipadikabhedabalena tvadīyasatyasvarūpāvabodhārthamanviṣyase cetyarthopi labhyataeva | ataeva uktaṃ ca kāmakalāvilāse śivaśaktimayasitaśoṇābinduyugalasya śāktavisargabhāvenaiva kāmakalātmanā nādamayavāgarthasṛṣṭihetutvam -- "sitaśoṇabinduyugalaṃ viviktaśivaśaktisaṃkucatprasaram | vāgarthasṛṣṭihetuḥ parasparānupaviṣṭavispaṣṭam | sphuṭitādarumādibandornādabrahmāṃkuro ravo'vyaktaḥ | kāmaḥ kamanīyatayā kalā ca dahanedavigrahau bindū" ityādinā vāgarthasṛṣṭihetutvamavibhinnaprakāśavimarśātmikāyā maṅgalādevyāḥ sphuṭamupavarṇitam | dūtīyāgayogakramopi jñānārṇave dvāviṃśatipaṭale kriyādūtīkramamupavarṇya, trayorviṃśatipaṭale jñānadūtīkramaprakāropi darśito yathā "dūtyantaraṃ pravikṣyāmi yena brahma sanātanam | praṇavākhyaṃ yāgagehaṃ praviśya suravandite" ityārabhya, "jātibhedastu dūtīnāṃ caturthā parikīrtitaḥ | hastenī śaṅkhinī caiva citriṇau padminī priye | caturvidhāhi dūtyastu sundaryadhārulocanāḥ | vaikharī hastinī jñeyā sthūlā yasmādvāranane | yayedaṃ dhāryate sarvaṃ brahmāṇḍaparipaṇḍalam | vargāṣṭake ca deveśi sūte diggajasañcayam | teneyaṃ kariṇī proktā madhyamā śaṅkhinī bhavet | śaṅkhinītu yathā bhadre viśuddhā pāpavarjitā | sarvadevapriyā sā hi śāṃtisaubhāgyaśobhitā | tathā dhanaspatigatā madhyamā vāgvarānane | citriṇī ballarī devī mahadoṣavināśinī | yasyāḥ phalaṃ varārohe śivaśaktimayaṃ sadā | śivaśaktimayaṃ devi prāṇimātraṃ jagatrayaṃ | teṣu sarveṣu paśyantī jīveṣu paramāśrayā | parabrahmaṇi saṃlīnā parā vākparameśvarī | tāṃ dūtīṃ tatra saṃpūjya sanādhikusumai śśuciḥ | padminī tu parājñeyā haṃsasvacchandacāriṇī | haṃso'dvayena padmaṃ hi tyaktvā saṃkoca madrije | vikāsayati saubhāgyaṃ tathā vastuprakāśataḥ | parā prakāśamāyāti padminotvamato bhavet | tasyā dehe varārohe kalāḥ pūjyāstu ṣoḍaśa" ityādinā parādiparimitavākpadānāṃ padminyādināyikāmūrtibhedānāṃ cābhedenādvetasyarūpasāmarasyasamādhistatraiva draṣṭavya iti dik || 53-75 ||
<2-54-76>
yadyapi kalikātānagaramānusandhānasamitīyo mūlacidgaganacandrikākośe ślokoyaṃ nopalabhyate | tathāpyanyatropalabdha ityatra prakṣipya vyākhyāyate |
ādimāṃtimagṛhītavarṇarā
śyātmikāhamiti yā svataḥprathā |
mantravīryamiti sādhitāgamai
stanmayo gururasi tvamambike || iti || 76 ||
asyeyamavataraṇikā evaṃ jñāna kriyādūpīyāgasāmarasyaṃ sapramāṇaṃ nirūpya vakṣyamāṇabrāhmīnārāyaṇīmāheśvaryādyakṣarasamāmnāyavyavahṛtanavākṣamātṛkāsādhāraṇyena dvayorahamo ramākalāyāśca pūrvoktatripuṭīṣaḍadhvasaptasrotādivargavarṇasvarūpakramaprathāyāṃ tattatpratyāhārakramasiddhagaurīmūrtimayaṃ mantravīryaśabditaṃ kauśivaśaktyabhedamapi saṃsādhayati -- ādimāntimetyādinā -- he ambike! ādiḥ (anutarākārādiḥ) māntimaḥ | śāṃbhavavisargakāryamakārāvasānakaśca | ata eva gṛhītaḥ | gṛhākāreṇa prāptaḥ | gṛhavāgartharūpopādhitādātmyenāvāptaḥ | ādāvamākalātmanopalabdhaḥ ityarthaḥ | tathā ādimaḥ | anuttarākārasvaravargavarṇānyatamarūpaḥ | antimaḥ śāktavisargabindudvayātmakaḥ | tatkāryabhūtaḥ | "śaṣasar‌" sūtrīvargavarṇānyatararūpo vā | śāmbhavavisargopahitastatkāryahalantyasūtrīyahakāravarṇātmako vā | paścādasāvahamākalātmanā vā upalabdhaścetyarthaḥ | tābhyāṃ tābhyāṃ ca krameṇa gṛhīkṛtassanjāgrahaśca kṛto yo varṇarāśiḥ | akārādimakārāntaḥ | tadādyanuttaravisargāntastattatkāryataścānyonyagarbhigarbhabhāvapratyāhārabhedairupalabdhaḥ svaravyañjako nādavyañjako vā varṇarāśiriti bhāvaḥ | tadātmikā | yā ahamiti svataḥprathā | amākalātmakaḥ svapāramārthikanāmarūpāhamātmakaśca pṛthūbhāva ityarthaḥ | saiveti vidheyaprādhānyavivakṣayā strītvaṃ | āgamaiḥ śāstraiḥ | mantravīyamiti svarūpamiti ca | sādhitā | "sojñātāvātmani svaṃ ca tiṣvātmīye" iti …noṣāt upādhyupahitaliṅgatrayagūḍhābhisandhidṛgbhedena saṃjātasādhanā siddhasādhanā ca kṛteti | saivatvaṃ | guru gaurīmūrtiḥ | tanmayo gurūrapi | tvamevāsi | samākalādau kāladṛgiva prātipadikena tvadīyadṛgbhedenaiva svarūpepi yāgayogādi bhedakalpanā | svarūpadṛśā tu na kimapīti bhāvaḥ || 54-76 ||
dvitīye vyavahāraikapramātṛyugalaspṛśi | vimarśe ciccandrikāsyabhāṣyādivyā cakorikā || 1 || dhanadānandanādhena kṛtā sarvārthasiddhidā | viceyamatra tataṃ mātṛmānayugaṃ budhaiḥ || 2 || ye śaktibhāvamaṇutāṃ samavāpuruccaiste gauravīṃ mupasadya sama (rcya) (rpya) mūrtiṃ | prauḍhāntarullasanadivyadaśāvalokamudghāṭayatu yadimāṃ sudidṛkṣavaḥ syuḥ || 3 || dhanadānandanādhīyaṃ bahudhā(')nyavivarjitaṃ mīyameva satataṃ sarve samupa (yuṃ)bhuṃ)jate || 4 || yathā guruḥ prasādaḥ syāśśabdenduśekhare | tatheyaṃ kālidāsānāṃ vyākhyā dīvyā cakorikā || 5 || …tpravṛtyabhimukhīkaraṇā (rthamayāca) daparā (rdhya)(dhya)taḥ | vācāṃ syuccāvamīti (mṛ) tu)ṣyatvatra vimarśakāḥ || 6 || "āgamāśca vikārāśca pratyayāhadhātubhiḥ | ucyāryate tatasteṣu neme prāptāḥ kalādayaḥ || 7 || evaṃ mahā …yakāravacopyatra nidarśanaṃ | kalanārahitesvāgamādīnāṃ pāṭhakarmaṇi ||
|| iti śrīdhanadānandanādhakṛtau cidgaganacandrikāvyākhyāyāṃ divyacakorikākhyāyāṃ dvitīyo vimarśaḥ samāptaḥ ||
|| śrīsadgurukāmākṣyambānandanādhacaraṇasarojayoḥ upāyanīkṛtaśceti śivam ||
